=== FILE: .\Algorithm\graph_algo.h ===

#pragma once
#include "../Structure/graph.h"
#include "../Structure/queue.h"
#include "../Structure/stack.h"
#include "../Structure/linked_list.h"

class graph_algo {
private:
    // HÃƒÂ m phÃ¡Â»Â¥ trÃ¡Â»Â£: KiÃ¡Â»Æ’m tra xem mÃ¡Â»â„¢t Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€°nh Ã„â€˜ÃƒÂ£ Ã„â€˜Ã†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£c thÃ„Æ’m hay chÃ†Â°a
    template <typename t>
    static bool is_visited(linked_list<t>& visited_list, t vertex) {
        node<t>* curr = visited_list.get_head();
        while (curr != nullptr) {
            // YÃƒÂªu cÃ¡ÂºÂ§u Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€˜i tÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£ng t (vÃƒÂ­ dÃ¡Â»Â¥: book) phÃ¡ÂºÂ£i nÃ¡ÂºÂ¡p chÃ¡Â»â€œng toÃƒÂ¡n tÃ¡Â»Â­ ==
            if (curr->get_data() == vertex) {
                return true; 
            }
            curr = curr->get_next();
        }
        return false;
    }

public:
    // 1. ThuÃ¡ÂºÂ­t toÃƒÂ¡n BFS (Breadth-First Search) - GÃ¡Â»Â£i ÃƒÂ½ sÃƒÂ¡ch theo lÃ¡Â»â€ºp
    template <typename t>
    static linked_list<t> BFS(graph<t>& g, t start_vertex) {
        linked_list<t> result;      // LÃ†Â°u thÃ¡Â»Â© tÃ¡Â»Â± duyÃ¡Â»â€¡t (Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»Æ’ lÃƒÂ m list gÃ¡Â»Â£i ÃƒÂ½)
        linked_list<t> visited;     // Ã„ÂÃƒÂ¡nh dÃ¡ÂºÂ¥u cÃƒÂ¡c sÃƒÂ¡ch Ã„â€˜ÃƒÂ£ quÃƒÂ©t
        queue<t> q;                 // HÃƒÂ ng Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»Â£i

        // BÃ¡ÂºÂ¯t Ã„â€˜Ã¡ÂºÂ§u tÃ¡Â»Â« Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€°nh gÃ¡Â»â€˜c
        q.push(start_vertex);
        visited.push_back(start_vertex);

        while (!q.is_empty()) {
            t current = q.front();  // LÃ¡ÂºÂ¥y ra phÃ¡ÂºÂ§n tÃ¡Â»Â­ Ã„â€˜Ã¡ÂºÂ§u (lÃ†Â°u ÃƒÂ½ Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€¢i thÃƒÂ nh phÃ†Â°Ã†Â¡ng thÃ¡Â»Â©c tÃ†Â°Ã†Â¡ng Ã¡Â»Â©ng nÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u queue cÃ¡Â»Â§a bÃ¡ÂºÂ¡n tÃƒÂªn khÃƒÂ¡c, vd: peek)
            q.pop();

            result.push_back(current);

            // LÃ¡ÂºÂ¥y danh sÃƒÂ¡ch hÃƒÂ ng xÃƒÂ³m cÃ¡Â»Â§a Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€°nh hiÃ¡Â»â€¡n tÃ¡ÂºÂ¡i (giÃ¡ÂºÂ£ Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€¹nh graph cÃƒÂ³ hÃƒÂ m get_neighbors)
            linked_list<t> neighbors = g.get_neighbors(current);
            node<t>* neighbor_node = neighbors.get_head();
            
            while (neighbor_node != nullptr) {
                t neighbor_data = neighbor_node->get_data();
                
                // NÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u hÃƒÂ ng xÃƒÂ³m chÃ†Â°a Ã„â€˜Ã†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£c thÃ„Æ’m -> Ã„â€˜ÃƒÂ¡nh dÃ¡ÂºÂ¥u vÃƒÂ  Ã„â€˜Ã†Â°a vÃƒÂ o hÃƒÂ ng Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»Â£i
                if (!is_visited(visited, neighbor_data)) {
                    visited.push_back(neighbor_data);
                    q.push(neighbor_data);
                }
                neighbor_node = neighbor_node->get_next();
            }
        }
        return result;
    }

    // 2. ThuÃ¡ÂºÂ­t toÃƒÂ¡n DFS (Depth-First Search) - PhÃƒÂ¢n cÃ¡Â»Â¥m nhÃƒÂ³m sÃƒÂ¡ch
    template <typename t>
    static linked_list<t> DFS(graph<t>& g, t start_vertex) {
        linked_list<t> result;
        linked_list<t> visited;
        stack<t> s;                 // NgÃ„Æ’n xÃ¡ÂºÂ¿p

        s.push(start_vertex);

        while (!s.empty()) {
            t current = s.peek();    // LÃ¡ÂºÂ¥y phÃ¡ÂºÂ§n tÃ¡Â»Â­ trÃƒÂªn cÃƒÂ¹ng
            s.pop();

            // KhÃƒÂ¡c vÃ¡Â»â€ºi BFS, DFS nhÃƒÂ©t vÃƒÂ o stack rÃ¡Â»â€œi mÃ¡Â»â€ºi kiÃ¡Â»Æ’m tra visited lÃƒÂºc lÃ¡ÂºÂ¥y ra
            if (!is_visited(visited, current)) {
                visited.push_back(current);
                result.push_back(current);

                linked_list<t> neighbors = g.get_neighbors(current);
                node<t>* neighbor_node = neighbors.get_head();
                
                // NhÃƒÂ©t toÃƒÂ n bÃ¡Â»â„¢ hÃƒÂ ng xÃƒÂ³m chÃ†Â°a thÃ„Æ’m vÃƒÂ o ngÃ„Æ’n xÃ¡ÂºÂ¿p Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»Æ’ Ã„â€˜i sÃƒÂ¢u
                while (neighbor_node != nullptr) {
                    t neighbor_data = neighbor_node->get_data();
                    if (!is_visited(visited, neighbor_data)) {
                        s.push(neighbor_data);
                    }
                    neighbor_node = neighbor_node->get_next();
                }
            }
        }
        return result;
    }
};




=== FILE: .\Algorithm\search_algo.h ===

#pragma once
#include <string>

class search_algo {
public:
    // 1. ChuyÃ¡Â»Æ’n chuÃ¡Â»â€”i vÃ¡Â»Â in thÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Âng (HÃ¡Â»â€” trÃ¡Â»Â£ tÃƒÂ¬m kiÃ¡ÂºÂ¿m khÃƒÂ´ng phÃƒÂ¢n biÃ¡Â»â€¡t Hoa/ThÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Âng)
    static std::string to_lower(std::string str) {
        std::string result;
        result.reserve(str.size());

        for (size_t i = 0; i < str.size();) {
            unsigned char c = (unsigned char)str[i];
            unsigned int cp = 0;
            size_t bytes = 1;

            if (c < 0x80) {
                cp = c;
            } else if ((c & 0xE0) == 0xC0 && i + 1 < str.size()) {
                cp = ((c & 0x1F) << 6) |
                     ((unsigned char)str[i + 1] & 0x3F);
                bytes = 2;
            } else if ((c & 0xF0) == 0xE0 && i + 2 < str.size()) {
                cp = ((c & 0x0F) << 12) |
                     (((unsigned char)str[i + 1] & 0x3F) << 6) |
                     ((unsigned char)str[i + 2] & 0x3F);
                bytes = 3;
            } else if ((c & 0xF8) == 0xF0 && i + 3 < str.size()) {
                cp = ((c & 0x07) << 18) |
                     (((unsigned char)str[i + 1] & 0x3F) << 12) |
                     (((unsigned char)str[i + 2] & 0x3F) << 6) |
                     ((unsigned char)str[i + 3] & 0x3F);
                bytes = 4;
            } else {
                result += str[i++];
                continue;
            }

            if (cp >= 'A' && cp <= 'Z') cp += 32;
            else if ((cp >= 0x00C0 && cp <= 0x00D6) ||
                     (cp >= 0x00D8 && cp <= 0x00DE)) cp += 32;
            else if ((cp >= 0x0100 && cp <= 0x012F && cp % 2 == 0) ||
                     (cp >= 0x0132 && cp <= 0x0137 && cp % 2 == 0) ||
                     (cp >= 0x014A && cp <= 0x0177 && cp % 2 == 0) ||
                     (cp >= 0x1E00 && cp <= 0x1E95 && cp % 2 == 0) ||
                     (cp >= 0x1EA0 && cp <= 0x1EF8 && cp % 2 == 0)) cp += 1;
            else if (cp >= 0x0139 && cp <= 0x0148 && cp % 2 == 1) cp += 1;
            else if (cp == 0x0130) cp = 0x0069;
            else if (cp == 0x0178) cp = 0x00FF;
            else if (cp == 0x01A0) cp = 0x01A1;
            else if (cp == 0x01AF) cp = 0x01B0;

            if (cp < 0x80) {
                result += (char)cp;
            } else if (cp < 0x800) {
                result += (char)(0xC0 | (cp >> 6));
                result += (char)(0x80 | (cp & 0x3F));
            } else if (cp < 0x10000) {
                result += (char)(0xE0 | (cp >> 12));
                result += (char)(0x80 | ((cp >> 6) & 0x3F));
                result += (char)(0x80 | (cp & 0x3F));
            } else {
                result += (char)(0xF0 | (cp >> 18));
                result += (char)(0x80 | ((cp >> 12) & 0x3F));
                result += (char)(0x80 | ((cp >> 6) & 0x3F));
                result += (char)(0x80 | (cp & 0x3F));
            }
            i += bytes;
        }
        return result;
    }

    // 2. GÃ¡Â»Ât bÃ¡Â»Â khoÃ¡ÂºÂ£ng trÃ¡ÂºÂ¯ng thÃ¡Â»Â«a Ã¡Â»Å¸ 2 Ã„â€˜Ã¡ÂºÂ§u do ngÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Âi dÃƒÂ¹ng gÃƒÂµ nhÃ¡ÂºÂ§m
    static std::string trim(std::string str) {
        if (str.empty()) return str;
        
        int start = 0;
        while (start < str.length() && str[start] == ' ') start++;
        
        int end = str.length() - 1;
        while (end >= 0 && str[end] == ' ') end--;
        
        if (start > end) return "";
        return str.substr(start, end - start + 1);
    }

    // 3. ThuÃ¡ÂºÂ­t toÃƒÂ¡n tÃƒÂ¬m chuÃ¡Â»â€”i con (TrÃ¡ÂºÂ£ vÃ¡Â»Â true nÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u pattern nÃ¡ÂºÂ±m trong text)
    static bool contains(std::string text, std::string pattern) {
        text = to_lower(trim(text));
        pattern = to_lower(trim(pattern));

        if (pattern.empty()) return true;
        if (text.length() < pattern.length()) return false;

        // TrÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£t dÃ¡Â»Âc theo text Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»Æ’ tÃƒÂ¬m pattern
        for (int i = 0; i <= text.length() - pattern.length(); i++) {
            int j;
            for (j = 0; j < pattern.length(); j++) {
                if (text[i + j] != pattern[j]) {
                    break; 
                }
            }
            if (j == pattern.length()) return true; // KhÃ¡Â»â€ºp 100%
        }
        return false;
    }
};




=== FILE: .\Algorithm\sort_algo.h ===

#pragma once
#include "../Structure/node.h"

class sort_algo {
private:
    // 1. Giai Ã„â€˜oÃ¡ÂºÂ¡n TÃƒÂ¡ch (Split): DÃƒÂ¹ng ThÃ¡Â»Â vÃƒÂ  RÃƒÂ¹a tÃƒÂ¬m Ã„â€˜iÃ¡Â»Æ’m giÃ¡Â»Â¯a
    template <typename t>
    static node<t>* split(node<t>* head) {
        node<t>* fast = head;
        node<t>* slow = head;

        // ThÃ¡Â»Â nhÃ¡ÂºÂ£y 2 bÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»â€ºc, RÃƒÂ¹a nhÃ¡ÂºÂ£y 1 bÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»â€ºc nhÃ¡Â»Â hÃƒÂ m get_next() cÃ¡Â»Â§a class node
        while (fast != nullptr && fast->get_next() != nullptr && fast->get_next()->get_next() != nullptr) {
            fast = fast->get_next()->get_next();
            slow = slow->get_next();
        }

        // CÃ¡ÂºÂ¯t Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»Â©t liÃƒÂªn kÃ¡ÂºÂ¿t Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»Æ’ chia Ã„â€˜ÃƒÂ´i
        node<t>* second_half = slow->get_next();
        slow->set_next(nullptr);
        if (second_half != nullptr) {
            second_half->set_prev(nullptr);
        }
        
        return second_half;
    }

    // 2. Giai Ã„â€˜oÃ¡ÂºÂ¡n TrÃ¡Â»â„¢n (Merge): KhÃƒÂ¢u cÃƒÂ¡c node lÃ¡ÂºÂ¡i theo trÃ¡ÂºÂ­t tÃ¡Â»Â± cÃ¡Â»Â§a hÃƒÂ m cmp
    template <typename t>
    static node<t>* merge(node<t>* first, node<t>* second, bool (*cmp)(t, t)) {
        // NÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u 1 trong 2 nÃ¡Â»Â­a rÃ¡Â»â€”ng, trÃ¡ÂºÂ£ vÃ¡Â»Â nÃ¡Â»Â­a cÃƒÂ²n lÃ¡ÂºÂ¡i
        if (first == nullptr) return second;
        if (second == nullptr) return first;

        // So sÃƒÂ¡nh theo quy tÃ¡ÂºÂ¯c (cmp) Ã„â€˜Ã†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£c truyÃ¡Â»Ân vÃƒÂ o
        if (cmp(first->get_data(), second->get_data())) {
            first->set_next(merge(first->get_next(), second, cmp));
            if (first->get_next() != nullptr) {
                first->get_next()->set_prev(first); 
            }
            first->set_prev(nullptr);
            return first;
        } else {
            second->set_next(merge(first, second->get_next(), cmp));
            if (second->get_next() != nullptr) {
                second->get_next()->set_prev(second);
            }
            second->set_prev(nullptr);
            return second;
        }
    }

public:
    // 3. HÃƒÂ m gÃ¡Â»Âi chÃƒÂ­nh: Ã„ÂÃ¡Â»â€¡ quy chia Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»Æ’ trÃ¡Â»â€¹
    // NhÃ¡ÂºÂ­n vÃƒÂ o tham chiÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u cÃ¡Â»Â§a con trÃ¡Â»Â head_ref Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»Æ’ cÃ¡ÂºÂ­p nhÃ¡ÂºÂ­t lÃ¡ÂºÂ¡i danh sÃƒÂ¡ch gÃ¡Â»â€˜c
    template <typename t>
    static void merge_sort(node<t>** head_ref, bool (*cmp)(t, t)) {
        node<t>* head = *head_ref;
        
        // Ã„ÂiÃ¡Â»Âu kiÃ¡Â»â€¡n dÃ¡Â»Â«ng: Danh sÃƒÂ¡ch rÃ¡Â»â€”ng hoÃ¡ÂºÂ·c chÃ¡Â»â€° cÃƒÂ³ 1 node
        if (head == nullptr || head->get_next() == nullptr) {
            return;
        }

        // Chia Ã„â€˜ÃƒÂ´i
        node<t>* second_half = split(head);

        // Ã„ÂÃ¡Â»â€¡ quy sÃ¡ÂºÂ¯p xÃ¡ÂºÂ¿p 2 nÃ¡Â»Â­a
        merge_sort(&head, cmp);
        merge_sort(&second_half, cmp);

        // TrÃ¡Â»â„¢n lÃ¡ÂºÂ¡i vÃƒÂ  gÃƒÂ¡n vÃƒÂ o head_ref
        *head_ref = merge(head, second_half, cmp);
    }
};




=== FILE: .\Controller\library_controller.cpp ===
#include "library_controller.h"

library_controller::library_controller() 
    : b_service("Data/book.txt"), u_service("Data/user.txt"), 
      br_service("Data/borrow.txt"), s_service("Data/shift.txt") {
    current_logged_in_user = nullptr;
    request_file = "Data/request.txt";
    position_file = "Data/position.txt";
    
    load_requests();
    load_positions(); 
    check_system_overdue(); 
}

library_controller::~library_controller() {
    save_requests();
}

void library_controller::load_requests() {
    std::ifstream file(request_file);
    if (!file.is_open()) return;

    std::string line;
    while (std::getline(file, line)) {
        if (!line.empty() && line.back() == '\r') line.pop_back();
        if (line.empty()) continue;

        std::stringstream ss(line);
        std::string req_id, b_id, u_id, b_date_str, due_date_str, status;
        std::getline(ss, req_id, ';');
        std::getline(ss, b_id, ';');
        std::getline(ss, u_id, ';');
        std::getline(ss, b_date_str, ';');
        std::getline(ss, due_date_str, ';');
        std::getline(ss, status, ';');

        int d1, m1, y1, d2, m2, y2;
        char slash;
        std::stringstream ss_b(b_date_str); ss_b >> d1 >> slash >> m1 >> slash >> y1;
        std::stringstream ss_d(due_date_str); ss_d >> d2 >> slash >> m2 >> slash >> y2;

        borrow req(req_id, b_id, u_id, date(d1, m1, y1), date(d2, m2, y2), status);
        book_request_queue.push(req);
    }
    file.close();
}

void library_controller::save_requests() {
    std::ofstream file(request_file, std::ios::trunc);
    if (!file.is_open()) return;

    queue<borrow> temp_queue;
    while (!book_request_queue.is_empty()) {
        borrow req = book_request_queue.front();
        book_request_queue.pop();
        file << req.get_id() << ";"
             << req.get_book_id() << ";"
             << req.get_user_id() << ";"
             << req.get_borrow_date() << ";"
             << req.get_due_date() << ";"
             << req.get_status() << "\n";
        temp_queue.push(req);
    }
    while (!temp_queue.is_empty()) {
        book_request_queue.push(temp_queue.front());
        temp_queue.pop();
    }
    file.close();
}

bool library_controller::login(std::string id, std::string password) {
    current_logged_in_user = u_service.login(id, password);
    if (current_logged_in_user != nullptr) {
        check_system_overdue(); 
    }
    return (current_logged_in_user != nullptr);
}

void library_controller::logout() {
    current_logged_in_user = nullptr;
}

user* library_controller::get_current_user() const {
    return current_logged_in_user;
}

std::string library_controller::get_current_role() const {
    if (current_logged_in_user == nullptr) return "";
    return current_logged_in_user->get_role();
}

linked_list<book> library_controller::search_books(std::string keyword) {
    return b_service.search_books(keyword);
}

linked_list<book> library_controller::get_related_books(std::string category, std::string exclude_book_id) {
    linked_list<book> result;
    linked_list<book> same_cat = b_service.search_books(category);
    node<book>* curr = same_cat.get_head();
    while (curr != nullptr) {
        if (curr->get_data().get_id() != exclude_book_id) {
            result.push_back(curr->get_data());
        }
        curr = curr->get_next();
    }
    return result;
}

// LÆ¯U Ká»† SÃCH KHI THÃŠM SÃCH
bool library_controller::add_new_book(std::string id, std::string title, std::string author, std::string category, int qty, int year, std::string shelf) {
    book new_b(id, title, author, category, qty, year);
    if (b_service.add_book(new_b)) {
        book_undo_stack.push(action_log<book>(id, "ADD", new_b));
        
        std::string lower_id = search_algo::to_lower(search_algo::trim(id));
        position_list.push_back(book_location(lower_id, category, shelf));
        save_positions();
        return true;
    }
    return false;
}

bool library_controller::update_book_info(std::string old_id, book book_n) {
    // NÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€¢i mÃƒÂ£ sÃƒÂ¡ch mÃƒÂ  sÃƒÂ¡ch cÃ…Â© Ã„â€˜ang cÃƒÂ³ ngÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Âi mÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£n -> KhÃƒÂ´ng cho Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€¢i Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»Æ’ trÃƒÂ¡nh sai lÃ¡Â»â€¡ch dÃ¡Â»Â¯ liÃ¡Â»â€¡u
    if (old_id != book_n.get_id() && br_service.is_book_borrowed(old_id)) {
        return false;
    }
    return b_service.update_book(old_id, book_n);
}

bool library_controller::delete_book(std::string id) {
    // RÃƒâ‚¬NG BUÃ¡Â»ËœC TOÃƒâ‚¬N VÃ¡ÂºÂ¸N: KhÃƒÂ´ng cho xÃƒÂ³a sÃƒÂ¡ch Ã„â€˜ang cÃƒÂ³ ngÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Âi mÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£n hoÃ¡ÂºÂ·c quÃƒÂ¡ hÃ¡ÂºÂ¡n chÃ†Â°a trÃ¡ÂºÂ£
    if (br_service.is_book_borrowed(id)) {
        return false; 
    }
    return b_service.delete_book(id);
}

node<book>* library_controller::get_book_by_id(std::string id) {
    return b_service.get_book_by_id(id);
}

void library_controller::sort_books(bool (*cmp)(book, book)) {
    b_service.sort_books(cmp);
}

linked_list<borrow> library_controller::filter_borrows_by_date(date start_date, date end_date) {
    return br_service.filter_borrows_by_date(start_date, end_date);
}

bool library_controller::borrow_book(std::string borrow_id, std::string book_id, std::string user_id, date borrow_date, date due_date) {
    return br_service.create_borrow_ticket(borrow_id, book_id, user_id, borrow_date, due_date, b_service, u_service);
}

bool library_controller::return_book(std::string borrow_id) {
    return br_service.return_book(borrow_id, b_service);
}

bool library_controller::delete_borrow(std::string borrow_id) {
    return br_service.delete_borrow_ticket(borrow_id);
}

node<borrow>* library_controller::get_borrow_by_id(std::string id) {
    return br_service.get_borrow_by_id(id);
}

linked_list<borrow> library_controller::get_my_borrows(std::string user_id, bool active_only) {
    return br_service.get_borrows_by_user(user_id, active_only);
}

void library_controller::check_system_overdue() {
    time_t t = time(0);
    tm* now = localtime(&t);
    if (now == nullptr) return; 
    date today(now->tm_mday, now->tm_mon + 1, now->tm_year + 1900);
    br_service.check_and_update_overdue(today);
}
bool library_controller::has_overdue_borrows(std::string user_id) const {
    return br_service.has_overdue_borrows(user_id);
}

bool library_controller::is_request_id_in_queue(std::string req_id) {
    queue<borrow> temp_queue;
    bool found = false;
    while (!book_request_queue.is_empty()) {
        borrow req = book_request_queue.front();
        book_request_queue.pop();
        if (req.get_id() == req_id) {
            found = true;
        }
        temp_queue.push(req);
    }
    while (!temp_queue.is_empty()) {
        book_request_queue.push(temp_queue.front());
        temp_queue.pop();
    }
    return found;
}

// HÃ€M Má»šI: Sinh mÃ£ REQ tá»± Ä‘á»™ng
std::string library_controller::generate_request_id() {
    // Biáº¿n tÄ©nh (static) sáº½ giá»¯ giÃ¡ trá»‹ trong suá»‘t quÃ¡ trÃ¬nh app cháº¡y
    static int counter = 1; 
    
    time_t now = time(0);
    
    // GhÃ©p thá»i gian (giÃ¢y) vá»›i bá»™ Ä‘áº¿m (counter)
    // VÃ­ dá»¥: REQ1732549200_1, REQ1732549200_2
    std::string unique_id = "REQ" + std::to_string(now) + "_" + std::to_string(counter);
    
    counter++; // TÄƒng bá»™ Ä‘áº¿m cho láº§n báº¥m tiáº¿p theo
    
    return unique_id;
}

bool library_controller::register_book_request(std::string req_id, std::string book_id, std::string user_id, date b_date, date due_date) {
    node<book>* b_node = b_service.get_book_by_id(book_id);
    if (b_node == nullptr) return false;
    if (b_node->get_data().get_quantity() <= 0) return false;
    if (due_date < b_date) return false;
    if (br_service.get_borrow_by_id(req_id) != nullptr) return false;
    if (is_request_id_in_queue(req_id)) return false;
    if (br_service.is_user_borrowing_book(user_id, book_id)) return false;
    if (br_service.has_overdue_borrows(user_id)) return false;
    if (br_service.count_active_borrows_by_user(user_id) >= 7) return false; // Cháº·n mÆ°á»£n > 7 cuá»‘n

    borrow ticket(req_id, book_id, user_id, b_date, due_date, "Cho duyet");
    book_request_queue.push(ticket);
    save_requests();
    return true;
}

bool library_controller::has_pending_requests() const {
    return !book_request_queue.is_empty();
}

borrow library_controller::peek_next_request() const {
    return book_request_queue.front();
}

bool library_controller::approve_next_request() {
    if (book_request_queue.is_empty()) return false;
    borrow req = book_request_queue.front();

    if (borrow_book(req.get_id(), req.get_book_id(), req.get_user_id(), req.get_borrow_date(), req.get_due_date())) {
        book_request_queue.pop();
        save_requests();
        return true;
    }
    return false; 
}

bool library_controller::reject_next_request() {
    if (book_request_queue.is_empty()) return false;
    book_request_queue.pop();
    save_requests(); 
    return true;
}

int library_controller::get_pending_request_count() const {
    return book_request_queue.get_size();
}

    return s_service.register_shift(d, session, admin_id, u_service);

bool library_controller::delete_user(std::string id) {

        return false; 
    }
    if (br_service.has_active_borrows(id)) {
        return false;
    }
    if (s_service.has_shifts(id)) {
        return false;
    }
    return u_service.delete_user(id);
}

    return u_service.get_user_by_id(id);
}

void library_controller::load_positions() {
    std::ifstream file(position_file);
    if (!file.is_open()) {
        file.open("position.txt");
    }
    if (!file.is_open()) return;

    std::string line;
    while (std::getline(file, line)) {
        if (!line.empty() && line.back() == '\r') line.pop_back();
        if (line.empty()) continue;

        std::stringstream ss(line);
        std::string b_id, cat, shelf;
        std::getline(ss, b_id, ';');
        std::getline(ss, cat, ';');
        std::getline(ss, shelf, ';');

        b_id = search_algo::to_lower(search_algo::trim(b_id));
        shelf = search_algo::trim(shelf);

        position_list.push_back(book_location(b_id, cat, shelf));
    }
    file.close();
}

void library_controller::save_positions() {
    std::ofstream file(position_file, std::ios::trunc);
    if (!file.is_open()) return;
    node<book_location>* curr = position_list.get_head();
    while (curr != nullptr) {
        file << curr->get_data().book_id << ";" << curr->get_data().category << ";" << curr->get_data().shelf << "\n";
        curr = curr->get_next();
    }
    file.close();
}

std::string library_controller::get_book_shelf(std::string book_id) {
    std::string target_id = search_algo::to_lower(search_algo::trim(book_id));
    node<book_location>* curr = position_list.get_head();

    while (curr != nullptr) {
        if (curr->get_data().book_id == target_id) {
            return curr->get_data().shelf;
        }
        curr = curr->get_next();
    }
    return "Chua xep ke";
}

bool library_controller::has_active_borrows(std::string user_id) const {
    return br_service.has_active_borrows(user_id);
}

linked_list<book> library_controller::filter_books_multi(std::string title, std::string author, std::string category, int year) {
    return b_service.filter_books(title, author, category, year);
}

int library_controller::get_user_active_borrow_count(std::string user_id) const {
    return br_service.count_active_borrows_by_user(user_id);
}

// THUáº¬T TOÃN Äá»’ THá»Š (GRAPH BFS) - Gá»¢I Ã SÃCH
linked_list<book> library_controller::get_graph_recommendations(std::string book_id) {
    linked_list<book> all = b_service.search_books("");
    node<book>* target = b_service.get_book_by_id(book_id);
    if (target == nullptr || all.get_size() <= 1) return linked_list<book>();

    int cap = all.get_size() + 2; 
    graph<book> g(cap > 100 ? cap : 100); 

    node<book>* curr = all.get_head();
    while (curr != nullptr) {
        g.add_vertex(curr->get_data());
        curr = curr->get_next();
    }

    for (int i = 0; i < g.get_num_vertices(); ++i) {
        book b1 = g.get_vertex_data(i);
        for (int j = i + 1; j < g.get_num_vertices(); ++j) {
            book b2 = g.get_vertex_data(j);
            if (search_algo::contains(b1.get_category(), b2.get_category()) ||
                search_algo::contains(b1.get_author(), b2.get_author())) {
                g.add_edge(i, j, false);
            }
        }
    }

    linked_list<book> bfs_list = graph_algo::BFS(g, target->get_data());
    linked_list<book> result;
    node<book>* r_curr = bfs_list.get_head();
    while (r_curr != nullptr) {
        if (!(r_curr->get_data() == target->get_data())) {
            result.push_back(r_curr->get_data());
        }
        r_curr = r_curr->get_next();
    }
    return result;
}

std::string library_controller::get_hot_category() {
    linked_list<book> all = b_service.search_books("");
    if (all.empty()) return "Giao trinh";

    std::string best_cat = all.get_head()->get_data().get_category();
    int max_cnt = -1;
    for (node<book>* p = all.get_head(); p != nullptr; p = p->get_next()) {
        int cnt = br_service.count_borrows_by_book(p->get_data().get_id());
        if (cnt > max_cnt) {
            max_cnt = cnt;
            best_cat = p->get_data().get_category();
        }
    }
    return best_cat;
}

// HOÃ€N TÃC (UNDO)
bool library_controller::can_undo_book() const { return !book_undo_stack.empty(); }

bool library_controller::undo_last_book_action(std::string& out_message) {
    if (book_undo_stack.empty()) {
        out_message = "Khong co thao tac sach nao trong Stack de hoan tac!";
        return false;
    }
    action_log<book> last = book_undo_stack.peek();
    book_undo_stack.pop();

    bool success = false;
    if (last.get_action_type() == "ADD") {
        success = b_service.delete_book(last.get_target_id());
        out_message = "Da hoan tac: Xoa sach vua them [" + last.get_target_id() + "]";
    } else if (last.get_action_type() == "DELETE") {
        success = b_service.add_book(last.get_backup_data());
        out_message = "Da hoan tac: Khoi phuc sach vua xoa [" + last.get_target_id() + "]";
    } else if (last.get_action_type() == "UPDATE") {
        success = b_service.update_book(last.get_target_id(), last.get_backup_data());
        out_message = "Da hoan tac: Khoi phuc thong tin cu cua sach [" + last.get_target_id() + "]";
    }
    return success;
}

bool library_controller::undo_book_action(std::string& out_message) {
    return undo_last_book_action(out_message);
}

bool library_controller::can_undo_user() const { return !user_undo_stack.empty(); }
bool library_controller::undo_user_action(std::string& out_message) { return false; }
bool library_controller::can_undo_borrow() const { return !borrow_undo_stack.empty(); }
bool library_controller::undo_borrow_action(std::string& out_message) { return false; }

=== FILE: .\GUI\library_gui.cpp ===
#include "library_gui.h"
#include "raylib.h"

#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include "ui_font.h"
#include "ui_layout.h"

namespace {
    const int WINDOW_W = 1220;
    const int WINDOW_H = 750;
    const int SIDEBAR_W = 280;
    const int CONTENT_X = 305;
    const int CONTENT_W = 890;
    const int ROWS_PER_PAGE = 6;

    void append_utf8(char* destination, int capacity, const char* source) {
        if (destination == nullptr || source == nullptr || capacity <= 1) return;

        int length = (int)std::strlen(destination);
        int i = 0;
        while (source[i] != '\0') {
            unsigned char first = (unsigned char)source[i];
            int count = 1;

            if (first < 0x80) count = 1;
            else if ((first & 0xE0) == 0xC0) count = 2;
            else if ((first & 0xF0) == 0xE0) count = 3;
            else if ((first & 0xF8) == 0xF0) count = 4;
            else { ++i; continue; }

            bool valid = true;
            for (int j = 1; j < count; ++j) {
                if (source[i + j] == '\0' ||
                    (((unsigned char)source[i + j]) & 0xC0) != 0x80) {
                    valid = false;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if (!valid) { ++i; continue; }

            // Bo qua ky tu dieu khien, nhung van giu dau cach.
            if (count == 1 && first < 32) {
                ++i;
                continue;
            }

            if (length + count >= capacity) break;
            std::memcpy(destination + length, source + i, count);
            length += count;
            destination[length] = '\0';
            i += count;
        }
    }

    void append_codepoint(char* destination, int capacity, int codepoint) {
        if (codepoint < 32 || codepoint > 0x10FFFF ||
            (codepoint >= 0xD800 && codepoint <= 0xDFFF)) return;

        int byte_count = 0;
        const char* encoded = CodepointToUTF8(codepoint, &byte_count);
        if (encoded != nullptr && byte_count > 0) {
            int length = (int)std::strlen(destination);
            if (length + byte_count < capacity) {
                std::memcpy(destination + length, encoded, byte_count);
                destination[length + byte_count] = '\0';
            }
        }
    }

    void erase_last_utf8_character(char* value) {
        int length = (int)std::strlen(value);
        if (length == 0) return;

        --length;
        while (length > 0 &&
               (((unsigned char)value[length]) & 0xC0) == 0x80) {
            --length;
        }
        value[length] = '\0';
    }

    int key_to_ascii(int key, bool shift) {
        if (key >= KEY_A && key <= KEY_Z) {
            int c = 'a' + (key - KEY_A);
            return shift ? c - 32 : c;
        }
        if (key >= KEY_ZERO && key <= KEY_NINE) {
            return '0' + (key - KEY_ZERO);
        }
        if (key == KEY_SPACE) return ' ';
        if (key == KEY_MINUS) return shift ? '_' : '-';
        if (key == KEY_EQUAL) return shift ? '+' : '=';
        if (key == KEY_PERIOD) return '.';
        if (key == KEY_COMMA) return ',';
        if (key == KEY_SLASH) return shift ? '?' : '/';
        if (key == KEY_SEMICOLON) return shift ? ':' : ';';
        if (key == KEY_APOSTROPHE) return shift ? '"' : '\'';
        if (key == KEY_LEFT_BRACKET) return shift ? '{' : '[';
        if (key == KEY_RIGHT_BRACKET) return shift ? '}' : ']';
        if (key == KEY_BACKSLASH) return shift ? '|' : '\\';
        return 0;
    }

    void push_utf8(std::string& out, int cp) {
        int count = 0;
        const char* encoded = CodepointToUTF8(cp, &count);
        if (encoded && count > 0) out.append(encoded, count);
    }

    bool is_vowel(char c) {
        return c == 'a' || c == 'e' || c == 'i' || c == 'o' || c == 'u' || c == 'y';
    }

    int shaped_vowel(char c, char shape) {
        if (c == 'a' && shape == 'a') return 0x00E2;
        if (c == 'a' && shape == 'w') return 0x0103;
        if (c == 'e' && shape == 'e') return 0x00EA;
        if (c == 'o' && shape == 'o') return 0x00F4;
        if (c == 'o' && shape == 'w') return 0x01A1;
        if (c == 'u' && shape == 'w') return 0x01B0;
        if (c == 'd' && shape == 'd') return 0x0111;
        return c;
    }

    int tone_vowel(int cp, int tone) {
        // tone: 1 sac, 2 huyen, 3 hoi, 4 nga, 5 nang
        static const int bases[] = {'a','e','i','o','u','y',0x0103,0x00E2,0x00EA,0x00F4,0x01A1,0x01B0};
        static const int table[][5] = {
            {0x00E1,0x00E0,0x1EA3,0x00E3,0x1EA1},
            {0x00E9,0x00E8,0x1EBB,0x1EBD,0x1EB9},
            {0x00ED,0x00EC,0x1EC9,0x0129,0x1ECB},
            {0x00F3,0x00F2,0x1ECF,0x00F5,0x1ECD},
            {0x00FA,0x00F9,0x1EE7,0x0169,0x1EE5},
            {0x00FD,0x1EF3,0x1EF7,0x1EF9,0x1EF5},
            {0x1EAF,0x1EB1,0x1EB3,0x1EB5,0x1EB7},
            {0x1EA5,0x1EA7,0x1EA9,0x1EAB,0x1EAD},
            {0x1EBF,0x1EC1,0x1EC3,0x1EC5,0x1EC7},
            {0x1ED1,0x1ED3,0x1ED5,0x1ED7,0x1ED9},
            {0x1EDB,0x1EDD,0x1EDF,0x1EE1,0x1EE3},
            {0x1EE9,0x1EEB,0x1EED,0x1EEF,0x1EF1}
        };
        for (int i = 0; i < 12; ++i) if (cp == bases[i]) return table[i][tone - 1];
        return cp;
    }

    std::string convert_telex_word(const std::string& raw) {
        std::vector<int> chars;
        int tone = 0;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < raw.size(); ++i) {
            char c = raw[i];
            if (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') c = (char)(c + 32);
            if (c == 's' || c == 'f' || c == 'r' || c == 'x' || c == 'j') {
                bool has_vowel = false;
                for (int v : chars) if (is_vowel((char)v) || v == 0x0103 || v == 0x00E2 || v == 0x00EA || v == 0x00F4 || v == 0x01A1 || v == 0x01B0) has_vowel = true;
                if (has_vowel && i == raw.size() - 1) {
                    tone = c == 's' ? 1 : c == 'f' ? 2 : c == 'r' ? 3 : c == 'x' ? 4 : 5;
                    continue;
                }
            }
            if (c == 'z' && i == raw.size() - 1 && tone == 0) continue;
            if (!chars.empty()) {
                int last = chars.back();
                if (c == 'w' && (last == 'a' || last == 'o' || last == 'u')) {
                    chars.back() = shaped_vowel((char)last, 'w');
                    continue;
                }
                if ((c == 'a' && last == 'a') || (c == 'e' && last == 'e') || (c == 'o' && last == 'o') || (c == 'd' && last == 'd')) {
                    chars.back() = shaped_vowel(c, c);
                    continue;
                }
                if (c == 'w' && last == 'u') { chars.back() = 0x01B0; continue; }
            }
            chars.push_back((unsigned char)c);
        }

        int vowel_positions[8];
        int vowel_count = 0;
        bool skip_u = chars.size() > 1 && chars[0] == 'q' && chars[1] == 'u';
        bool skip_i = chars.size() > 2 && chars[0] == 'g' && chars[1] == 'i';
        for (int i = 0; i < (int)chars.size(); ++i) {
            int c = chars[i];
            if ((skip_u && i == 1) || (skip_i && i == 1)) continue;
            if (is_vowel((char)c) || c == 0x0103 || c == 0x00E2 || c == 0x00EA || c == 0x00F4 || c == 0x01A1 || c == 0x01B0) {
                if (vowel_count < 8) vowel_positions[vowel_count++] = i;
            }
        }
        if (tone && vowel_count) {
            int target = vowel_positions[vowel_count - 1];
            if (vowel_count >= 3) {
                target = vowel_positions[vowel_count / 2];
                if (chars[vowel_positions[vowel_count - 1]] == 0x00EA) target = vowel_positions[vowel_count - 1];
            } else if (vowel_count == 2) {
                int first = chars[vowel_positions[0]], second = chars[vowel_positions[1]];
                if ((first == 'i' && second == 'a') || (first == 'u' && second == 'a') || (first == 'u' && second == 0x01A1)) target = vowel_positions[0];
            }
            chars[target] = tone_vowel(chars[target], tone);
        }

        if (!raw.empty() && raw[0] >= 'A' && raw[0] <= 'Z' && !chars.empty()) {
            if (chars[0] >= 'a' && chars[0] <= 'z') chars[0] -= 32;
            static const int lower[] = {'a','e','i','o','u','y',0x0103,0x00E2,0x00EA,0x00F4,0x01A1,0x01B0,0x0111};
            static const int upper[] = {'A','E','I','O','U','Y',0x0102,0x00C2,0x00CA,0x00D4,0x01A0,0x01AF,0x0110};
            for (int i = 0; i < 13; ++i) {
                if (chars[0] == lower[i]) { chars[0] = upper[i]; break; }
            }
            // Cac nguyen am da co dau thanh: doi sang ma Unicode viet hoa tuong ung.
            static const int lower_tone[][5] = {
                {0x00E1,0x00E0,0x1EA3,0x00E3,0x1EA1}, {0x00E9,0x00E8,0x1EBB,0x1EBD,0x1EB9},
                {0x00ED,0x00EC,0x1EC9,0x0129,0x1ECB}, {0x00F3,0x00F2,0x1ECF,0x00F5,0x1ECD},
                {0x00FA,0x00F9,0x1EE7,0x0169,0x1EE5}, {0x00FD,0x1EF3,0x1EF7,0x1EF9,0x1EF5},
                {0x1EAF,0x1EB1,0x1EB3,0x1EB5,0x1EB7}, {0x1EA5,0x1EA7,0x1EA9,0x1EAB,0x1EAD},
                {0x1EBF,0x1EC1,0x1EC3,0x1EC5,0x1EC7}, {0x1ED1,0x1ED3,0x1ED5,0x1ED7,0x1ED9},
                {0x1EDB,0x1EDD,0x1EDF,0x1EE1,0x1EE3}, {0x1EE9,0x1EEB,0x1EED,0x1EEF,0x1EF1}
            };
            static const int upper_tone[][5] = {
                {0x00C1,0x00C0,0x1EA2,0x00C3,0x1EA0}, {0x00C9,0x00C8,0x1EBA,0x1EBC,0x1EB8},
                {0x00CD,0x00CC,0x1EC8,0x0128,0x1ECA}, {0x00D3,0x00D2,0x1ECE,0x00D5,0x1ECC},
                {0x00DA,0x00D9,0x1EE6,0x0168,0x1EE4}, {0x00DD,0x1EF2,0x1EF6,0x1EF8,0x1EF4},
                {0x1EAE,0x1EB0,0x1EB2,0x1EB4,0x1EB6}, {0x1EA4,0x1EA6,0x1EA8,0x1EAA,0x1EAC},
                {0x1EBE,0x1EC0,0x1EC2,0x1EC4,0x1EC6}, {0x1ED0,0x1ED2,0x1ED4,0x1ED6,0x1ED8},
                {0x1EDA,0x1EDC,0x1EDE,0x1EE0,0x1EE2}, {0x1EE8,0x1EEA,0x1EEC,0x1EEE,0x1EF0}
            };
            for (int i = 0; i < 12; ++i) for (int j = 0; j < 5; ++j)
                if (chars[0] == lower_tone[i][j]) chars[0] = upper_tone[i][j];
        }

        std::string out;
        for (int cp : chars) push_utf8(out, cp);
        return out;
    }

    Color page_background = {245, 247, 251, 255};
    Color panel_background = {255, 255, 255, 255};
    Color sidebar_background = {235, 239, 246, 255};

    void draw_panel() {
        DrawRectangleRounded(
            {(float)CONTENT_X, 102.0f, (float)CONTENT_W, 578.0f},
            0.03f, 8, panel_background);
    }

    void draw_table_header(const char* const* headers, const int* columns,
                           int count, int y) {
        DrawRectangle(CONTENT_X + 15, y, CONTENT_W - 30, 38, DARKBLUE);
        for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
            DrawText(headers[i], columns[i], y + 9, 18, WHITE);
        }
    }

    void draw_paging_hint(int page, int total, int y) {
        int total_pages = total == 0 ? 1 : (total + ROWS_PER_PAGE - 1) / ROWS_PER_PAGE;
        DrawText(TextFormat("Trang %d/%d", page + 1, total_pages),
                 CONTENT_X + 395, y, 19, DARKGRAY);
    }

    // ---- Cac ham doi chuoi luu trong file du lieu (khong dau) sang tieng Viet co dau.
    // ---- Chi dung de HIEN THI, du lieu goc va logic nghiep vu khong thay doi.
    std::string display_status(const std::string& status) {
        if (status == "Dang muon") return "Äang mÆ°á»£n";
        if (status == "Da tra") return "ÄÃ£ tráº£";
        if (status == "Qua han") return "QuÃ¡ háº¡n";
        if (status == "Cho duyet") return "Chá» duyá»‡t";
        return status;
    }

    std::string display_session(const std::string& session) {
        if (session == "sang") return "SÃ¡ng";
        if (session == "chieu") return "Chiá»u";
        return session;
    }

    std::string display_role(const std::string& role) {
        if (role == "manager") return "Quáº£n lÃ½";
        if (role == "admin") return "Quáº£n trá»‹ viÃªn";
        if (role == "user") return "Äá»™c giáº£";
        return role;
    }

    std::string display_shelf(const std::string& shelf) {
        if (shelf == "Chua xep ke") return "ChÆ°a xáº¿p ká»‡";
        return shelf;
    }

    std::string format_date(const date& d) {
        return std::to_string(d.get_day()) + "/" +
               std::to_string(d.get_month()) + "/" +
               std::to_string(d.get_year());
    }

    // Chap nhan "sang"/"sÃ¡ng" va "chieu"/"chiá»u"; tra ve gia tri luu trong he thong.
    std::string normalize_session(const char* text) {
        std::string s = text == nullptr ? "" : text;
        size_t start = 0;
        while (start < s.size() && s[start] == ' ') ++start;
        size_t end = s.size();
        while (end > start && s[end - 1] == ' ') --end;
        s = s.substr(start, end - start);

        std::string lower;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < s.size(); ++i) {
            char c = s[i];
            lower += (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') ? (char)(c + 32) : c;
        }
        if (lower == "sang" || lower == "sÃ¡ng") return "sang";
        if (lower == "chieu" || lower == "chiá»u") return "chieu";
        return "";
    }
}

library_gui::library_gui(library_controller& controller)
    : controller(controller) {
    reset();
}

void library_gui::reset() {
    current_page = DASHBOARD;
    history.clear();
    logout_requested = false;
    list_page = 0;
    active_field = nullptr;
    active_field_capacity = 0;
    telex_raw_word.clear();
    telex_field = nullptr;
    telex_word_start = 0;
    telex_enabled = true;
    message.clear();
    message_is_error = false;
    showing_related = false;
    selected_book_id.clear();
    confirm_open = false;
    confirm_just_opened = false;
    input_locked = false;
    confirm_title.clear();
    confirm_text.clear();
    confirm_ok_label.clear();
    confirm_ok_color = BLUE;
    confirm_action = nullptr;
    clear_fields();
}

bool library_gui::wants_logout() const {
    return logout_requested;
}

void library_gui::clear_fields() {
    field_a[0] = '\0';
    field_b[0] = '\0';
    field_c[0] = '\0';
    field_d[0] = '\0';
    field_e[0] = '\0';
    field_f[0] = '\0';
    field_g[0] = '\0';
    active_field = nullptr;
    active_field_capacity = 0;
    telex_raw_word.clear();
    telex_field = nullptr;
    telex_word_start = 0;
    list_page = 0;
}

void library_gui::switch_page(page next_page) {
    current_page = next_page;
    message.clear();
    message_is_error = false;
    showing_related = false;
    clear_fields();
}

// Di vao trang con (them/sua/xoa...): nho trang hien tai de nut "Quay lai" tro ve dung buoc truoc.
void library_gui::open_page(page next_page) {
    if (next_page == current_page) return;
    history.push_back(current_page);
    switch_page(next_page);
}

// Chon muc tu menu ben trai: bat dau mot luong moi, "Quay lai" se ve Tong quan.
void library_gui::open_root_page(page next_page) {
    history.clear();
    if (next_page != DASHBOARD) history.push_back(DASHBOARD);
    switch_page(next_page);
}

void library_gui::go_back() {
    if (history.empty()) {
        switch_page(DASHBOARD);
        return;
    }
    page previous = history.back();
    history.pop_back();
    switch_page(previous);
}

void library_gui::set_message(const std::string& text, bool is_error) {
    message = text;
    message_is_error = is_error;
}

void library_gui::ask_confirm(const std::string& title, const std::string& text,
                              const std::string& ok_label, Color ok_color,
                              std::function<void()> action) {
    confirm_open = true;
    confirm_just_opened = true;   // bo qua cÃº click vá»«a má»Ÿ há»™p thoáº¡i
    confirm_title = title;
    confirm_text = text;
    confirm_ok_label = ok_label;
    confirm_ok_color = ok_color;
    confirm_action = action;
}

void library_gui::draw_confirm_dialog() {
    if (!confirm_open) return;

    // Hop thoai tu xu ly nut cua no, nen mo khoa dau vao cho no.
    // Frame vua mo thi van khoa de cu click mo hop thoai khong bi tinh la "Xac nhan".
    input_locked = confirm_just_opened;
    confirm_just_opened = false;

    DrawRectangle(0, 0, WINDOW_W, WINDOW_H, {0, 0, 0, 130});

    Rectangle box = {340, 220, 540, 290};
    DrawRectangleRounded(box, 0.05f, 10, WHITE);
    DrawRectangleRoundedLines(box, 0.05f, 10, DARKBLUE);

    DrawText(confirm_title.c_str(), (int)box.x + 28, (int)box.y + 22, 26, DARKBLUE);
    DrawRectangle((int)box.x + 28, (int)box.y + 60, (int)box.width - 56, 2, LIGHTGRAY);

    // Tach noi dung theo dau xuong dong.
    std::string line;
    int line_index = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i <= confirm_text.size(); ++i) {
        if (i == confirm_text.size() || confirm_text[i] == '\n') {
            DrawText(line.c_str(), (int)box.x + 28,
                     (int)box.y + 78 + line_index * 30, 20, DARKGRAY);
            line.clear();
            ++line_index;
        } else {
            line += confirm_text[i];
        }
    }

    bool cancelled = draw_button({box.x + 60, box.y + 220, 190, 48}, "Há»§y", GRAY);
    bool confirmed = draw_button({box.x + 290, box.y + 220, 190, 48},
                                 confirm_ok_label.c_str(), confirm_ok_color);

    if (confirmed) {
        std::function<void()> action = confirm_action;
        confirm_open = false;
        confirm_action = nullptr;
        if (action) action();
    } else if (cancelled) {
        confirm_open = false;
        confirm_action = nullptr;
        set_message("ÄÃ£ há»§y thao tÃ¡c.");
    }
}

bool library_gui::is_blank(const char* text) const {
    if (text == nullptr) return true;
    while (*text != '\0') {
        if (*text != ' ' && *text != '\t') return false;
        ++text;
    }
    return true;
}

bool library_gui::read_integer(const char* text, int& value,
                               int min_value, int max_value) const {
    if (is_blank(text)) return false;

    char* end = nullptr;
    long parsed = std::strtol(text, &end, 10);
    if (*end != '\0' || parsed < min_value || parsed > max_value) {
        return false;
    }

    value = (int)parsed;
    return true;
}

bool library_gui::read_date(const char* text, date& value) const {
    int day = 0;
    int month = 0;
    int year = 0;
    char extra = '\0';

    if (std::sscanf(text, "%d/%d/%d%c", &day, &month, &year, &extra) != 3) {
        return false;
    }

    date parsed(day, month, year);
    if (parsed.get_day() != day ||
        parsed.get_month() != month ||
        parsed.get_year() != year) {
        return false;
    }

    value = parsed;
    return true;
}

void library_gui::collect_text_input() {
    if (input_locked || active_field == nullptr || active_field_capacity <= 1) return;

    if (IsKeyPressed(KEY_F2)) {
        telex_enabled = !telex_enabled;
        telex_raw_word.clear();
        telex_field = nullptr;
    }

    if (telex_field != active_field) {
        telex_field = active_field;
        telex_raw_word.clear();
        telex_word_start = (int)std::strlen(active_field);
    }

    bool shift = IsKeyDown(KEY_LEFT_SHIFT) || IsKeyDown(KEY_RIGHT_SHIFT);
    bool control = IsKeyDown(KEY_LEFT_CONTROL) || IsKeyDown(KEY_RIGHT_CONTROL);
    int key = GetKeyPressed();
    while (key > 0) {
        if (control && (key == KEY_V)) {
            const char* clipboard = GetClipboardText();
            if (clipboard) append_utf8(active_field, active_field_capacity, clipboard);
            telex_raw_word.clear();
            telex_word_start = (int)std::strlen(active_field);
        } else if (key == KEY_BACKSPACE) {
            if (telex_enabled && !telex_raw_word.empty() && telex_field == active_field) {
                telex_raw_word.pop_back();
                active_field[telex_word_start] = '\0';
                std::string converted = convert_telex_word(telex_raw_word);
                append_utf8(active_field, active_field_capacity, converted.c_str());
                if (telex_raw_word.empty()) telex_word_start = (int)std::strlen(active_field);
            } else {
                erase_last_utf8_character(active_field);
                telex_word_start = (int)std::strlen(active_field);
            }
        } else if (key == KEY_F2) {
            // Da xu ly phim chuyen che do o tren.
        } else {
            int c = key_to_ascii(key, shift);
            if (c != 0) {
                if (telex_enabled && c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') c += 32;
                if (telex_enabled && c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') {
                    if (telex_raw_word.empty()) telex_word_start = (int)std::strlen(active_field);
                    telex_raw_word += (char)c;
                    active_field[telex_word_start] = '\0';
                    std::string converted = convert_telex_word(telex_raw_word);
                    append_utf8(active_field, active_field_capacity, converted.c_str());
                } else {
                    telex_raw_word.clear();
                    char text[2] = {(char)c, '\0'};
                    append_utf8(active_field, active_field_capacity, text);
                    telex_word_start = (int)std::strlen(active_field);
                }
            }
        }
        key = GetKeyPressed();
    }
}

void library_gui::draw_input(Rectangle box, const char* label,
                             char* value, int capacity) {
    DrawText(label, (int)box.x, (int)box.y - 24, 18, DARKGRAY);

    bool focused = active_field == value;
    DrawRectangleRounded(box, 0.08f, 6, WHITE);
    DrawRectangleRoundedLines(box, 0.08f, 6, focused ? BLUE : LIGHTGRAY);
    DrawText(value, (int)box.x + 12, (int)box.y + 11, 20, DARKGRAY);

    if (!input_locked &&
        CheckCollisionPointRec(ui_layout::mouse_position(), box) &&
        IsMouseButtonPressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT)) {
        active_field = value;
        active_field_capacity = capacity;
        telex_raw_word.clear();
        telex_field = value;
        telex_word_start = (int)std::strlen(value);
    }
}

bool library_gui::draw_button(Rectangle box, const char* label, Color color) {
    Vector2 mouse = ui_layout::mouse_position();
    bool hovered = !input_locked && CheckCollisionPointRec(mouse, box);
    Color fill = hovered ? Color{(unsigned char)(color.r > 25 ? color.r - 25 : 0),
                                 (unsigned char)(color.g > 25 ? color.g - 25 : 0),
                                 (unsigned char)(color.b > 25 ? color.b - 25 : 0),
                                 255}
                         : color;
    DrawRectangleRounded(box, 0.12f, 6, fill);

    int font_size = 19;
    int text_width = MeasureText(label, font_size);
    DrawText(label, (int)(box.x + (box.width - text_width) / 2),
             (int)(box.y + (box.height - font_size) / 2), font_size, WHITE);

    return hovered && IsMouseButtonPressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT);
}

void library_gui::draw_message() {
    if (message.empty()) return;
    DrawText(message.c_str(), CONTENT_X + 20, 695, 20,
             message_is_error ? RED : DARKGREEN);
}

void library_gui::draw_back_button() {
    if (current_page != DASHBOARD &&
        draw_button({CONTENT_X + CONTENT_W - 150, 115, 130, 42}, "Quay láº¡i", GRAY)) {
        go_back();
    }
}

void library_gui::draw_shell(const std::string& role) {
    ClearBackground(page_background);
    DrawRectangle(0, 0, WINDOW_W, 82, DARKBLUE);
    DrawText("PBL2 - QUáº¢N LÃ THÆ¯ VIá»†N", 28, 25, 28, WHITE);

    std::string role_name = display_role(role);
    int role_width = MeasureText(role_name.c_str(), 22);
    DrawText(role_name.c_str(), WINDOW_W - 28 - role_width, 29, 22, WHITE);

    DrawRectangle(0, 82, SIDEBAR_W, WINDOW_H - 82, sidebar_background);
    DrawText("CHá»¨C NÄ‚NG", 28, 110, 21, DARKGRAY);
    draw_panel();
}

void library_gui::draw_sidebar(const std::string& role) {
    struct menu_item {
        const char* label;
        page destination;
    };

    std::vector<menu_item> menu;

    if (role == "manager") {
        menu = {
            {"Tá»•ng quan", DASHBOARD},
            {"Quáº£n lÃ½ sÃ¡ch", BOOKS},
            {"Phiáº¿u mÆ°á»£n", BORROWS},
            {"YÃªu cáº§u mÆ°á»£n", REQUESTS},
            {"Ca trá»±c", SHIFTS},
            {"TÃ­nh lÆ°Æ¡ng", SALARY},
            {"NgÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng", USERS},
            {"Vá»‹ trÃ­ ká»‡", SHELF}
        };
    } else if (role == "admin") {
        menu = {
            {"Tá»•ng quan", DASHBOARD},
            {"Quáº£n lÃ½ sÃ¡ch", BOOKS},
            {"Phiáº¿u mÆ°á»£n", BORROWS},
            {"YÃªu cáº§u mÆ°á»£n", REQUESTS},
            {"Lá»c phiáº¿u mÆ°á»£n", BORROW_FILTER},
            {"Ca trá»±c cá»§a tÃ´i", SHIFTS},
            {"LÆ°Æ¡ng cá»§a tÃ´i", SALARY},
            {"Vá»‹ trÃ­ ká»‡", SHELF}
        };
    } else {
        menu = {
            {"Tá»•ng quan", DASHBOARD},
            {"TÃ¬m kiáº¿m sÃ¡ch", USER_SEARCH},
            {"ÄÄƒng kÃ½ mÆ°á»£n", USER_REQUEST},
            {"Vá»‹ trÃ­ ká»‡ sÃ¡ch", SHELF},
            {"SÃ¡ch Ä‘ang mÆ°á»£n", USER_ACTIVE_BORROWS},
            {"Lá»‹ch sá»­ mÆ°á»£n", USER_HISTORY}
        };
    }

    Vector2 mouse = ui_layout::mouse_position();
    for (int i = 0; i < (int)menu.size(); ++i) {
        Rectangle item = {18, (float)(150 + i * 62), 244, 48};
        bool hovered = !input_locked && CheckCollisionPointRec(mouse, item);
        bool selected = current_page == menu[i].destination;

        if (selected) {
            DrawRectangleRounded(item, 0.12f, 6, BLUE);
        } else if (hovered) {
            DrawRectangleRounded(item, 0.12f, 6, {215, 225, 240, 255});
        }

        DrawText(menu[i].label, 32, 164 + i * 62, 20,
                 selected ? WHITE : DARKGRAY);

        if (hovered && IsMouseButtonPressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT)) {
            open_root_page(menu[i].destination);
        }
    }

    if (draw_button({28, 665, 224, 50}, "ÄÄ‚NG XUáº¤T", RED)) {
        ask_confirm("XÃ¡c nháº­n Ä‘Äƒng xuáº¥t",
                    "Báº¡n cÃ³ cháº¯c cháº¯n muá»‘n Ä‘Äƒng xuáº¥t\nkhá»i há»‡ thá»‘ng khÃ´ng?",
                    "ÄÄƒng xuáº¥t", RED,
                    [this]() { logout_requested = true; });
    }
}
