}

void library_gui::draw_books(const std::string& role, bool user_search) {
    DrawText(user_search ? "TÃ¬m kiáº¿m sÃ¡ch" : "Quáº£n lÃ½ sÃ¡ch",
             CONTENT_X + 25, 120, 30, DARKBLUE);
    draw_back_button();

    draw_input({CONTENT_X + 20, 185, 340, 44},
               "MÃ£ sÃ¡ch, tÃªn sÃ¡ch hoáº·c tÃªn tÃ¡c giáº£",
               field_a, sizeof(field_a));
    if (draw_button({CONTENT_X + 375, 185, 95, 44}, "TÃ¬m")) {
        list_page = 0;
        selected_book_id.clear();
    }
    if (draw_button({CONTENT_X + 485, 185, 110, 44}, "Táº¥t cáº£", GRAY)) {
        field_a[0] = '\0';
        list_page = 0;
        selected_book_id.clear();
    }

    if (!user_search) {
        if (draw_button({CONTENT_X + 615, 185, 145, 44}, "ThÃªm sÃ¡ch")) {
            open_page(BOOK_ADD);
            return;
        }
        if (draw_button({CONTENT_X + 515, 245, 140, 44}, "Sá»­a sÃ¡ch", ORANGE)) {
            open_page(BOOK_EDIT);
            return;
        }
        if (draw_button({CONTENT_X + 670, 245, 140, 44}, "XÃ³a sÃ¡ch", RED)) {
            open_page(BOOK_DELETE);
            return;
        }
    }

    linked_list<book> books = controller.search_books(field_a);
    int total = books.get_size();
    int page_count = total == 0 ? 1 : (total + ROWS_PER_PAGE - 1) / ROWS_PER_PAGE;
    if (list_page >= page_count) list_page = page_count - 1;

    DrawText("Káº¿t quáº£ tÃ¬m kiáº¿m", 325, 250, 18, DARKGRAY);
    const char* headers[] = {"MÃ£", "TÃªn sÃ¡ch", "TÃ¡c giáº£", "Thá»ƒ loáº¡i"};
    const int columns[] = {330, 405, 675, 865};
    draw_table_header(headers, columns, 4, 278);

    node<book>* current = books.get_head();
    int skip = list_page * ROWS_PER_PAGE;
    while (current != nullptr && skip-- > 0) current = current->get_next();

    int row = 0;
    while (current != nullptr && row < 4) {
        book item = current->get_data();
        int y = 318 + row * 34;
        Rectangle row_box = {CONTENT_X + 15.0f, (float)y,
                             CONTENT_W - 30.0f, 32.0f};
        bool selected = item.get_id() == selected_book_id;
        DrawRectangleRec(row_box, selected ? Color{205, 225, 250, 255}
                                           : (row % 2 == 0 ? Color{245, 248, 252, 255}
                                                           : Color{232, 239, 248, 255}));
        DrawText(item.get_id().c_str(), 330, y + 8, 15, DARKGRAY);
        DrawText(item.get_title().c_str(), 405, y + 8, 15, DARKGRAY);
        DrawText(item.get_author().c_str(), 675, y + 8, 15, DARKGRAY);
        DrawText(item.get_category().c_str(), 865, y + 8, 15, DARKGRAY);
        if (CheckCollisionPointRec(GetMousePosition(), row_box) &&
            IsMouseButtonPressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT)) {
            selected_book_id = item.get_id();
        }
        current = current->get_next();
        ++row;
    }

    if (total == 0) {
        DrawText("KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y sÃ¡ch phÃ¹ há»£p.", 330, 325, 18, RED);
    }

    if (draw_button({CONTENT_X + 230, 458, 90, 34}, "TrÆ°á»›c", GRAY) &&
        list_page > 0) --list_page;
    if (draw_button({CONTENT_X + 550, 458, 90, 34}, "Sau", GRAY) &&
        list_page + 1 < page_count) ++list_page;
    draw_paging_hint(list_page, total, 466);

    DrawText("SÃCH Gá»¢I Ã", 325, 505, 19, DARKGREEN);
    if (selected_book_id.empty()) {
        DrawText("Chá»n má»™t cuá»‘n sÃ¡ch trong káº¿t quáº£ Ä‘á»ƒ xem gá»£i Ã½.",
                 330, 540, 17, GRAY);
        return;
    }

    node<book>* selected_node = controller.get_book_by_id(selected_book_id);
    if (selected_node == nullptr) {
        selected_book_id.clear();
        DrawText("KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y sÃ¡ch Ä‘Ã£ chá»n.", 330, 540, 17, RED);
        return;
    }

    book source = selected_node->get_data();
    DrawText(TextFormat("Dá»±a trÃªn: %s", source.get_title().c_str()),
             470, 505, 16, DARKGRAY);
    linked_list<book> suggestions = controller.get_recommended_books(
        source.get_author(), source.get_category(), source.get_id());

    if (suggestions.get_size() == 0) {
        DrawText("ChÆ°a cÃ³ sÃ¡ch cÃ¹ng tÃ¡c giáº£ hoáº·c thá»ƒ loáº¡i.", 330, 540, 17, GRAY);
        return;
    }

    node<book>* suggest = suggestions.get_head();
    int suggestion_row = 0;
    while (suggest != nullptr && suggestion_row < 4) {
        book item = suggest->get_data();
        int y = 535 + suggestion_row * 30;
        DrawRectangle(CONTENT_X + 15, y, CONTENT_W - 30, 28,
                      suggestion_row % 2 == 0 ? Color{239, 248, 241, 255}
                                              : Color{229, 242, 233, 255});
        DrawText(item.get_title().c_str(), 330, y + 7, 15, DARKGRAY);
        bool same_author = item.get_author() == source.get_author();
        bool same_category = item.get_category() == source.get_category();
        std::string reason = same_author && same_category
                                 ? "CÃ¹ng tÃ¡c giáº£ + thá»ƒ loáº¡i"
                                 : same_author ? "CÃ¹ng tÃ¡c giáº£"
                                               : "CÃ¹ng thá»ƒ loáº¡i";
        DrawText(reason.c_str(), 865, y + 7, 15, DARKGREEN);
        suggest = suggest->get_next();
        ++suggestion_row;
    }
}

void library_gui::draw_book_form(bool editing) {
    DrawText(editing ? "Sá»­a thÃ´ng tin sÃ¡ch" : "ThÃªm sÃ¡ch má»›i",
             CONTENT_X + 25, 120, 30, DARKBLUE);
    draw_back_button();

    if (!editing) {
        draw_input({340, 195, 360, 44}, "MÃ£ sÃ¡ch", field_a, sizeof(field_a));
        draw_input({740, 195, 400, 44}, "TÃªn sÃ¡ch", field_b, sizeof(field_b));
        draw_input({340, 280, 360, 44}, "TÃ¡c giáº£", field_c, sizeof(field_c));
        draw_input({740, 280, 400, 44}, "Thá»ƒ loáº¡i", field_d, sizeof(field_d));
        draw_input({340, 365, 360, 44}, "Sá»‘ lÆ°á»£ng (0 - 10000)", field_e, sizeof(field_e));
        draw_input({740, 365, 400, 44}, "NÄƒm xuáº¥t báº£n", field_f, sizeof(field_f));

        if (draw_button({530, 470, 220, 48}, "LÆ°u sÃ¡ch")) {
            int quantity = 0;
            int year = 0;
            if (is_blank(field_a) || is_blank(field_b) || is_blank(field_c) ||
                is_blank(field_d) ||
                !read_integer(field_e, quantity, 0, 10000) ||
                !read_integer(field_f, year, 1000, 9999)) {
                set_message("Nháº­p Ä‘áº§y Ä‘á»§ thÃ´ng tin vÃ  sá»‘ há»£p lá»‡.", true);
            } else if (controller.get_book_by_id(field_a) != nullptr) {
                set_message("KhÃ´ng thÃªm Ä‘Æ°á»£c: mÃ£ sÃ¡ch Ä‘Ã£ tá»“n táº¡i.", true);
            } else {
                std::string id = field_a;
                std::string title = field_b;
                std::string author = field_c;
                std::string category = field_d;
                ask_confirm("XÃ¡c nháº­n thÃªm sÃ¡ch",
                            "ThÃªm sÃ¡ch má»›i vÃ o thÆ° viá»‡n?\n"
                            "MÃ£ sÃ¡ch: " + id + "\n"
                            "TÃªn sÃ¡ch: " + title + "\n"
                            "Sá»‘ lÆ°á»£ng: " + std::to_string(quantity),
                            "ThÃªm sÃ¡ch", BLUE,
                            [this, id, title, author, category, quantity, year]() {
                    if (controller.add_new_book(id, title, author, category,
                                                quantity, year)) {
                        set_message("ÄÃ£ thÃªm sÃ¡ch má»›i.");
                        clear_fields();
                    } else {
                        set_message("KhÃ´ng thÃªm Ä‘Æ°á»£c: mÃ£ sÃ¡ch Ä‘Ã£ tá»“n táº¡i.", true);
                    }
                });
            }
        }
        return;
    }

    draw_input({340, 195, 360, 44}, "MÃ£ sÃ¡ch cÅ©", field_a, sizeof(field_a));
    draw_input({740, 195, 400, 44}, "MÃ£ sÃ¡ch má»›i", field_b, sizeof(field_b));
    draw_input({340, 280, 360, 44}, "TÃªn sÃ¡ch", field_c, sizeof(field_c));
    draw_input({740, 280, 400, 44}, "TÃ¡c giáº£", field_d, sizeof(field_d));
    draw_input({340, 365, 360, 44}, "Thá»ƒ loáº¡i", field_e, sizeof(field_e));
    draw_input({740, 365, 400, 44}, "Sá»‘ lÆ°á»£ng (0 - 10000)", field_f, sizeof(field_f));
    draw_input({340, 450, 360, 44}, "NÄƒm xuáº¥t báº£n", field_g, sizeof(field_g));

    if (draw_button({530, 545, 220, 48}, "Cáº­p nháº­t sÃ¡ch", ORANGE)) {
        int quantity = 0;
        int year = 0;
        if (is_blank(field_a) || is_blank(field_b) || is_blank(field_c) ||
            is_blank(field_d) || is_blank(field_e) ||
            !read_integer(field_f, quantity, 0, 10000) ||
            !read_integer(field_g, year, 1000, 9999)) {
            set_message("Nháº­p Ä‘áº§y Ä‘á»§ thÃ´ng tin vÃ  sá»‘ há»£p lá»‡.", true);
        } else if (controller.get_book_by_id(field_a) == nullptr) {
            set_message("KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y mÃ£ sÃ¡ch cÅ©.", true);
        } else {
            std::string old_id = field_a;
            std::string new_id = field_b;
            std::string title = field_c;
            std::string author = field_d;
            std::string category = field_e;
            ask_confirm("XÃ¡c nháº­n cáº­p nháº­t sÃ¡ch",
                        "Cáº­p nháº­t thÃ´ng tin sÃ¡ch " + old_id + "?\n"
                        "MÃ£ sÃ¡ch má»›i: " + new_id + "\n"
                        "TÃªn sÃ¡ch: " + title + "\n"
                        "Sá»‘ lÆ°á»£ng: " + std::to_string(quantity),
                        "Cáº­p nháº­t", ORANGE,
                        [this, old_id, new_id, title, author, category, quantity, year]() {
                book replacement(new_id, title, author, category, quantity, year);
                if (controller.update_book_info(old_id, replacement)) {
                    set_message("ÄÃ£ cáº­p nháº­t thÃ´ng tin sÃ¡ch.");
                    clear_fields();
                } else {
                    set_message("Cáº­p nháº­t tháº¥t báº¡i: kiá»ƒm tra mÃ£ sÃ¡ch cÅ© vÃ  mÃ£ sÃ¡ch má»›i.", true);
                }
            });
        }
    }
}

void library_gui::draw_book_delete() {
    DrawText("XÃ³a sÃ¡ch", CONTENT_X + 25, 120, 30, DARKBLUE);
    draw_back_button();
    draw_input({360, 220, 420, 46}, "MÃ£ sÃ¡ch cáº§n xÃ³a", field_a, sizeof(field_a));

    if (draw_button({460, 315, 200, 48}, "XÃ³a sÃ¡ch", RED)) {
        if (is_blank(field_a)) {
            set_message("Nháº­p mÃ£ sÃ¡ch cáº§n xÃ³a.", true);
            return;
        }
        node<book>* found = controller.get_book_by_id(field_a);
        if (found == nullptr) {
            set_message("KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y mÃ£ sÃ¡ch.", true);
            return;
        }

        std::string id = field_a;
        std::string title = found->get_data().get_title();
        ask_confirm("XÃ¡c nháº­n xÃ³a sÃ¡ch",
                    "Báº¡n cÃ³ cháº¯c cháº¯n muá»‘n xÃ³a sÃ¡ch nÃ y?\n"
                    "MÃ£ sÃ¡ch: " + id + "\n"
                    "TÃªn sÃ¡ch: " + title,
                    "XÃ³a sÃ¡ch", RED,
                    [this, id]() {
            if (controller.delete_book(id)) {
                set_message("ÄÃ£ xÃ³a sÃ¡ch.");
                clear_fields();
            } else {
                set_message("SÃ¡ch Ä‘ang cÃ³ ngÆ°á»i mÆ°á»£n, khÃ´ng thá»ƒ xÃ³a.", true);
            }
        });
    }
}

void library_gui::draw_borrows() {
    DrawText("Quáº£n lÃ½ phiáº¿u mÆ°á»£n", CONTENT_X + 25, 120, 30, DARKBLUE);
    draw_back_button();

    DrawText("Chá»n nghiá»‡p vá»¥:", 340, 195, 22, DARKGRAY);
    if (draw_button({340, 240, 210, 50}, "Láº­p phiáº¿u mÆ°á»£n")) {
        open_page(BORROW_CREATE);
        return;
    }
    if (draw_button({580, 240, 200, 50}, "XÃ¡c nháº­n tráº£", ORANGE)) {
        open_page(BORROW_RETURN);
        return;
    }
    if (draw_button({810, 240, 200, 50}, "XÃ³a phiáº¿u", RED)) {
        open_page(BORROW_DELETE);
        return;
    }

    DrawText("Má»—i phiáº¿u cáº§n cÃ³ mÃ£ phiáº¿u, mÃ£ sÃ¡ch, mÃ£ Ä‘á»™c giáº£,",
             340, 345, 21, DARKGRAY);
    DrawText("ngÃ y mÆ°á»£n vÃ  thá»i háº¡n 7, 14 hoáº·c 30 ngÃ y.",
             340, 380, 21, DARKGRAY);
}

void library_gui::draw_borrow_create() {
    DrawText("Láº­p phiáº¿u mÆ°á»£n sÃ¡ch", CONTENT_X + 25, 120, 30, DARKBLUE);
    draw_back_button();

    draw_input({340, 195, 360, 44}, "MÃ£ phiáº¿u", field_a, sizeof(field_a));
    draw_input({740, 195, 360, 44}, "MÃ£ sÃ¡ch", field_b, sizeof(field_b));
    draw_input({340, 280, 360, 44}, "MÃ£ Ä‘á»™c giáº£", field_c, sizeof(field_c));
    draw_input({740, 280, 360, 44}, "NgÃ y mÆ°á»£n (DD/MM/YYYY)", field_d, sizeof(field_d));
    draw_input({340, 365, 360, 44}, "Thá»i háº¡n: 7, 14 hoáº·c 30 ngÃ y",
               field_e, sizeof(field_e));

    if (!draw_button({530, 465, 210, 48}, "Táº¡o phiáº¿u")) return;

    date borrow_date;
    int duration = 0;
    if (is_blank(field_a) || is_blank(field_b) || is_blank(field_c) ||
        !read_date(field_d, borrow_date) ||
        !read_integer(field_e, duration, 1, 30) ||
        (duration != 7 && duration != 14 && duration != 30)) {
        set_message("Kiá»ƒm tra thÃ´ng tin, ngÃ y vÃ  thá»i háº¡n mÆ°á»£n.", true);
        return;
    }

    std::string borrow_id = field_a;
    std::string book_id = field_b;
    std::string user_id = field_c;

    if (controller.get_user_by_id(user_id) == nullptr) {
        set_message("KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y mÃ£ Ä‘á»™c giáº£.", true);
        return;
    }
    if (controller.has_overdue_borrows(user_id)) {
        set_message("Äá»™c giáº£ cÃ²n sÃ¡ch quÃ¡ háº¡n chÆ°a tráº£, khÃ´ng thá»ƒ mÆ°á»£n.", true);
        return;
    }
    node<book>* book_node = controller.get_book_by_id(book_id);
    if (book_node == nullptr) {
        set_message("KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y mÃ£ sÃ¡ch.", true);
        return;
    }

    std::string title = book_node->get_data().get_title();
    date due_date = borrow_date.add_days(duration);
    std::string due_text = format_date(due_date);

    ask_confirm("XÃ¡c nháº­n láº­p phiáº¿u mÆ°á»£n",
                "Táº¡o phiáº¿u mÆ°á»£n vá»›i thÃ´ng tin sau?\n"
                "MÃ£ phiáº¿u: " + borrow_id + "\n"
                "SÃ¡ch: " + book_id + " - " + title + "\n"
                "Äá»™c giáº£: " + user_id + "\n"
                "Háº¡n tráº£: " + due_text,
                "Táº¡o phiáº¿u", BLUE,
                [this, borrow_id, book_id, user_id, borrow_date, due_date, due_text]() {
        if (controller.borrow_book(borrow_id, book_id, user_id,
                                   borrow_date, due_date)) {
            set_message("ÄÃ£ táº¡o phiáº¿u. Háº¡n tráº£: " + due_text);
            clear_fields();
        } else {
            set_message("Táº¡o phiáº¿u tháº¥t báº¡i. Kiá»ƒm tra mÃ£ phiáº¿u, "
                        "sá»‘ lÆ°á»£ng sÃ¡ch hoáº·c thÃ´ng tin mÆ°á»£n.", true);
        }
    });
}

void library_gui::draw_borrow_return(bool deleting) {
    DrawText(deleting ? "XÃ³a phiáº¿u mÆ°á»£n" : "XÃ¡c nháº­n tráº£ sÃ¡ch",
             CONTENT_X + 25, 120, 30, DARKBLUE);
    draw_back_button();

    draw_input({360, 220, 420, 46}, "MÃ£ phiáº¿u mÆ°á»£n", field_a, sizeof(field_a));
    const char* label = deleting ? "XÃ³a phiáº¿u" : "XÃ¡c nháº­n tráº£";
    Color color = deleting ? RED : ORANGE;

    if (!draw_button({460, 315, 200, 48}, label, color)) return;

    if (is_blank(field_a)) {
        set_message("Nháº­p mÃ£ phiáº¿u mÆ°á»£n.", true);
        return;
    }

    std::string id = field_a;
    if (deleting) {
        ask_confirm("XÃ¡c nháº­n xÃ³a phiáº¿u mÆ°á»£n",
                    "Báº¡n cÃ³ cháº¯c cháº¯n muá»‘n xÃ³a phiáº¿u mÆ°á»£n\n" + id + " khÃ´ng?",
                    "XÃ³a phiáº¿u", RED,
                    [this, id]() {
            if (controller.delete_borrow(id)) {
                set_message("ÄÃ£ xÃ³a phiáº¿u mÆ°á»£n.");
                clear_fields();
            } else {
                set_message("KhÃ´ng xÃ³a Ä‘Æ°á»£c. Phiáº¿u pháº£i tá»“n táº¡i vÃ  sÃ¡ch Ä‘Ã£ tráº£.", true);
            }
        });
    } else {
        ask_confirm("XÃ¡c nháº­n tráº£ sÃ¡ch",
                    "XÃ¡c nháº­n Ä‘á»™c giáº£ Ä‘Ã£ tráº£ sÃ¡ch\ncho phiáº¿u mÆ°á»£n " + id + "?",
                    "XÃ¡c nháº­n tráº£", ORANGE,
                    [this, id]() {
            if (controller.return_book(id)) {
                set_message("ÄÃ£ xÃ¡c nháº­n tráº£ sÃ¡ch.");
                clear_fields();
            } else {
                set_message("KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y phiáº¿u hoáº·c sÃ¡ch Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c tráº£.", true);
            }
        });
    }
}

void library_gui::draw_requests() {
    DrawText("Duyá»‡t yÃªu cáº§u mÆ°á»£n sÃ¡ch", CONTENT_X + 25, 120, 30, DARKBLUE);
    draw_back_button();

    if (!controller.has_pending_requests()) {
        DrawText("KhÃ´ng cÃ³ yÃªu cáº§u nÃ o trong hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i.", 340, 235, 22, DARKGRAY);
        return;
    }

    borrow request = controller.peek_next_request();
    DrawText(TextFormat("Sá»‘ yÃªu cáº§u Ä‘ang chá»: %d",
                        controller.get_pending_request_count()),
             340, 190, 23, DARKBLUE);
    DrawText(TextFormat("MÃ£ phiáº¿u: %s", request.get_id().c_str()),
             360, 250, 21, DARKGRAY);
    DrawText(TextFormat("MÃ£ sÃ¡ch: %s", request.get_book_id().c_str()),
             360, 290, 21, DARKGRAY);
    DrawText(TextFormat("MÃ£ Ä‘á»™c giáº£: %s", request.get_user_id().c_str()),
             360, 330, 21, DARKGRAY);
    DrawText(TextFormat("NgÃ y mÆ°á»£n: %d/%d/%d",
                        request.get_borrow_date().get_day(),
                        request.get_borrow_date().get_month(),
                        request.get_borrow_date().get_year()),
             360, 370, 21, DARKGRAY);
    DrawText(TextFormat("Háº¡n tráº£: %d/%d/%d",
                        request.get_due_date().get_day(),
                        request.get_due_date().get_month(),
                        request.get_due_date().get_year()),
             360, 410, 21, DARKGRAY);

    std::string summary = "MÃ£ phiáº¿u: " + request.get_id() + "\n"
                          "MÃ£ sÃ¡ch: " + request.get_book_id() + "\n"
                          "MÃ£ Ä‘á»™c giáº£: " + request.get_user_id();

    if (draw_button({360, 485, 195, 48}, "Duyá»‡t", DARKGREEN)) {
        ask_confirm("XÃ¡c nháº­n duyá»‡t yÃªu cáº§u",
                    "Duyá»‡t yÃªu cáº§u mÆ°á»£n sÃ¡ch nÃ y?\n" + summary,
                    "Duyá»‡t", DARKGREEN,
                    [this]() {
            if (controller.approve_next_request()) {
                set_message("ÄÃ£ duyá»‡t yÃªu cáº§u vÃ  táº¡o phiáº¿u mÆ°á»£n.");
            } else {
                set_message("Duyá»‡t tháº¥t báº¡i: háº¿t sÃ¡ch hoáº·c trÃ¹ng mÃ£ phiáº¿u.", true);
            }
        });
        return;
    }
    if (draw_button({585, 485, 195, 48}, "Tá»« chá»‘i", RED)) {
        ask_confirm("XÃ¡c nháº­n tá»« chá»‘i yÃªu cáº§u",
                    "Tá»« chá»‘i yÃªu cáº§u mÆ°á»£n sÃ¡ch nÃ y?\n" + summary,
                    "Tá»« chá»‘i", RED,
                    [this]() {
            controller.reject_next_request();
            set_message("ÄÃ£ tá»« chá»‘i yÃªu cáº§u Ä‘áº§u hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i.");
        });
    }
}

void library_gui::draw_borrow_filter() {
    DrawText("Lá»c phiáº¿u mÆ°á»£n theo ngÃ y", CONTENT_X + 25, 120, 30, DARKBLUE);
    draw_back_button();

    draw_input({330, 185, 310, 44}, "Tá»« ngÃ y (DD/MM/YYYY)", field_a, sizeof(field_a));
    draw_input({670, 185, 310, 44}, "Äáº¿n ngÃ y (DD/MM/YYYY)", field_b, sizeof(field_b));
    if (draw_button({1010, 185, 110, 44}, "Lá»c")) list_page = 0;

    date start_date;
    date end_date;
    if (is_blank(field_a) || is_blank(field_b)) {
        DrawText("Nháº­p khoáº£ng ngÃ y Ä‘á»ƒ hiá»ƒn thá»‹ danh sÃ¡ch phiáº¿u mÆ°á»£n.",
                 330, 275, 20, DARKGRAY);
        return;
    }
    if (!read_date(field_a, start_date) || !read_date(field_b, end_date) ||
        end_date < start_date) {
        DrawText("Khoáº£ng ngÃ y khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡.", 330, 275, 20, RED);
        return;
    }

    linked_list<borrow> records =
        controller.filter_borrows_by_date(start_date, end_date);
    int total = records.get_size();
    int page_count = total == 0 ? 1 : (total + ROWS_PER_PAGE - 1) / ROWS_PER_PAGE;
    if (list_page >= page_count) list_page = page_count - 1;

    const char* headers[] = {"MÃ£ phiáº¿u", "MÃ£ sÃ¡ch", "MÃ£ Ä‘á»™c giáº£", "NgÃ y mÆ°á»£n", "Háº¡n tráº£", "Tráº¡ng thÃ¡i"};
    const int columns[] = {330, 460, 595, 740, 885, 1030};
    draw_table_header(headers, columns, 6, 250);

    node<borrow>* current = records.get_head();
    int skip = list_page * ROWS_PER_PAGE;
    while (current != nullptr && skip-- > 0) current = current->get_next();
    int row = 0;
    while (current != nullptr && row < ROWS_PER_PAGE) {
        borrow item = current->get_data();
        int y = 290 + row * 44;
        DrawRectangle(CONTENT_X + 15, y, CONTENT_W - 30, 42,
                      row % 2 == 0 ? Color{245, 248, 252, 255}
                                   : Color{232, 239, 248, 255});
        DrawText(item.get_id().c_str(), 330, y + 11, 17, DARKGRAY);
        DrawText(item.get_book_id().c_str(), 460, y + 11, 17, DARKGRAY);
        DrawText(item.get_user_id().c_str(), 595, y + 11, 17, DARKGRAY);
        DrawText(TextFormat("%d/%d/%d", item.get_borrow_date().get_day(),
                            item.get_borrow_date().get_month(),
                            item.get_borrow_date().get_year()),
                 740, y + 11, 17, DARKGRAY);
        DrawText(TextFormat("%d/%d/%d", item.get_due_date().get_day(),
                            item.get_due_date().get_month(),
                            item.get_due_date().get_year()),
                 885, y + 11, 17, DARKGRAY);
        DrawText(display_status(item.get_status()).c_str(), 1030, y + 11, 17, DARKGRAY);
        current = current->get_next();
        ++row;
    }
    if (total == 0) DrawText("KhÃ´ng cÃ³ phiáº¿u mÆ°á»£n trong khoáº£ng ngÃ y nÃ y.",
                             330, 315, 20, RED);

    if (draw_button({CONTENT_X + 230, 595, 110, 40}, "TrÆ°á»›c", GRAY) &&
        list_page > 0) --list_page;
    if (draw_button({CONTENT_X + 550, 595, 110, 40}, "Sau", GRAY) &&
        list_page + 1 < page_count) ++list_page;
    draw_paging_hint(list_page, total, 605);
}

void library_gui::draw_shifts(const std::string& role) {
    DrawText(role == "manager" ? "Quáº£n lÃ½ ca trá»±c" : "Ca trá»±c cá»§a tÃ´i",
             CONTENT_X + 25, 120, 30, DARKBLUE);
    draw_back_button();

    if (draw_button({330, 175, 170, 44}, "ÄÄƒng kÃ½ ca")) {
        open_page(SHIFT_REGISTER);
        return;
    }
    if (draw_button({525, 175, 170, 44}, "RÃºt khá»i ca", ORANGE)) {
        open_page(SHIFT_CANCEL);
        return;
    }
    if (role == "manager" &&
        draw_button({720, 175, 170, 44}, "XÃ³a ca trá»±c", RED)) {
        open_page(SHIFT_DELETE);
        return;
    }

    linked_list<shift> shifts = role == "manager"
        ? controller.get_all_shifts()
        : controller.get_my_shifts(controller.get_current_user()->get_id());

    int total = shifts.get_size();
    int page_count = total == 0 ? 1 : (total + ROWS_PER_PAGE - 1) / ROWS_PER_PAGE;
    if (list_page >= page_count) list_page = page_count - 1;

    const char* headers[] = {"NgÃ y", "Ca", "Sá»‘ ngÆ°á»i", "Danh sÃ¡ch admin"};
    const int columns[] = {335, 535, 680, 840};
    draw_table_header(headers, columns, 4, 240);

    node<shift>* current = shifts.get_head();
    int skip = list_page * ROWS_PER_PAGE;
    while (current != nullptr && skip-- > 0) current = current->get_next();
    int row = 0;
    while (current != nullptr && row < ROWS_PER_PAGE) {
        shift item = current->get_data();
        date day = item.get_shift_date();
        int y = 280 + row * 44;
        DrawRectangle(CONTENT_X + 15, y, CONTENT_W - 30, 42,
                      row % 2 == 0 ? Color{245, 248, 252, 255}
                                   : Color{232, 239, 248, 255});
        DrawText(TextFormat("%d/%d/%d", day.get_day(), day.get_month(), day.get_year()),
                 335, y + 11, 17, DARKGRAY);
        DrawText(display_session(item.get_session()).c_str(), 535, y + 11, 17, DARKGRAY);
        DrawText(TextFormat("%d/4", item.get_current_count()),
                 680, y + 11, 17, DARKGRAY);
        DrawText(item.get_all_admins_string().c_str(), 840, y + 11, 17, DARKGRAY);
        current = current->get_next();
        ++row;
    }
    if (total == 0) {
        DrawText("ChÆ°a cÃ³ ca trá»±c phÃ¹ há»£p.", 335, 310, 20, DARKGRAY);
    }

    if (draw_button({CONTENT_X + 230, 595, 110, 40}, "TrÆ°á»›c", GRAY) &&
        list_page > 0) --list_page;
    if (draw_button({CONTENT_X + 550, 595, 110, 40}, "Sau", GRAY) &&
        list_page + 1 < page_count) ++list_page;
    draw_paging_hint(list_page, total, 605);
}

void library_gui::draw_shift_form(bool cancelling, bool deleting) {
    const char* title = deleting ? "XÃ³a ca trá»±c" :
                        cancelling ? "RÃºt khá»i ca trá»±c" : "ÄÄƒng kÃ½ ca trá»±c";
    DrawText(title, CONTENT_X + 25, 120, 30, DARKBLUE);
    draw_back_button();

    draw_input({340, 200, 360, 44}, "NgÃ y (DD/MM/YYYY)", field_a, sizeof(field_a));
    draw_input({740, 200, 360, 44}, "Ca: sÃ¡ng hoáº·c chiá»u", field_b, sizeof(field_b));

    bool manager = controller.get_current_role() == "manager";
    if (!deleting && manager) {
        draw_input({340, 290, 360, 44}, "MÃ£ admin/manager", field_c, sizeof(field_c));
    } else if (!deleting) {
        DrawText(TextFormat("NhÃ¢n viÃªn: %s",
                            controller.get_current_user()->get_id().c_str()),
                 340, 300, 21, DARKGRAY);
    }

    const char* action = deleting ? "XÃ³a ca" : cancelling ? "RÃºt khá»i ca" : "ÄÄƒng kÃ½";
    Color action_color = deleting ? RED : cancelling ? ORANGE : BLUE;
    if (!draw_button({530, 400, 210, 48}, action, action_color)) return;

    date shift_date;
    std::string session = normalize_session(field_b);
    if (!read_date(field_a, shift_date) || session.empty()) {
        set_message("NgÃ y hoáº·c ca trá»±c khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡.", true);
        return;
    }

    std::string date_text = format_date(shift_date);
    std::string session_text = display_session(session);

    if (deleting) {
        ask_confirm("XÃ¡c nháº­n xÃ³a ca trá»±c",
                    "XÃ³a toÃ n bá»™ ca trá»±c nÃ y?\n"
                    "NgÃ y: " + date_text + "\n"
                    "Ca: " + session_text,
                    "XÃ³a ca", RED,
                    [this, shift_date, session]() {
            if (controller.delete_shift(shift_date, session)) {
                set_message("ÄÃ£ xÃ³a toÃ n bá»™ ca trá»±c.");
                clear_fields();
            } else {
                set_message("KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y ca trá»±c.", true);
            }
        });
        return;
    }

    std::string person_id;
    if (manager) {
        person_id = field_c;
    } else {
        person_id = controller.get_current_user()->get_id();
    }
    if (is_blank(person_id.c_str())) {
        set_message("Nháº­p mÃ£ nhÃ¢n viÃªn.", true);
        return;
    }

    ask_confirm(cancelling ? "XÃ¡c nháº­n rÃºt khá»i ca" : "XÃ¡c nháº­n Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ ca",
                std::string(cancelling ? "RÃºt nhÃ¢n viÃªn khá»i ca trá»±c?\n"
                                       : "ÄÄƒng kÃ½ nhÃ¢n viÃªn vÃ o ca trá»±c?\n") +
                "NhÃ¢n viÃªn: " + person_id + "\n"
                "NgÃ y: " + date_text + "\n"
                "Ca: " + session_text,
                cancelling ? "RÃºt khá»i ca" : "ÄÄƒng kÃ½",
                cancelling ? ORANGE : BLUE,
                [this, cancelling, shift_date, session, person_id]() {
        bool success = cancelling
            ? controller.cancel_shift(shift_date, session, person_id)
            : controller.register_shift(shift_date, session, person_id);
        if (success) {
            set_message(cancelling ? "ÄÃ£ rÃºt khá»i ca trá»±c." : "ÄÃ£ Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ ca trá»±c.");
            clear_fields();
        } else {
            set_message(cancelling
                        ? "KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y nhÃ¢n viÃªn trong ca trá»±c."
                        : "KhÃ´ng Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ Ä‘Æ°á»£c: ca Ä‘áº§y, trÃ¹ng ca hoáº·c sai vai trÃ².",
                        true);
        }
    });
}

void library_gui::draw_salary(const std::string& role) {
    DrawText(role == "manager" ? "TÃ­nh lÆ°Æ¡ng ca trá»±c" : "LÆ°Æ¡ng ca trá»±c cá»§a tÃ´i",
             CONTENT_X + 25, 120, 30, DARKBLUE);
    draw_back_button();

    if (role == "manager") {
        draw_input({340, 205, 300, 44}, "MÃ£ admin/manager", field_a, sizeof(field_a));
        draw_input({675, 205, 210, 44}, "ThÃ¡ng, 0 = táº¥t cáº£", field_b, sizeof(field_b));
        draw_input({920, 205, 210, 44}, "NÄƒm, 0 = táº¥t cáº£", field_c, sizeof(field_c));
    } else {
        draw_input({380, 205, 280, 44}, "ThÃ¡ng, 0 = táº¥t cáº£", field_a, sizeof(field_a));
        draw_input({710, 205, 280, 44}, "NÄƒm, 0 = táº¥t cáº£", field_b, sizeof(field_b));
    }

    if (draw_button({530, 320, 210, 48}, "TÃ­nh lÆ°Æ¡ng", DARKGREEN)) {
        std::string id = role == "manager" ? std::string(field_a)
                                           : controller.get_current_user()->get_id();
        const char* month_text = role == "manager" ? field_b : field_a;
        const char* year_text = role == "manager" ? field_c : field_b;
        int month = 0;
        int year = 0;

        bool month_ok = is_blank(month_text) || read_integer(month_text, month, 0, 12);
        bool year_ok = is_blank(year_text) || read_integer(year_text, year, 0, 2026);
        if (is_blank(id.c_str()) || !month_ok || !year_ok) {
            set_message("Nháº­p mÃ£, thÃ¡ng vÃ  nÄƒm há»£p lá»‡.", true);
        } else {
            long long total = controller.get_admin_salary(id, month, year);
            set_message(TextFormat("Tá»•ng lÆ°Æ¡ng: %lld VNÄ (%lld buá»•i)",
                                   total, total / 200000));
        }
    }
}

void library_gui::draw_users() {
    DrawText("Quáº£n lÃ½ ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng", CONTENT_X + 25, 120, 30, DARKBLUE);
    draw_back_button();

    if (draw_button({340, 185, 175, 48}, "ThÃªm tÃ i khoáº£n")) {
        open_page(USER_ADD);
        return;
    }
    if (draw_button({545, 185, 175, 48}, "Sá»­a tÃ i khoáº£n", ORANGE)) {
        open_page(USER_EDIT);
        return;
    }
    if (draw_button({750, 185, 175, 48}, "XÃ³a tÃ i khoáº£n", RED)) {
        open_page(USER_DELETE);
        return;
    }

    draw_input({340, 295, 380, 44}, "MÃ£ tÃ i khoáº£n cáº§n tra cá»©u",
               field_a, sizeof(field_a));
    if (draw_button({750, 295, 150, 44}, "Tra cá»©u")) {
        if (is_blank(field_a)) {
            set_message("Nháº­p mÃ£ tÃ i khoáº£n.", true);
        } else {
            node<user>* found = controller.get_user_by_id(field_a);
            if (found == nullptr) {
                set_message("KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y tÃ i khoáº£n.", true);
            } else {
                user item = found->get_data();
                set_message("MÃ£: " + item.get_id() + " | TÃªn: " +
                            item.get_name() + " | Vai trÃ²: " +
                            display_role(item.get_role()));
            }
        }
    }
}

void library_gui::draw_user_form(bool editing) {
    DrawText(editing ? "Cáº­p nháº­t tÃ i khoáº£n" : "ThÃªm tÃ i khoáº£n má»›i",
             CONTENT_X + 25, 120, 30, DARKBLUE);
    draw_back_button();

    if (editing) {
        draw_input({340, 185, 360, 44}, "MÃ£ tÃ i khoáº£n cÅ©", field_a, sizeof(field_a));
        draw_input({740, 185, 360, 44}, "MÃ£ tÃ i khoáº£n má»›i", field_b, sizeof(field_b));
        draw_input({340, 270, 360, 44}, "Há» vÃ  tÃªn", field_c, sizeof(field_c));
        draw_input({740, 270, 360, 44}, "Máº­t kháº©u", field_d, sizeof(field_d));
        draw_input({340, 355, 360, 44}, "Vai trÃ²: manager, admin, user",
                   field_e, sizeof(field_e));
    } else {
        draw_input({340, 195, 360, 44}, "MÃ£ tÃ i khoáº£n", field_a, sizeof(field_a));
        draw_input({740, 195, 360, 44}, "Há» vÃ  tÃªn", field_b, sizeof(field_b));
        draw_input({340, 280, 360, 44}, "Máº­t kháº©u", field_c, sizeof(field_c));
        draw_input({740, 280, 360, 44}, "Vai trÃ²: manager, admin, user",
                   field_d, sizeof(field_d));
    }

    if (!draw_button({530, 465, 220, 48},
                     editing ? "Cáº­p nháº­t" : "ThÃªm tÃ i khoáº£n")) {
        return;
    }

    std::string old_id = field_a;
    std::string id = editing ? field_b : field_a;
    std::string name = editing ? field_c : field_b;
    std::string password = editing ? field_d : field_c;
    std::string role = editing ? field_e : field_d;
    bool supported_role = role == "manager" || role == "admin" || role == "user";

    if (is_blank(old_id.c_str()) || is_blank(id.c_str()) || is_blank(name.c_str()) ||
        is_blank(password.c_str()) || !supported_role) {
        set_message("Nháº­p Ä‘áº§y Ä‘á»§ thÃ´ng tin vÃ  vai trÃ² há»£p lá»‡.", true);
        return;
    }

    std::string summary = "MÃ£ tÃ i khoáº£n: " + id + "\n"
                          "Há» vÃ  tÃªn: " + name + "\n"
                          "Vai trÃ²: " + display_role(role);

    if (editing) {
        ask_confirm("XÃ¡c nháº­n cáº­p nháº­t tÃ i khoáº£n",
                    "Cáº­p nháº­t tÃ i khoáº£n " + old_id + "?\n" + summary,
                    "Cáº­p nháº­t", ORANGE,
                    [this, old_id, id, name, password, role]() {
            if (controller.update_user_info(old_id, user(id, name, password, role))) {
                set_message("ÄÃ£ cáº­p nháº­t tÃ i khoáº£n.");
                clear_fields();
            } else {
                set_message("Cáº­p nháº­t tháº¥t báº¡i.", true);
            }
        });
    } else {
        ask_confirm("XÃ¡c nháº­n thÃªm tÃ i khoáº£n",
                    "ThÃªm tÃ i khoáº£n má»›i?\n" + summary,
                    "ThÃªm tÃ i khoáº£n", BLUE,
                    [this, id, name, password, role]() {
            if (controller.add_new_user(id, name, password, role)) {
                set_message("ÄÃ£ thÃªm tÃ i khoáº£n.");
                clear_fields();
            } else {
                set_message("MÃ£ tÃ i khoáº£n Ä‘Ã£ tá»“n táº¡i.", true);
            }
        });
    }
}

void library_gui::draw_user_delete() {
    DrawText("XÃ³a tÃ i khoáº£n", CONTENT_X + 25, 120, 30, DARKBLUE);
    draw_back_button();
    draw_input({360, 220, 420, 46}, "MÃ£ tÃ i khoáº£n cáº§n xÃ³a",
               field_a, sizeof(field_a));

    if (!draw_button({460, 315, 210, 48}, "XÃ³a tÃ i khoáº£n", RED)) return;

    if (is_blank(field_a)) {
        set_message("Nháº­p mÃ£ tÃ i khoáº£n cáº§n xÃ³a.", true);
        return;
    }
    node<user>* found = controller.get_user_by_id(field_a);
    if (found == nullptr) {
        set_message("KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y tÃ i khoáº£n.", true);
        return;
    }
    if (controller.has_active_borrows(field_a)) {
        set_message("KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ xÃ³a tÃ i khoáº£n Ä‘ang mÆ°á»£n sÃ¡ch "
                    "hoáº·c cÃ²n sÃ¡ch quÃ¡ háº¡n chÆ°a tráº£.", true);
        return;
    }

    std::string id = field_a;
    std::string name = found->get_data().get_name();
    ask_confirm("XÃ¡c nháº­n xÃ³a tÃ i khoáº£n",
                "Báº¡n cÃ³ cháº¯c cháº¯n muá»‘n xÃ³a tÃ i khoáº£n nÃ y?\n"
                "MÃ£ tÃ i khoáº£n: " + id + "\n"
                "Há» vÃ  tÃªn: " + name,
                "XÃ³a tÃ i khoáº£n", RED,
                [this, id]() {
        if (controller.delete_user(id)) {
            set_message("ÄÃ£ xÃ³a tÃ i khoáº£n.");
            clear_fields();
        } else {
            set_message("XÃ³a tÃ i khoáº£n tháº¥t báº¡i.", true);
        }
    });
}

                set_message("KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y sÃ¡ch.", true);

                            " | Thá»ƒ loáº¡i: " + item.get_category() +

void library_gui::draw_user_request() {
    DrawText("ÄÄƒng kÃ½ mÆ°á»£n sÃ¡ch", CONTENT_X + 25, 120, 30, DARKBLUE);
    draw_back_button();

    draw_input({340, 195, 360, 44}, "MÃ£ yÃªu cáº§u / mÃ£ phiáº¿u", field_a, sizeof(field_a));
    draw_input({740, 195, 360, 44}, "MÃ£ sÃ¡ch", field_b, sizeof(field_b));
    draw_input({340, 280, 360, 44}, "NgÃ y mÆ°á»£n (DD/MM/YYYY)", field_c, sizeof(field_c));
    draw_input({740, 280, 360, 44}, "Thá»i háº¡n: 7, 14 hoáº·c 30 ngÃ y",
               field_d, sizeof(field_d));

    if (!draw_button({530, 390, 210, 48}, "Gá»­i yÃªu cáº§u")) return;

    date borrow_date;
    int duration = 0;
    if (is_blank(field_a) || is_blank(field_b) ||
        !read_date(field_c, borrow_date) ||
        !read_integer(field_d, duration, 1, 30) ||
        (duration != 7 && duration != 14 && duration != 30)) {
        set_message("Kiá»ƒm tra thÃ´ng tin, ngÃ y vÃ  thá»i háº¡n mÆ°á»£n.", true);
        return;
    }

    user* current_user = controller.get_current_user();
    if (current_user == nullptr) {
        set_message("Vui lÃ²ng Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p láº¡i.", true);
        return;
    }
    std::string user_id = current_user->get_id();
    if (controller.has_overdue_borrows(user_id)) {
        set_message("Báº¡n cÃ²n sÃ¡ch quÃ¡ háº¡n chÆ°a tráº£, "
                    "khÃ´ng thá»ƒ Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ mÆ°á»£n sÃ¡ch.", true);
        return;
    }
    std::string request_id = field_a;
    std::string book_id = field_b;
    node<book>* book_node = controller.get_book_by_id(book_id);
    if (book_node == nullptr) {
        set_message("KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y mÃ£ sÃ¡ch.", true);
        return;
    }

    std::string title = book_node->get_data().get_title();
    date due_date = borrow_date.add_days(duration);

    ask_confirm("XÃ¡c nháº­n Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ mÆ°á»£n sÃ¡ch",
                "Gá»­i yÃªu cáº§u mÆ°á»£n sÃ¡ch nÃ y?\n"
                "SÃ¡ch: " + book_id + " - " + title + "\n"
                "NgÃ y mÆ°á»£n: " + format_date(borrow_date) + "\n"
                "Háº¡n tráº£: " + format_date(due_date),
                "Gá»­i yÃªu cáº§u", BLUE,
                [this, request_id, book_id, user_id, borrow_date, due_date]() {
        if (controller.register_book_request(request_id, book_id, user_id,
                                             borrow_date, due_date)) {
            set_message("ÄÃ£ Ä‘Æ°a yÃªu cáº§u vÃ o hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i.");
            clear_fields();
        } else {
            set_message("Gá»­i yÃªu cáº§u tháº¥t báº¡i. Kiá»ƒm tra mÃ£ yÃªu cáº§u, "
                        "sá»‘ lÆ°á»£ng sÃ¡ch hoáº·c lá»‹ch sá»­ mÆ°á»£n.", true);
        }
    });
}

void library_gui::draw_my_borrows(bool active_only) {
    DrawText(active_only ? "SÃ¡ch Ä‘ang mÆ°á»£n" : "Lá»‹ch sá»­ mÆ°á»£n sÃ¡ch",
             CONTENT_X + 25, 120, 30, DARKBLUE);
    draw_back_button();

    linked_list<borrow> records = controller.get_my_borrows(
        controller.get_current_user()->get_id(), active_only);
    int total = records.get_size();
    int page_count = total == 0 ? 1 : (total + ROWS_PER_PAGE - 1) / ROWS_PER_PAGE;
    if (list_page >= page_count) list_page = page_count - 1;

    const char* headers[] = {"MÃ£ phiáº¿u", "TÃªn sÃ¡ch", "NgÃ y mÆ°á»£n", "Háº¡n tráº£", "Tráº¡ng thÃ¡i"};
    const int columns[] = {330, 465, 765, 910, 1045};
    draw_table_header(headers, columns, 5, 185);

    node<borrow>* current = records.get_head();
    int skip = list_page * ROWS_PER_PAGE;
    while (current != nullptr && skip-- > 0) current = current->get_next();
    int row = 0;
    while (current != nullptr && row < ROWS_PER_PAGE) {
        borrow item = current->get_data();
        node<book>* book_node = controller.get_book_by_id(item.get_book_id());
        std::string title = book_node == nullptr ? item.get_book_id()
                                                 : book_node->get_data().get_title();
        int y = 225 + row * 46;
        DrawRectangle(CONTENT_X + 15, y, CONTENT_W - 30, 44,
                      row % 2 == 0 ? Color{245, 248, 252, 255}
                                   : Color{232, 239, 248, 255});
        DrawText(item.get_id().c_str(), 330, y + 12, 17, DARKGRAY);
        DrawText(title.c_str(), 465, y + 12, 17, DARKGRAY);
        DrawText(TextFormat("%d/%d/%d", item.get_borrow_date().get_day(),
                            item.get_borrow_date().get_month(),
                            item.get_borrow_date().get_year()),
                 765, y + 12, 17, DARKGRAY);
        DrawText(TextFormat("%d/%d/%d", item.get_due_date().get_day(),
                            item.get_due_date().get_month(),
                            item.get_due_date().get_year()),
                 910, y + 12, 17, DARKGRAY);
        DrawText(display_status(item.get_status()).c_str(), 1045, y + 12, 17, DARKGRAY);
        current = current->get_next();
        ++row;
    }

    if (total == 0) {
        DrawText(active_only ? "Báº¡n khÃ´ng cÃ³ sÃ¡ch Ä‘ang mÆ°á»£n."
                             : "Báº¡n chÆ°a cÃ³ lá»‹ch sá»­ mÆ°á»£n sÃ¡ch.",
                 330, 260, 20, DARKGRAY);
    }
    if (draw_button({CONTENT_X + 230, 595, 110, 40}, "TrÆ°á»›c", GRAY) &&
        list_page > 0) --list_page;
    if (draw_button({CONTENT_X + 550, 595, 110, 40}, "Sau", GRAY) &&
        list_page + 1 < page_count) ++list_page;
    draw_paging_hint(list_page, total, 605);
}

void library_gui::draw(const std::string& role) {
    BeginDrawing();
    ClearBackground(page_background);

    BeginMode2D(ui_layout::camera());

    // Khi hop thoai xac nhan dang mo, khoa moi thao tac o phia sau.
    input_locked = confirm_open;

    collect_text_input();
    draw_shell(role);
    DrawText(telex_enabled ? "Bo go Telex: BAT (F2 de tat)" : "Bo go Telex: TAT (F2 de bat)",
             CONTENT_X + 20, 84, 16, telex_enabled ? DARKGREEN : GRAY);
    draw_sidebar(role);

    switch (current_page) {
        case DASHBOARD:           draw_dashboard(role); break;
        case BOOKS:               draw_books(role, false); break;
        case USER_SEARCH:         draw_books(role, true); break;
        case BOOK_ADD:            draw_book_form(false); break;
        case BOOK_EDIT:           draw_book_form(true); break;
        case BOOK_DELETE:         draw_book_delete(); break;
        case BORROWS:             draw_borrows(); break;
        case BORROW_CREATE:       draw_borrow_create(); break;
        case BORROW_RETURN:       draw_borrow_return(false); break;
        case BORROW_DELETE:       draw_borrow_return(true); break;
        case REQUESTS:            draw_requests(); break;
        case BORROW_FILTER:       draw_borrow_filter(); break;
        case SHIFTS:              draw_shifts(role); break;
        case SHIFT_REGISTER:      draw_shift_form(false, false); break;
        case SHIFT_CANCEL:        draw_shift_form(true, false); break;
        case SHIFT_DELETE:        draw_shift_form(false, true); break;
        case SALARY:              draw_salary(role); break;
        case USERS:               draw_users(); break;
        case USER_ADD:            draw_user_form(false); break;
        case USER_EDIT:           draw_user_form(true); break;
        case USER_DELETE:         draw_user_delete(); break;
        case SHELF:               draw_shelf(); break;
        case USER_REQUEST:        draw_user_request(); break;
        case USER_ACTIVE_BORROWS: draw_my_borrows(true); break;
        case USER_HISTORY:        draw_my_borrows(false); break;
    }

    draw_message();
    draw_confirm_dialog();

    EndMode2D();
    EndDrawing();
}

=== FILE: .\GUI\login.cpp ===
#include "login.h"
#include "raylib.h"
#include <cstring>
#include "ui_font.h"
#include "ui_layout.h"

login_screen::login_screen(library_controller& c)
    : controller(c), focus_id(true), focus_password(false),
      show_password(false), logged_in(false) {
    id[0] = '\0';
    password[0] = '\0';
}

void login_screen::try_login() {
    if (id[0] == '\0' || password[0] == '\0') {
        message = "Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p Ä‘áº§y Ä‘á»§ thÃ´ng tin!";
        return;
    }

    if (controller.login(id, password)) {
        logged_in = true;
        role = controller.get_current_role();
        message = "ÄÄƒng nháº­p thÃ nh cÃ´ng!";
    } else {
        message = "Sai mÃ£ tÃ i khoáº£n hoáº·c máº­t kháº©u!";
    }
}

void login_screen::handle_input() {
    if (logged_in) {
        if (IsKeyPressed(KEY_ENTER) ||
            IsKeyPressed(KEY_ESCAPE)) {
            controller.logout();
            logged_in = false;
            role.clear();
            message.clear();
            id[0] = '\0';
            password[0] = '\0';
            focus_id = true;
            focus_password = false;
            show_password = false;
        }
        return;
    }

    if (IsKeyPressed(KEY_TAB)) {
        focus_id = !focus_id;
        focus_password = !focus_password;
    }

    if (IsKeyPressed(KEY_ENTER)) {
        try_login();
        return;
    }

    int key = GetCharPressed();

    while (key > 0) {
        if (key >= 32 && key <= 126) {
            if (focus_id) {
                int len = (int)strlen(id);
                if (len < (int)sizeof(id) - 1) {
                    id[len] = (char)key;
                    id[len + 1] = '\0';
                }
            } else if (focus_password) {
                int len = (int)strlen(password);
                if (len < (int)sizeof(password) - 1) {
                    password[len] = (char)key;
                    password[len + 1] = '\0';
                }
            }
        }
        key = GetCharPressed();
    }

    if (IsKeyPressed(KEY_BACKSPACE)) {
        if (focus_id) {
            int len = (int)strlen(id);
            if (len > 0) id[len - 1] = '\0';
        } else if (focus_password) {
            int len = (int)strlen(password);
            if (len > 0) password[len - 1] = '\0';
        }
    }
}

void login_screen::draw_input_box(
    int x, int y, int w, int h,
    const char* label, const char* value, bool focused) {

    DrawText(label, x, y - 30, 20, DARKGRAY);

    DrawRectangleRounded(
        {(float)x, (float)y, (float)w, (float)h},
        0.12f, 8, WHITE);

    DrawRectangleRoundedLines(
        {(float)x, (float)y, (float)w, (float)h},
        0.12f, 8, focused ? BLUE : LIGHTGRAY);

    DrawText(value, x + 16, y + 17, 22, DARKGRAY);
}

void login_screen::draw() {
    handle_input();

    BeginDrawing();
    ClearBackground({245, 247, 251, 255});

    BeginMode2D(ui_layout::camera());

    if (logged_in) {
        DrawText("ÄÄ‚NG NHáº¬P THÃ€NH CÃ”NG",
                 410, 240, 34, DARKGREEN);

        DrawText(TextFormat("Vai trÃ²: %s", role == "manager" ? "Quáº£n lÃ½" : role == "admin" ? "Quáº£n trá»‹ viÃªn" : "Äá»™c giáº£"),
                 490, 310, 28, DARKBLUE);

        DrawText("Nháº¥n ENTER hoáº·c ESC Ä‘á»ƒ Ä‘Äƒng xuáº¥t",
                 400, 400, 22, GRAY);
    } else {
        DrawRectangleRounded(
            {360, 95, 500, 555}, 0.06f, 12, WHITE);

        int title_w = MeasureText("QUáº¢N LÃ THÆ¯ VIá»†N", 32);
        DrawText("QUáº¢N LÃ THÆ¯ VIá»†N",
                 360 + (500 - title_w) / 2, 135, 32, DARKBLUE);

        int sub_w = MeasureText("ÄÄ‚NG NHáº¬P Há»† THá»NG", 21);
        DrawText("ÄÄ‚NG NHáº¬P Há»† THá»NG",
                 360 + (500 - sub_w) / 2, 185, 21, GRAY);

        draw_input_box(
            415, 270, 390, 56,
            "MÃ£ tÃ i khoáº£n", id, focus_id);

        std::string displayed_password;

        if (show_password) {
            displayed_password = password;
        } else {
            displayed_password.assign(strlen(password), '*');
        }

        draw_input_box(
            415, 375, 390, 56,
            "Máº­t kháº©u", displayed_password.c_str(),
            focus_password);

        Rectangle eye = {740, 380, 55, 46};

        DrawText(show_password ? "áº¨N" : "HIá»†N",
                 745, 393, 18, DARKBLUE);

        Rectangle button = {415, 465, 390, 54};

        DrawRectangleRounded(button, 0.15f, 8, BLUE);
        int btn_w = MeasureText("ÄÄ‚NG NHáº¬P", 22);
        DrawText("ÄÄ‚NG NHáº¬P", 415 + (390 - btn_w) / 2, 481, 22, WHITE);

        DrawText("TAB: chuyá»ƒn Ã´ | ENTER: Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p",
                 415, 540, 18, GRAY);

        if (!message.empty()) {
            Color color = message == "ÄÄƒng nháº­p thÃ nh cÃ´ng!"
                          ? DARKGREEN : RED;
            DrawText(message.c_str(), 415, 580, 19, color);
        }

        if (IsMouseButtonPressed(MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT)) {
            Vector2 mouse = ui_layout::mouse_position();

            if (CheckCollisionPointRec(mouse, eye)) {
                show_password = !show_password;
            } else if (CheckCollisionPointRec(mouse, button)) {
                try_login();
            } else if (CheckCollisionPointRec(
                           mouse, {415, 270, 390, 56})) {
                focus_id = true;
                focus_password = false;
            } else if (CheckCollisionPointRec(
                           mouse, {415, 375, 390, 56})) {
                focus_id = false;
                focus_password = true;
            }
        }
    }

    EndMode2D();
    EndDrawing();
}

bool login_screen::is_logged_in() const {
    return logged_in;
}

std::string login_screen::get_role() const {
    return role;
}

void login_screen::reset() {
    controller.logout();
    logged_in = false;
    role = "";
    message = "";
    id[0] = '\0';
    password[0] = '\0';
    show_password = false;
    focus_id = true;
    focus_password = false;
}

=== FILE: .\GUI\ui_font.h ===
#pragma once

#include "raylib.h"

// Khai bao font dung chung cho cac tep GUI.
extern Font app_font;

// Chinh he so nay (vi du 1.05f hoac 1.1f) neu muon phong to toan bo chu them nua
const float FONT_SCALE = 1.0f;

inline void DrawReadableText(const char* text, int x, int y,
                             int font_size, Color color) {
    DrawTextEx(app_font, text, {(float)x, (float)y},
               (float)font_size * FONT_SCALE, 1.0f, color);
}

inline int MeasureReadableText(const char* text, int font_size) {
    Vector2 size = MeasureTextEx(app_font, text, (float)font_size * FONT_SCALE, 1.0f);
    return (int)size.x;
}

// Sau khi include tep nay, cac lenh DrawText va MeasureText tu dong dung Segoe UI.
#define DrawText(text, x, y, font_size, color) DrawReadableText(text, x, y, font_size, color)
#define MeasureText(text, font_size) MeasureReadableText(text, font_size)



=== FILE: .\GUI\ui_layout.h ===

#pragma once
#include "raylib.h"

namespace ui_layout {
    const float BASE_WIDTH = 1220.0f;
    const float BASE_HEIGHT = 750.0f;

    inline float scale() {
        float sx = GetScreenWidth() / BASE_WIDTH;
        float sy = GetScreenHeight() / BASE_HEIGHT;
        return sx < sy ? sx : sy;
    }

    inline Vector2 offset() {
        float s = scale();
        return {
            (GetScreenWidth() - BASE_WIDTH * s) / 2.0f,
            (GetScreenHeight() - BASE_HEIGHT * s) / 2.0f
        };
    }

    inline Camera2D camera() {
        Camera2D c = {0};
        c.offset = offset();
        c.target = {0, 0};
        c.rotation = 0;
        c.zoom = scale();
        return c;
    }

    inline Vector2 mouse_position() {
        Vector2 p = GetMousePosition();
        Vector2 o = offset();
        float s = scale();

        return {
            (p.x - o.x) / s,
            (p.y - o.y) / s
        };
    }
}




=== FILE: .\Model\action_log.h ===

#pragma once
#include <string>
#include "library_entity.h"

// Class Template Action Log dÃƒÂ¹ng chung cho mÃ¡Â»Âi Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€˜i tÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£ng T (book, user, borrow...)
template <typename T>
class action_log {
private:
    std::string target_id;    // MÃƒÂ£ cÃ¡Â»Â§a Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€˜i tÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£ng bÃ¡Â»â€¹ tÃƒÂ¡c Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â„¢ng (VD: B001, US02, BR05)
    std::string action_type;  // LoÃ¡ÂºÂ¡i hÃƒÂ nh Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â„¢ng: "ADD", "DELETE", "UPDATE"
    T backup_data;            // BÃ¡ÂºÂ£n sao lÃ†Â°u dÃ¡Â»Â¯ liÃ¡Â»â€¡u trÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»â€ºc khi thay Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€¢i

public:
    action_log() : target_id(""), action_type(""), backup_data() {}

    action_log(std::string id, std::string type, const T& data)
        : target_id(id), action_type(type), backup_data(data) {}

    std::string get_target_id() const { return target_id; }
    std::string get_action_type() const { return action_type; }
    T get_backup_data() const { return backup_data; }
};




=== FILE: .\Model\book.cpp ===

#include "book.h"

using namespace std;

book::book() : library_entity("") {
    this->title = "";
    this->author = "";
    this->category = "";
    this->quantity = 0;
    this->year = 0;
}

book::book(string id, string title, string author, string category, int quantity, int year) 
    : library_entity(id) {
    this->title = title;
    this->author = author;
    this->category = category;
    this->quantity = quantity;
    this->year = year;
}

string book::get_title() const { return title; }
string book::get_author() const { return author; }
string book::get_category() const { return category; }
int book::get_quantity() const { return quantity; }
int book::get_year() const { return year; }

void book::set_title(string title) { this->title = title; }
void book::set_author(string author) { this->author = author; }
void book::set_category(string category) { this->category = category; }
void book::set_quantity(int quantity) {
    if (quantity >= 0) {
        this->quantity = quantity;
    }
}
void book::set_year(int year) { this->year = year; }

book& book::operator++() {
    quantity++;
    return *this;
}

book book::operator++(int) {
    book temp = *this;
    quantity++;
    return temp;
}

book& book::operator--() {
    if (quantity > 0) {
        quantity--;
    }
    return *this;
}

book book::operator--(int) {
    book temp = *this;
    if (quantity > 0) {
        quantity--;
    }
    return temp;
}

string book::get_entity_type() const {
    return "BOOK";
}
    
string book::to_record_string() const {
    return id + ";" + title + ";" + author + ";" + category + ";" + to_string(quantity) + ";" + to_string(year);
}

istream& operator>>(istream& in, book& b) {
    cout << "Ma sach: "; getline(in >> ws, b.id);
    cout << "Ten sach: "; getline(in, b.title);
    cout << "Tac gia: "; getline(in, b.author);
    cout << "The loai: "; getline(in, b.category);
    cout << "So luong: "; in >> b.quantity;
    while (in && b.quantity < 0) { cout << "So luong phai >= 0, nhap lai: "; in >> b.quantity; }
    cout << "Nam xuat ban: "; in >> b.year;
    return in;
}

ostream& operator<<(ostream& out, const book& b) {
    out << "Ma sach: " << b.id << '\n'
        << "Ten sach: " << b.title << '\n'
        << "Tac gia: " << b.author << '\n'
        << "The loai: " << b.category << '\n'
        << "So luong: " << b.quantity << '\n'
        << "Nam xuat ban: " << b.year;
    return out;
}

bool book::operator==(const book& other) const {
    return this->id == other.id;
}

    std::string get_entity_type() const override;
    std::string to_record_string() const override;

    friend std::istream& operator>>(std::istream& in, book& b);
    friend std::ostream& operator<<(std::ostream& out, const book& b);

    bool operator==(const book& other) const;
};




=== FILE: .\Model\borrow.cpp ===

#include "borrow.h"
#include <sstream>

using namespace std;

borrow::borrow() : library_entity("") {
    this->book_id = "";
    this->user_id = "";
    this->borrow_date = date();
    this->due_date = date();
    this->status = "";
}

borrow::borrow(string id, string book_id, string user_id, date borrow_date, date due_date, string status) 
    : library_entity(id) {
    this->book_id = book_id;
    this->user_id = user_id;
    this->borrow_date = borrow_date;
    this->due_date = due_date;
    this->status = status;
}

string borrow::get_book_id() const { return book_id; }
string borrow::get_user_id() const { return user_id; }
date borrow::get_borrow_date() const { return borrow_date; }
date borrow::get_due_date() const { return due_date; }
string borrow::get_status() const { return status; }

void borrow::set_book_id(string book_id) { this->book_id = book_id; }
void borrow::set_user_id(string user_id) { this->user_id = user_id; }
void borrow::set_borrow_date(date borrow_date) { this->borrow_date = borrow_date; }
void borrow::set_due_date(date due_date) { this->due_date = due_date; }
void borrow::set_status(string status) { this->status = status; }

string borrow::get_entity_type() const {
    return "BORROW";
}

string borrow::to_record_string() const {
    stringstream ss;
    ss << id << ";" << book_id << ";" << user_id << ";" << borrow_date << ";" << due_date << ";" << status;
    return ss.str();
}

istream& operator>>(istream& in, borrow& b) {
    cout << "Ma phieu muon: "; getline(in >> ws, b.id);
    cout << "Ma sach: "; getline(in >> ws, b.book_id);
    cout << "Ma nguoi muon: "; getline(in >> ws, b.user_id);
    cout << "Ngay muon (DD MM YYYY): "; in >> b.borrow_date;
    cout << "Han tra (DD MM YYYY): "; in >> b.due_date;
    cout << "Trang thai: "; getline(in >> ws, b.status);
    return in;
}

ostream& operator<<(ostream& out, const borrow& b) {
    out << "Ma phieu muon: " << b.id << '\n'
        << "Ma sach: " << b.book_id << '\n'
        << "Ma nguoi muon: " << b.user_id << '\n'
        << "Ngay muon: " << b.borrow_date << '\n'
        << "Han tra: " << b.due_date << '\n'
        << "Trang thai: " << b.status;
    return out;
}

=== FILE: .\Model\date.cpp ===

#include "date.h"
#include <stdexcept>

date::date() {
    day = 1;
    month = 1;
    year = 2026;
}

date::date(int day, int month, int year) {
    this->day = day;
    this->month = month;
    this->year = year;

    if (!is_valid()) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("Ngay thang nam khong hop le");
    }
}

bool date::is_leap_year() const {
    return year % 400 == 0 ||
           (year % 4 == 0 && year % 100 != 0);
}

int date::days_in_month() const {
    if (month == 2)
        return is_leap_year() ? 29 : 28;

    if (month == 4 || month == 6 ||
        month == 9 || month == 11)
        return 30;

    return 31;
}

bool date::is_valid() const {
    if (year < 1)
        return false;

    if (month < 1 || month > 12)
        return false;

    if (day < 1 || day > days_in_month())
        return false;

    return true;
}

void date::input() {
    do {
        std::cout << "nhap ngay thang nam: ";
        std::cin >> day >> month >> year;

        if (!is_valid())
            std::cout << "ngay khong hop le!\n";

    } while (!is_valid());
}

void date::output() const {
    std::cout << day << "/" << month << "/" << year;
}

int date::get_day() const {
    return day;
}

int date::get_month() const {
    return month;
}

int date::get_year() const {
    return year;
}

void date::set_day(int day) {
    int old = this->day;
    this->day = day;

    if (!is_valid())
        this->day = old;
}

void date::set_month(int month) {
    int old = this->month;
    this->month = month;

    if (!is_valid())
        this->month = old;
}

void date::set_year(int year) {
    int old = this->year;
    this->year = year;

    if (!is_valid())
        this->year = old;
}

date& date::operator++() {
    day++;

    if (day > days_in_month()) {
        day = 1;
        month++;

        if (month > 12) {
            month = 1;
            year++;
        }
    }

    return *this;
}

date date::operator++(int) {
    date temp = *this;
    ++(*this);
    return temp;
}

date date::add_days(int days) const {
    date result = *this;

    for (int i = 0; i < days; i++) {
        ++result;
    }

    return result;
}

bool date::operator==(const date& other) const {
    return day == other.day &&
           month == other.month &&
           year == other.year;
}

bool date::operator!=(const date& other) const {
    return !(*this == other);
}

bool date::operator<(const date& other) const {
    if (year != other.year)
        return year < other.year;

    if (month != other.month)
        return month < other.month;

    return day < other.day;
}

bool date::operator>(const date& other) const {
    return other < *this;
}

bool date::operator<=(const date& other) const {
    return !(*this > other);
}

bool date::operator>=(const date& other) const {
    return !(*this < other);
}

int date::operator-(const date& other) const {
    date a = *this;
    date b = other;

    int count = 0;

    if (a == b)
        return 0;

    if (a > b) {
        while (b < a) {
            ++b;
            count++;
        }
    }
    else {
        while (a < b) {
            ++a;
            count++;
        }
    }

    return (a == *this) ? count : -count;
}

std::istream& operator>>(std::istream& in, date& d) {
    do {
        in >> d.day >> d.month >> d.year;

        if (!d.is_valid())
            std::cout << "ngay khong hop le, nhap lai: ";

    } while (!d.is_valid());

    return in;
}

std::ostream& operator<<(std::ostream& out, const date& d) {
    out << d.day << "/" << d.month << "/" << d.year;
    return out;
}
std::string date::get_day_of_week() const {
    int d = this->day;
    int m = this->month;
    int y = this->year;
    
    // ThuÃ¡ÂºÂ­t toÃƒÂ¡n yÃƒÂªu cÃ¡ÂºÂ§u thÃƒÂ¡ng 1, 2 phÃ¡ÂºÂ£i Ã„â€˜Ã†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£c tÃƒÂ­nh lÃƒÂ  thÃƒÂ¡ng 13, 14 cÃ¡Â»Â§a nÃ„Æ’m trÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»â€ºc
    if (m < 3) {
        m += 12;
        y -= 1;
    }
    
    int k = y % 100;
    int j = y / 100;
    
    // CÃƒÂ´ng thÃ¡Â»Â©c Zeller
    int f = d + ((13 * (m + 1)) / 5) + k + (k / 4) + (j / 4) + (5 * j);
    int day_of_week = f % 7;
    
    // Zeller trÃ¡ÂºÂ£ vÃ¡Â»Â: 0 = ThÃ¡Â»Â© 7, 1 = ChÃ¡Â»Â§ nhÃ¡ÂºÂ­t, 2 = ThÃ¡Â»Â© 2...
    if(day_of_week == 0 ) return "thu 7";
        else if(day_of_week == 1 ) return "chu nhat";
             else if(day_of_week == 2 ) return "thu 2";
                  else if(day_of_week == 3 ) return "thu 3";
                        else if(day_of_week == 4 ) return "thu 4";
                            else if(day_of_week == 5 ) return "thu 5";
                                 else if(day_of_week == 6 ) return "thu 6";
    return "";
}

#pragma once
#include <string>

// LÃ¡Â»Å¡P TRÃ¡Â»ÂªU TÃ†Â¯Ã¡Â»Â¢NG CÃ†Â  SÃ¡Â»Å¾ CHO MÃ¡Â»Å’I THÃ¡Â»Â°C THÃ¡Â»â€š TRONG THÃ†Â¯ VIÃ¡Â»â€ N (ABSTRACTION)
class library_entity {

    std::string id;

    library_entity() : id("") {}
    library_entity(std::string id) : id(id) {}
    virtual ~library_entity() {}

    virtual std::string to_record_string() const = 0;

=== FILE: .\Model\library_exception.h ===

#pragma once
#include <exception>
#include <string>

class library_exception : public std::exception {
private:
    std::string msg;

public:
    explicit library_exception(const std::string& message) : msg(message) {}
    virtual ~library_exception() noexcept {}

    const char* what() const noexcept override {
        return msg.c_str();
    }
};




=== FILE: .\Model\role_permission.h ===

#pragma once
#include <string>
#include <iostream>

// INTERFACE TRÃ¡Â»ÂªU TÃ†Â¯Ã¡Â»Â¢NG CHO PHÃƒâ€šN QUYÃ¡Â»â‚¬N (STRATEGY PATTERN)
class role_permission {
public:
    virtual ~role_permission() {}

    // CÃƒÂ¡c hÃƒÂ m thuÃ¡ÂºÂ§n Ã¡ÂºÂ£o Ã„â€˜a hÃƒÂ¬nh
    virtual std::string get_role_name() const = 0;
    virtual int get_max_menu_choice() const = 0;
    virtual bool can_manage_users() const = 0;
    virtual void show_menu(const std::string& user_name) const = 0;
};

// 1. KÃ¡ÂºÂ¾ THÃ¡Â»ÂªA: QuyÃ¡Â»Ân Manager (ToÃƒÂ n quyÃ¡Â»Ân)
class manager_permission : public role_permission {
public:
    std::string get_role_name() const override { return "manager"; }
    int get_max_menu_choice() const override { return 17; }
    bool can_manage_users() const override { return true; }

    void show_menu(const std::string& user_name) const override {
        std::cout << "\n==================== MANAGER MENU (" << user_name << " - TOAN QUYEN) ====================\n";
        std::cout << "--- QUAN LY SACH ---\n";
        std::cout << " 1. Tra cuu sach (Goi y sach lien quan)\n";
        std::cout << " 2. Them sach moi vao kho\n";
        std::cout << " 3. Sua thong tin sach\n";
        std::cout << " 4. Xoa sach khoi kho\n";
        std::cout << "--- QUAN LY MUON / TRA & DUYET HANG DOI ---\n";
        std::cout << " 5. Lap phieu muon sach truc tiep\n";
        std::cout << " 6. Xac nhan tra sach\n";
        std::cout << " 7. Xoa phieu muon\n";
        std::cout << " 8. Duyet yeu cau dang ky muon sach (Hang doi FIFO Queue)\n";
        std::cout << "--- QUAN LY CA TRUC & LUONG ---\n";
        std::cout << " 9. Xem toan bo lich truc thu vien\n";
        std::cout << "10. Dang ky ca truc cho nhan vien\n";
        std::cout << "11. Rut ten nhan vien khoi ca truc\n";
        std::cout << "12. Xoa toan bo mot ca truc\n";
        std::cout << "13. Tinh luong ca truc cua Admin (200.000 VND / buoi)\n";
        std::cout << "--- QUAN LY NGUOI DUNG ---\n";
        std::cout << "14. Them tai khoan moi (Admin / User / Manager)\n";
        std::cout << "15. Xoa tai khoan\n";
        std::cout << "16. Cap nhat tai khoan\n";
        std::cout << "17. Tra cuu vi tri ke cua sach theo Ma ID\n";
        std::cout << " 0. Dang xuat\n";
        std::cout << "====================================================================\n";
    }
};

// 2. KÃ¡ÂºÂ¾ THÃ¡Â»ÂªA: QuyÃ¡Â»Ân Admin (QuÃ¡ÂºÂ£n trÃ¡Â»â€¹ viÃƒÂªn)
class admin_permission : public role_permission {
public:
    std::string get_role_name() const override { return "admin"; }

    void show_menu(const std::string& user_name) const override {
        std::cout << "\n================ ADMIN MENU (QUAN TRI VIEN: " << user_name << ") ================\n";
        std::cout << "--- QUAN LY SACH ---\n";

        std::cout << "===========================================================================\n";
    }
};

// 3. KÃ¡ÂºÂ¾ THÃ¡Â»ÂªA: QuyÃ¡Â»Ân Ã„ÂÃ¡Â»â„¢c giÃ¡ÂºÂ£ (User)
class reader_permission : public role_permission {
public:
    std::string get_role_name() const override { return "user"; }
    int get_max_menu_choice() const override { return 5; }
    bool can_manage_users() const override { return false; }

    void show_menu(const std::string& user_name) const override {
        std::cout << "\n================ DOC GIA (USER: " << user_name << ") ================\n";
        std::cout << "1. Tim kiem sach & xem sach goi y cung the loai\n";
        std::cout << "2. Dang ky muon sach\n";
        std::cout << "3. Tra cuu vi tri ke sach de den lay\n";
        std::cout << "4. Xem sach dang muon\n";
        std::cout << "5. Xem lich su muon sach\n";
        std::cout << "0. Dang xuat\n";
        std::cout << "====================================================================\n";
    }
};

// FACTORY METHOD PATTERN: KhÃ¡Â»Å¸i tÃ¡ÂºÂ¡o Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€˜i tÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£ng quyÃ¡Â»Ân hÃ¡ÂºÂ¡n tÃ†Â°Ã†Â¡ng Ã¡Â»Â©ng
class permission_factory {
public:
    static role_permission* create(const std::string& role_str) {
        if (role_str == "manager") return new manager_permission();
        if (role_str == "admin")   return new admin_permission();
        return new reader_permission();
    }
};




=== FILE: .\Model\shift.cpp ===

#include "shift.h"

using namespace std;

shift::shift() {
    this->session = "";
    this->current_count = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        admins[i] = "";
    }
    // shift_date sÃ¡ÂºÂ½ tÃ¡Â»Â± gÃ¡Â»Âi constructor mÃ¡ÂºÂ·c Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€¹nh cÃ¡Â»Â§a class date
}

shift::shift(date shift_date, string session) {
    this->shift_date = shift_date;
    this->session = session;
    this->current_count = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        admins[i] = "";
    }
}

date shift::get_shift_date() const { return shift_date; }
string shift::get_session() const { return session; }
int shift::get_current_count() const { return current_count; }

string shift::get_admin_at(int index) const {
    if (index >= 0 && index < current_count) return admins[index];
    return "";
}

void shift::set_shift_date(date shift_date) { this->shift_date = shift_date; }
void shift::set_session(string session) { this->session = session; }

bool shift::add_admin(string admin_id) {
    if (has_admin(admin_id)) return false; // Ã„ÂÃƒÂ£ Ã„â€˜Ã„Æ’ng kÃƒÂ­ rÃ¡Â»â€œi
    if (current_count >= 4) return false;  // Ã„ÂÃƒÂ£ Ã„â€˜Ã¡ÂºÂ§y slot

    admins[current_count] = admin_id;
    current_count++;
    return true;
}

bool shift::remove_admin(string admin_id) {
    int pos = -1;
    for (int i = 0; i < current_count; i++) {
        if (admins[i] == admin_id) {
            pos = i;
            break;
        }
    }

    if (pos == -1) return false; // KhÃƒÂ´ng tÃƒÂ¬m thÃ¡ÂºÂ¥y admin trong ca nÃƒÂ y

    // DÃ¡Â»â€œn mÃ¡ÂºÂ£ng Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»Æ’ lÃ¡ÂºÂ¥p chÃ¡Â»â€” trÃ¡Â»â€˜ng
    for (int i = pos; i < current_count - 1; i++) {
        admins[i] = admins[i + 1];
    }
    admins[current_count - 1] = "";
    current_count--;
    return true;
}

bool shift::has_admin(string admin_id) const {
    for (int i = 0; i < current_count; i++) {
        if (admins[i] == admin_id) return true;
    }
    return false;
}

string shift::get_all_admins_string() const {
    string result = "";
    for (int i = 0; i < current_count; i++) {
        result += admins[i];
        if (i < current_count - 1) result += ",";
    }
    return result;
}
#include <sstream> // Ã„ÂÃ¡ÂºÂ£m bÃ¡ÂºÂ£o cÃƒÂ³ thÃ†Â° viÃ¡Â»â€¡n nÃƒÂ y Ã¡Â»Å¸ Ã„â€˜Ã¡ÂºÂ§u file hoÃ¡ÂºÂ·c trÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»â€ºc hÃƒÂ m

std::string shift::to_record_string() const {
    std::stringstream ss;
    ss << shift_date << ";" << session << ";" << get_all_admins_string();
    return ss.str();
}
