=== FILE: .\Model\user.cpp ===

#include "user.h"

using namespace std;

void user::update_permission_strategy() {
    delete permission_strategy;
    permission_strategy = permission_factory::create(this->role);
}

user::user() : library_entity(""), name(""), password(""), role("user"), permission_strategy(nullptr) {
    update_permission_strategy();
}

user::user(string id, string name, string password, string role) 
    : library_entity(id), name(name), password(password), role(role), permission_strategy(nullptr) {
    update_permission_strategy();
}

user::user(const user& other) 
    : library_entity(other.id), name(other.name), password(other.password), role(other.role), permission_strategy(nullptr) {
    update_permission_strategy();
}

user& user::operator=(const user& other) {
    if (this != &other) {
        this->id = other.id;
        this->name = other.name;
        this->password = other.password;
        this->role = other.role;
        update_permission_strategy();
    }
    return *this;
}

user::~user() {
    delete permission_strategy;
}

string user::get_name() const { return name; }
string user::get_password() const { return password; }
string user::get_role() const { return role; }

void user::set_name(string name) { this->name = name; }
void user::set_password(string password) { this->password = password; }
void user::set_role(string role) { 
    this->role = role; 
    update_permission_strategy();
}

void user::display_role_menu() const {
    if (permission_strategy != nullptr) {
        permission_strategy->show_menu(this->name);
    }
}

int user::get_max_menu_option() const {
    return (permission_strategy != nullptr) ? permission_strategy->get_max_menu_choice() : 0;
}

bool user::has_user_management_privilege() const {
    return (permission_strategy != nullptr) ? permission_strategy->can_manage_users() : false;
}

string user::get_entity_type() const {
    return "USER";
}

string user::to_record_string() const {
    return id + ";" + name + ";" + password + ";" + role;
}

istream& operator>>(istream& in, user& u) {
    cout << "Ma nguoi dung: "; getline(in >> ws, u.id);
    cout << "Ho ten: "; getline(in, u.name);
    cout << "Mat khau: "; getline(in, u.password);
    cout << "Vai tro: "; getline(in, u.role);
    u.update_permission_strategy();
    return in;
}

ostream& operator<<(ostream& out, const user& u) {
    out << "Ma nguoi dung: " << u.id << '\n'
        << "Ho ten: " << u.name << '\n'
        << "Mat khau: [an]" << '\n'
        << "Vai tro: " << u.role;
    return out;
}




=== FILE: .\Model\user.h ===

#pragma once
#include <string>
#include <iostream>
#include "library_entity.h"
#include "role_permission.h"

class user : public library_entity {
private:
    std::string name;
    std::string password;
    std::string role;
    role_permission* permission_strategy; // Con trÃ¡Â»Â Ã„Âa hÃƒÂ¬nh (Strategy Pattern)

    void update_permission_strategy();

public:
    user();
    user(std::string id, std::string name, std::string password, std::string role);
    user(const user& other);
    user& operator=(const user& other);
    virtual ~user();

    std::string get_name() const;
    std::string get_password() const;
    std::string get_role() const;

    void set_name(std::string name);
    void set_password(std::string password);
    void set_role(std::string role);

    // CÃƒÂ¡c hÃƒÂ nh vi Ã„Âa hÃƒÂ¬nh (Polymorphic Dispatch)
    void display_role_menu() const;
    int get_max_menu_option() const;
    bool has_user_management_privilege() const;

    // Ghi Ã„â€˜ÃƒÂ¨ (Override) hÃƒÂ m thuÃ¡ÂºÂ§n Ã¡ÂºÂ£o tÃ¡Â»Â« lÃ¡Â»â€ºp trÃ¡Â»Â«u tÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£ng library_entity
    std::string get_entity_type() const override;
    std::string to_record_string() const override;

    friend std::istream& operator>>(std::istream& in, user& u);
    friend std::ostream& operator<<(std::ostream& out, const user& u);
};




=== FILE: .\Service\book_service.cpp ===

#include "book_service.h"

book_service::book_service(std::string filename) {
    data_file = filename;
    load_data();
}

book_service::~book_service() {
    save_data(); // TÃ¡Â»Â± Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â„¢ng lÃ†Â°u file khi tÃ¡ÂºÂ¯t chÃ†Â°Ã†Â¡ng trÃƒÂ¬nh
}

// --- 1. Ã„ÂÃ¡Â»Å’C DÃ¡Â»Â® LIÃ¡Â»â€ U TÃ¡Â»Âª FILE ---
bool book_service::load_data() {
    std::ifstream file(data_file);
    if (!file.is_open()) return false;

    std::string line;
    while (std::getline(file, line)) {
        if (line.empty()) continue;
        
        book b;
        parse_line(line, b); // TÃƒÂ¡ch dÃƒÂ²ng thÃƒÂ nh Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€˜i tÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£ng book (tÃƒÂ¹y Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€¹nh dÃ¡ÂºÂ¡ng file cÃ¡Â»Â§a bÃ¡ÂºÂ¡n)
        
        // Ã„ÂÃ¡ÂºÂ©y vÃƒÂ o Danh sÃƒÂ¡ch liÃƒÂªn kÃ¡ÂºÂ¿t
        book_list.push_back(b);
        
        // Ã„ÂÃ„Æ’ng kÃƒÂ½ ngay Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€¹a chÃ¡Â»â€° node Ã„â€˜ÃƒÂ³ vÃƒÂ o BÃ¡ÂºÂ£ng bÃ„Æ’m
        node<book>* new_node = book_list.get_tail(); 
        book_index.insert(b.get_id(), new_node);
    }
    file.close();
    return true;
}

// --- 2. GHI DÃ¡Â»Â® LIÃ¡Â»â€ U XUÃ¡Â»ÂNG FILE ---
bool book_service::save_data() {
    return file_helper::save_list_to_file(data_file, book_list);
}

// --- 3. THÃƒÅ M SÃƒÂCH ---
bool book_service::add_book(book new_book) {
    // Check O(1) xem mÃƒÂ£ sÃƒÂ¡ch Ã„â€˜ÃƒÂ£ tÃ¡Â»â€œn tÃ¡ÂºÂ¡i chÃ†Â°a
    if (book_index.find(new_book.get_id()) != nullptr) {
        return false; // BÃƒÂ¡o lÃ¡Â»â€”i trÃƒÂ¹ng ID
    }

    book_list.push_back(new_book);
    book_index.insert(new_book.get_id(), book_list.get_tail());

    save_data(); // LÃ†Â°u ngay xuÃ¡Â»â€˜ng file
    return true;
}

// --- 4. XÃƒâ€œA SÃƒÂCH ---
bool book_service::delete_book(std::string id) {
    node<book>* target = book_index.find(id);
    if (target == nullptr) return false; // KhÃƒÂ´ng tÃƒÂ¬m thÃ¡ÂºÂ¥y

    // HÃ¡Â»Â§y mÃ¡Â»Â¥c lÃ¡Â»Â¥c trÃƒÂªn BÃ¡ÂºÂ£ng bÃ„Æ’m trÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»â€ºc
    book_index.remove(id);
    
    // XÃƒÂ³a node khÃ¡Â»Âi DSLK (YÃƒÂªu cÃ¡ÂºÂ§u linked_list cÃƒÂ³ hÃƒÂ m remove_node(node*))
    book_list.remove_node(target); 

    save_data(); // LÃ†Â°u ngay xuÃ¡Â»â€˜ng file
    return true;
}

// --- 5. CÃ¡ÂºÂ¬P NHÃ¡ÂºÂ¬T THÃƒâ€NG TIN (Ã„ÂiÃ¡Â»Æ’m nhÃ¡ÂºÂ¡y cÃ¡ÂºÂ£m) ---
bool book_service::update_book(std::string old_id, book new_data) {
    node<book>* target = book_index.find(old_id);
    if (target == nullptr) return false;

    std::string new_id = new_data.get_id();

    // NÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u Admin Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€¢i MÃƒÂ£ sÃƒÂ¡ch (ID)
    if (old_id != new_id) {
        // KiÃ¡Â»Æ’m tra xem ID mÃ¡Â»â€ºi cÃƒÂ³ bÃ¡Â»â€¹ trÃƒÂ¹ng vÃ¡Â»â€ºi sÃƒÂ¡ch khÃƒÂ¡c khÃƒÂ´ng
        if (book_index.find(new_id) != nullptr) return false; 
        
        book_index.remove(old_id);             // XÃƒÂ³a chÃ¡Â»â€° mÃ¡Â»Â¥c cÃ…Â©
        target->set_data(new_data);            // Ghi Ã„â€˜ÃƒÂ¨ dÃ¡Â»Â¯ liÃ¡Â»â€¡u mÃ¡Â»â€ºi
        book_index.insert(new_id, target);     // CÃ¡ÂºÂ¥p chÃ¡Â»â€° mÃ¡Â»Â¥c mÃ¡Â»â€ºi
    } else {
        // ChÃ¡Â»â€° sÃ¡Â»Â­a tÃƒÂªn/tÃƒÂ¡c giÃ¡ÂºÂ£, giÃ¡Â»Â¯ nguyÃƒÂªn mÃƒÂ£ ID
        target->set_data(new_data);
    }

    save_data(); // LÃ†Â°u ngay xuÃ¡Â»â€˜ng file
    return true;
}

// --- 6. TÃƒÅ’M KIÃ¡ÂºÂ¾M CHÃƒÂNH XÃƒÂC (TÃ¡Â»â€˜c Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â„¢ O(1)) ---
node<book>* book_service::get_book_by_id(std::string id) {
    return book_index.find(id); // DÃƒÂ¹ng BÃ¡ÂºÂ£ng bÃ„Æ’m
}

// --- 7. TÃƒÅ’M KIÃ¡ÂºÂ¾M MÃ¡Â»Å¾ RÃ¡Â»ËœNG (TÃ¡Â»â€˜c Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â„¢ O(N)) ---
linked_list<book> book_service::search_books(std::string keyword) {
    linked_list<book> result;
    node<book>* curr = book_list.get_head();

    while (curr != nullptr) {
        book b = curr->get_data();
        
        // GÃ¡Â»Âi thuÃ¡ÂºÂ­t toÃƒÂ¡n xÃ¡Â»Â­ lÃƒÂ½ chuÃ¡Â»â€”i Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»Æ’ soi vÃƒÂ o TÃƒÂªn, TÃƒÂ¡c giÃ¡ÂºÂ£, vÃƒÂ  ThÃ¡Â»Æ’ loÃ¡ÂºÂ¡i
        if (search_algo::contains(b.get_id(), keyword) ||
            search_algo::contains(b.get_title(), keyword) ||
            search_algo::contains(b.get_author(), keyword)) {
            
            result.push_back(b);
        }
        curr = curr->get_next();
    }
    return result;
}

linked_list<book> book_service::get_recommended_books(
    std::string author, std::string category, std::string exclude_book_id) {
    linked_list<book> result;

    // Æ¯u tiÃªn sÃ¡ch cÃ¹ng tÃ¡c giáº£.
    node<book>* current = book_list.get_head();
    while (current != nullptr) {
        book item = current->get_data();
        if (item.get_id() != exclude_book_id &&
            !author.empty() && item.get_author() == author) {
            result.push_back(item);
        }
        current = current->get_next();
    }

    // Bá»• sung sÃ¡ch cÃ¹ng thá»ƒ loáº¡i, khÃ´ng thÃªm trÃ¹ng sÃ¡ch Ä‘Ã£ cÃ³.
    current = book_list.get_head();
    while (current != nullptr) {
        book item = current->get_data();
        bool already_added = false;

        node<book>* added = result.get_head();
        while (added != nullptr) {
            if (added->get_data().get_id() == item.get_id()) {
                already_added = true;
                break;
            }
            added = added->get_next();
        }

        if (item.get_id() != exclude_book_id &&
            !category.empty() &&
            item.get_category() == category &&
            !already_added) {
            result.push_back(item);
        }
        current = current->get_next();
    }

    return result;
}

// --- 8. SÃ¡ÂºÂ®P XÃ¡ÂºÂ¾P BÃ¡ÂºÂ°NG MERGE SORT ---
void book_service::sort_books(bool (*cmp)(book, book)) {
    node<book>* head_ptr = book_list.get_head();    

    sort_algo::merge_sort(&head_ptr, cmp);
    book_list.update_after_sort(head_ptr);
}

// --- TÃƒÂ¡ch dÃƒÂ²ng tÃ¡Â»Â« book.txt (Ã„ÂÃ¡Â»â€¹nh dÃ¡ÂºÂ¡ng: ID;Title;Author;Category;Quantity;Year) ---
void book_service::parse_line(std::string line, book& b) {
    if (!line.empty() && line.back() == '\r') {
        line.pop_back();
    }   
    std::stringstream ss(line);
    std::string id, title, author, category;
    std::string qty_str, year_str;

    // Ã„ÂÃ¡Â»Âc cÃƒÂ¡c trÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Âng dÃ¡ÂºÂ¡ng chuÃ¡Â»â€”i phÃƒÂ¢n cÃƒÂ¡ch bÃ¡Â»Å¸i dÃ¡ÂºÂ¥u ';'
    std::getline(ss, id, ';');
    std::getline(ss, title, ';');
    std::getline(ss, author, ';');
    std::getline(ss, category, ';');
    std::getline(ss, qty_str, ';');
    std::getline(ss, year_str, ';');

    // ChuyÃ¡Â»Æ’n Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€¢i cÃƒÂ¡c trÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Âng sÃ¡Â»â€˜ lÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£ng vÃƒÂ  nÃ„Æ’m xuÃ¡ÂºÂ¥t bÃ¡ÂºÂ£n tÃ¡Â»Â« string sang int an toÃƒÂ n
    int quantity = 0;
    int year = 0;
    try
    {
        if (!qty_str.empty())
            quantity = std::stoi(search_algo::trim(qty_str));
    }
    catch (...)
    {
        quantity = 0;
    }

    try
    {
        if (!year_str.empty())
            year = std::stoi(search_algo::trim(year_str));
    }
    catch (...)
    {
        year = 0;
    }
    // KhÃ¡Â»Å¸i tÃ¡ÂºÂ¡o Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€˜i tÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£ng book hoÃƒÂ n chÃ¡Â»â€°nh
    b = book(id, title, author, category, quantity, year);
}

linked_list<book> book_service::filter_books(std::string title_kw, std::string author_kw, std::string category_kw, int year) {
    linked_list<book> result;
    node<book>* curr = book_list.get_head();

    while (curr != nullptr) {
        book b = curr->get_data();
        bool match = true;

        if (!title_kw.empty() && !search_algo::contains(b.get_title(), title_kw)) match = false;
        if (!author_kw.empty() && !search_algo::contains(b.get_author(), author_kw)) match = false;
        if (!category_kw.empty() && !search_algo::contains(b.get_category(), category_kw)) match = false;
        if (year > 0 && b.get_year() != year) match = false;

        if (match) {
            result.push_back(b);
        }
        curr = curr->get_next();
    }
    return result;
}




=== FILE: .\Service\book_service.h ===

#pragma once
#include <string>
#include <iostream>
#include <fstream>
#include <sstream>
#include "../Model/book.h"        // Class chÃ¡Â»Â©a thÃƒÂ´ng tin thÃ¡Â»Â±c thÃ¡Â»Æ’ sÃƒÂ¡ch
#include "file_helper.h" // Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€¹nh dÃ¡ÂºÂ¡ng dÃ¡Â»Â¯ liÃ¡Â»â€¡u
#include "../Structure/linked_list.h" // CÃ¡ÂºÂ¥u trÃƒÂºc lÃ†Â°u trÃ¡Â»Â¯ chÃƒÂ­nh
#include "../Structure/hash_table.h"  // BÃ¡ÂºÂ£ng bÃ„Æ’m tra cÃ¡Â»Â©u nhanh
#include "../Algorithm/search_algo.h" // ThuÃ¡ÂºÂ­t toÃƒÂ¡n tÃƒÂ¬m kiÃ¡ÂºÂ¿m chuÃ¡Â»â€”i
#include "../Algorithm/sort_algo.h"        // ThuÃ¡ÂºÂ­t toÃƒÂ¡n sÃ¡ÂºÂ¯p xÃ¡ÂºÂ¿p

class book_service {
private:
    linked_list<book> book_list;
    hash_table<book> book_index;
    std::string data_file;

    // HÃƒÂ m phÃ¡Â»Â¥ trÃ¡Â»Â£ tÃƒÂ¡ch chuÃ¡Â»â€”i (split) Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»Æ’ Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»Âc file txt
    void parse_line(std::string line, book& b);

public:
    book_service(std::string filename = "book.txt");
    ~book_service();

    // 1. NhÃƒÂ³m thao tÃƒÂ¡c File (I/O)
    bool load_data();
    bool save_data();

    // 2. NhÃƒÂ³m NghiÃ¡Â»â€¡p vÃ¡Â»Â¥ CRUD
    bool add_book(book new_book);
    bool delete_book(std::string id);
    bool update_book(std::string old_id, book new_data);

    // 3. NhÃƒÂ³m Tra cÃ¡Â»Â©u & CÃ¡ÂºÂ¥u trÃƒÂºc
    node<book>* get_book_by_id(std::string id);
    linked_list<book> search_books(std::string keyword);
    linked_list<book> get_recommended_books(std::string author, std::string category, std::string exclude_book_id);
    
    // HÃƒÂ m truyÃ¡Â»Ân con trÃ¡Â»Â hÃƒÂ m so sÃƒÂ¡nh Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»Æ’ Sort (VD: cmp_by_year, cmp_by_title)
    void sort_books(bool (*cmp)(book, book)); 
    void display_all();

    // LÃ¡Â»Âc kÃ¡ÂºÂ¿t hÃ¡Â»Â£p nhiÃ¡Â»Âu tiÃƒÂªu chÃƒÂ­ (truyÃ¡Â»Ân chuÃ¡Â»â€”i rÃ¡Â»â€”ng "" hoÃ¡ÂºÂ·c year = 0 nÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u bÃ¡Â»Â qua tiÃƒÂªu chÃƒÂ­ Ã„â€˜ÃƒÂ³)
    linked_list<book> filter_books(std::string title_kw, std::string author_kw, std::string category_kw, int year = 0);
};




=== FILE: .\Service\borrow_service.cpp ===

#include "borrow_service.h"

borrow_service::borrow_service(std::string filename) {
    data_file = filename;
    load_data();
}

borrow_service::~borrow_service() {
    save_data(); // TÃ¡Â»Â± Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â„¢ng Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€œng bÃ¡Â»â„¢ file khi thoÃƒÂ¡t
}

// --- TÃƒÂ¡ch dÃƒÂ²ng tÃ¡Â»Â« borrow.txt (Ã„ÂÃ¡Â»â€¹nh dÃ¡ÂºÂ¡ng: BorrowID;BookID;UserID;BorrowDate;DueDate;Status) ---
void borrow_service::parse_line(std::string line, borrow& b) {
    if (!line.empty() && line.back() == '\r') {
        line.pop_back();
    }
    std::stringstream ss(line);
    std::string id, book_id, user_id, b_date_str, due_date_str, status;

    std::getline(ss, id, ';');
    std::getline(ss, book_id, ';');
    std::getline(ss, user_id, ';');
    std::getline(ss, b_date_str, ';');
    std::getline(ss, due_date_str, ';');
    std::getline(ss, status, ';');

    int d1 = 1, m1 = 1, y1 = 2026;
    char slash = '/';
    std::stringstream ss_b(search_algo::trim(b_date_str));
    ss_b >> d1 >> slash >> m1 >> slash >> y1;
    date borrow_date(d1, m1, y1);

    int d2 = 1, m2 = 1, y2 = 2026;
    std::stringstream ss_d(search_algo::trim(due_date_str));
    ss_d >> d2 >> slash >> m2 >> slash >> y2;
    date due_date(d2, m2, y2);

    b = borrow(search_algo::trim(id), search_algo::trim(book_id), 
               search_algo::trim(user_id), borrow_date, due_date, search_algo::trim(status));
}

// --- 1. Ã„ÂÃ¡Â»Å’C DÃ¡Â»Â® LIÃ¡Â»â€ U TÃ¡Â»Âª FILE ---
bool borrow_service::load_data() {
    std::ifstream file(data_file);
    if (!file.is_open()) return false;

    std::string line;
    while (std::getline(file, line)) {
        if (line.empty()) continue;
        
        borrow b;
        parse_line(line, b);
        
        borrow_list.push_back(b);
        node<borrow>* new_node = borrow_list.get_tail(); 
        borrow_index.insert(b.get_id(), new_node); // Ã„ÂÃ„Æ’ng kÃƒÂ½ bÃ¡ÂºÂ£ng bÃ„Æ’m O(1)
    }
    file.close();
    return true;
}

// --- 2. GHI DÃ¡Â»Â® LIÃ¡Â»â€ U XUÃ¡Â»ÂNG FILE ---
bool borrow_service::save_data() {
    return file_helper::save_list_to_file(data_file, borrow_list);
}

// --- 3. TÃ¡ÂºÂ O PHIÃ¡ÂºÂ¾U MÃ†Â¯Ã¡Â»Â¢N SÃƒÂCH (DÃƒÂ¹ng toÃƒÂ¡n tÃ¡Â»Â­ --) ---
bool borrow_service::create_borrow_ticket(std::string borrow_id, std::string book_id, std::string user_id, date borrow_date, date due_date, book_service& b_service, user_service& u_service) {
    // Check trÃƒÂ¹ng mÃƒÂ£ phiÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u mÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£n O(1)
    if (borrow_index.find(borrow_id) != nullptr) return false;

    // Check xem User cÃƒÂ³ tÃ¡Â»â€œn tÃ¡ÂºÂ¡i khÃƒÂ´ng
    if (u_service.get_user_by_id(user_id) == nullptr) return false;

    // Check xem User cÃƒÂ³ Ã„â€˜ang nÃ¡Â»Â£ sÃƒÂ¡ch quÃƒÂ¡ hÃ¡ÂºÂ¡n khÃƒÂ´ng
    if (has_overdue_borrows(user_id)) return false;

    // GIÃ¡Â»Å¡I HÃ¡ÂºÂ N MÃ†Â¯Ã¡Â»Â¢N TÃ¡Â»ÂI Ã„ÂA 7 CUÃ¡Â»ÂN Ã„ÂANG MÃ†Â¯Ã¡Â»Â¢N
    if (count_active_borrows_by_user(user_id) >= 7) return false;

    // Check xem User cÃƒÂ³ Ã„â€˜ang mÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£n chÃƒÂ­nh cuÃ¡Â»â€˜n sÃƒÂ¡ch nÃƒÂ y chÃ†Â°a trÃ¡ÂºÂ£ khÃƒÂ´ng
    if (is_user_borrowing_book(user_id, book_id)) return false;

    // Check xem Book cÃƒÂ³ tÃ¡Â»â€œn tÃ¡ÂºÂ¡i vÃƒÂ  cÃƒÂ²n sÃ¡Â»â€˜ lÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£ng khÃƒÂ´ng
    node<book>* book_node = b_service.get_book_by_id(book_id);
    if (book_node == nullptr) return false;
    if (due_date < borrow_date) return false; // NgÃƒÂ y hÃ¡ÂºÂ¹n trÃ¡ÂºÂ£ khÃƒÂ´ng Ã„â€˜Ã†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£c trÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»â€ºc ngÃƒÂ y mÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£n
    
    // LÃ¡ÂºÂ¥y tham chiÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u trÃ¡Â»Â±c tiÃ¡ÂºÂ¿p Ã„â€˜Ã¡ÂºÂ¿n Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€˜i tÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£ng book bÃƒÂªn trong node kho sÃƒÂ¡ch
    book& target_book = book_node->get_Data();
    if (target_book.get_quantity() <= 0) return false; // HÃ¡ÂºÂ¿t sÃƒÂ¡ch trong kho

    // DÃƒâ„¢NG TOÃƒÂN TÃ¡Â»Â¬ -- Ã„ÂÃ¡Â»â€š TRÃ¡Â»Âª KHO TRÃ¡Â»Â°C TIÃ¡ÂºÂ¾P TRÃƒÅ N RAM
    --target_book;

    // TÃ¡ÂºÂ¡o phiÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u mÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£n mÃ¡Â»â€ºi vÃ¡Â»â€ºi trÃ¡ÂºÂ¡ng thÃƒÂ¡i ban Ã„â€˜Ã¡ÂºÂ§u lÃƒÂ  "Dang muon"
    borrow new_ticket(borrow_id, book_id, user_id, borrow_date, due_date, "Dang muon");

    borrow_list.push_back(new_ticket);
    borrow_index.insert(borrow_id, borrow_list.get_tail());

    // TÃ¡Â»Â± Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â„¢ng lÃ†Â°u ngay xuÃ¡Â»â€˜ng file Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»Æ’ Ã„â€˜Ã¡ÂºÂ£m bÃ¡ÂºÂ£o an toÃƒÂ n dÃ¡Â»Â¯ liÃ¡Â»â€¡u
    save_data();
    b_service.save_data();

    return true;
}

// --- 4. TRÃ¡ÂºÂ¢ SÃƒÂCH (DÃƒÂ¹ng toÃƒÂ¡n tÃ¡Â»Â­ ++) ---
bool borrow_service::return_book(std::string borrow_id, book_service& b_service) {
    node<borrow>* ticket_node = borrow_index.find(borrow_id);
    if (ticket_node == nullptr) return false;
    
    // LÃ¡ÂºÂ¥y tham chiÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u trÃ¡Â»Â±c tiÃ¡ÂºÂ¿p Ã„â€˜Ã¡ÂºÂ¿n Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€˜i tÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£ng borrow nÃ¡ÂºÂ±m bÃƒÂªn trong node
    borrow& ticket = ticket_node->get_Data();
    
    // NÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u sÃƒÂ¡ch Ã„â€˜ÃƒÂ£ trÃ¡ÂºÂ£ rÃ¡Â»â€œi thÃƒÂ¬ khÃƒÂ´ng xÃ¡Â»Â­ lÃƒÂ½ nÃ¡Â»Â¯a
    if (ticket.get_status() == "Da tra") return false;

    // CÃ¡ÂºÂ­p nhÃ¡ÂºÂ­t trÃ¡ÂºÂ¡ng thÃƒÂ¡i phiÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u thÃƒÂ nh "Da tra"
    ticket.set_status("Da tra");

    // HoÃƒÂ n lÃ¡ÂºÂ¡i sÃ¡Â»â€˜ lÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£ng sÃƒÂ¡ch vÃƒÂ o kho (+1)
    node<book>* book_node = b_service.get_book_by_id(ticket.get_book_id());
    if (book_node != nullptr) {
        // LÃ¡ÂºÂ¥y tham chiÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u trÃ¡Â»Â±c tiÃ¡ÂºÂ¿p Ã„â€˜Ã¡ÂºÂ¿n Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€˜i tÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£ng book trong node kho sÃƒÂ¡ch
        book& target_book = book_node->get_Data(); 
        
        // DÃƒâ„¢NG TOÃƒÂN TÃ¡Â»Â¬ ++ Ã„ÂÃ¡Â»â€š HOÃƒâ‚¬N KHO TRÃ¡Â»Â°C TIÃ¡ÂºÂ¾P TRÃƒÅ N RAM
        ++target_book; 
    }

    // TÃ¡Â»Â± Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â„¢ng lÃ†Â°u ngay xuÃ¡Â»â€˜ng file
    save_data();
    b_service.save_data();

    return true;
}

// --- 5. TÃ¡Â»Â° Ã„ÂÃ¡Â»ËœNG KIÃ¡Â»â€šM TRA QUÃƒÂ HÃ¡ÂºÂ N ---
void borrow_service::check_and_update_overdue(date current_date) {
    bool has_changed = false;
    node<borrow>* curr = borrow_list.get_head();
    while (curr != nullptr) {
        borrow& ticket = curr->get_Data();

        // NÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u phiÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u Ã„â€˜ang mÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£n vÃƒÂ  ngÃƒÂ y hiÃ¡Â»â€¡n tÃ¡ÂºÂ¡i vÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£t quÃƒÂ¡ ngÃƒÂ y hÃ¡ÂºÂ¹n trÃ¡ÂºÂ£ (dÃƒÂ¹ng toÃƒÂ¡n tÃ¡Â»Â­ > Ã„â€˜ÃƒÂ£ overload Ã¡Â»Å¸ class date)
        if (ticket.get_status() == "Dang muon" && current_date > ticket.get_due_date()) {
            ticket.set_status("Qua han");
            has_changed = true;
        }
        curr = curr->get_next();
    }
    if (has_changed) {
        save_data();
    }
}

// --- 7. TRA CÃ¡Â»Â¨U NHANH THEO ID ---
node<borrow>* borrow_service::get_borrow_by_id(std::string id) {
    return borrow_index.find(id);
}

// LÃ¡Â»Âc phiÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u mÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£n theo User
linked_list<borrow> borrow_service::get_borrows_by_user(std::string user_id, bool active_only) {
    linked_list<borrow> result;
    node<borrow>* curr = borrow_list.get_head();
    while (curr != nullptr) {
        borrow b = curr->get_data();
        if (b.get_user_id() == user_id) {
            // active_only = true: chÃ¡Â»â€° lÃ¡ÂºÂ¥y phiÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u chÃ†Â°a trÃ¡ÂºÂ£ (Dang muon hoÃ¡ÂºÂ·c Qua han)
            // active_only = false: lÃ¡ÂºÂ¥y toÃƒÂ n bÃ¡Â»â„¢ phiÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u cÃ¡Â»Â§a User, kÃ¡Â»Æ’ cÃ¡ÂºÂ£ Ã„â€˜ÃƒÂ£ trÃ¡ÂºÂ£
            if (!active_only || b.get_status() != "Da tra") {
                result.push_back(b);
            }
        }
        curr = curr->get_next();
    }
    return result;
}

// LÃ¡Â»Âc theo khoÃ¡ÂºÂ£ng ngÃƒÂ y mÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£n
linked_list<borrow> borrow_service::filter_borrows_by_date(date start_date, date end_date) {
    linked_list<borrow> result;
    node<borrow>* curr = borrow_list.get_head();

    while (curr != nullptr) {
        borrow b = curr->get_data();
        date borrow_date = b.get_borrow_date();

        if (borrow_date >= start_date && borrow_date <= end_date) {
            result.push_back(b);
        }

        curr = curr->get_next();
    }

    return result;
}

// --- XÃƒâ€œA PHIÃ¡ÂºÂ¾U MÃ†Â¯Ã¡Â»Â¢N ---
bool borrow_service::delete_borrow_ticket(std::string borrow_id) {
    // 1. TÃƒÂ¬m node phiÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u mÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£n qua bÃ¡ÂºÂ£ng bÃ„Æ’m O(1)
    node<borrow>* target = borrow_index.find(borrow_id);
    if (target == nullptr) {
        return false; // KhÃƒÂ´ng tÃƒÂ¬m thÃ¡ÂºÂ¥y mÃƒÂ£ phiÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u
    }

    // CHÃ¡Â»Ë† CHO PHÃƒâ€°P XÃƒâ€œA KHI Ã„ÂÃƒÆ’ TRÃ¡ÂºÂ¢ SÃƒÂCH XONG! ("Da tra")
    // TuyÃ¡Â»â€¡t Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€˜i khÃƒÂ´ng cho xÃƒÂ³a khi "Dang muon" hoÃ¡ÂºÂ·c "Qua han" Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»Æ’ trÃƒÂ¡nh mÃ¡ÂºÂ¥t sÃƒÂ¡ch
    if (target->get_Data().get_status() != "Da tra") {
        return false; 
    }

    // 2. XÃƒÂ³a chÃ¡Â»â€° mÃ¡Â»Â¥c trÃƒÂªn BÃ¡ÂºÂ£ng bÃ„Æ’m trÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»â€ºc
    borrow_index.remove(borrow_id);

    // 3. XÃƒÂ³a node khÃ¡Â»Âi Danh sÃƒÂ¡ch liÃƒÂªn kÃ¡ÂºÂ¿t kÃƒÂ©p
    borrow_list.remove_node(target);

    // TÃ¡Â»Â± Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â„¢ng lÃ†Â°u thay Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€¢i
    save_data();

    return true;
}

// --- KIÃ¡Â»â€šM TRA RÃƒâ‚¬NG BUÃ¡Â»ËœC TOÃƒâ‚¬N VÃ¡ÂºÂ¸N ---
bool borrow_service::is_book_borrowed(std::string book_id) const {
    node<borrow>* curr = borrow_list.get_head();
    while (curr != nullptr) {
        borrow b = curr->get_data();
        if (b.get_book_id() == book_id && b.get_status() != "Da tra") {
            return true; // SÃƒÂ¡ch Ã„â€˜ang cÃƒÂ³ ngÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Âi mÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£n hoÃ¡ÂºÂ·c quÃƒÂ¡ hÃ¡ÂºÂ¡n
        }
        curr = curr->get_next();
    }
    return false;
}

bool borrow_service::has_active_borrows(std::string user_id) const {
    node<borrow>* curr = borrow_list.get_head();
    while (curr != nullptr) {
        borrow b = curr->get_data();
        if (b.get_user_id() == user_id && b.get_status() != "Da tra") {
            return true; // NgÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Âi dÃƒÂ¹ng cÃƒÂ²n sÃƒÂ¡ch chÃ†Â°a trÃ¡ÂºÂ£
        }
        curr = curr->get_next();
    }
    return false;
}

bool borrow_service::is_user_borrowing_book(std::string user_id, std::string book_id) const {
    node<borrow>* curr = borrow_list.get_head();
    while (curr != nullptr) {
        borrow b = curr->get_data();
        if (b.get_user_id() == user_id && b.get_book_id() == book_id && b.get_status() != "Da tra") {
            return true; // Ã„Âang mÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£n cuÃ¡Â»â€˜n nÃƒÂ y rÃ¡Â»â€œi
        }
        curr = curr->get_next();
    }
    return false;
}

bool borrow_service::has_overdue_borrows(std::string user_id) const {
    node<borrow>* curr = borrow_list.get_head();
    while (curr != nullptr) {
        borrow b = curr->get_data();
        if (b.get_user_id() == user_id && b.get_status() == "Qua han") {
            return true; // Ã„Âang cÃƒÂ³ sÃƒÂ¡ch quÃƒÂ¡ hÃ¡ÂºÂ¡n chÃ†Â°a trÃ¡ÂºÂ£
        }
        curr = curr->get_next();
    }
    return false;
}
int borrow_service::count_active_borrows_by_user(std::string user_id) const {
    int count = 0;
    node<borrow>* curr = borrow_list.get_head();
    while (curr != nullptr) {
        borrow b = curr->get_data();
        if (b.get_user_id() == user_id && b.get_status() != "Da tra") {
            count++;
        }
        curr = curr->get_next();
    }
    return count;
}

int borrow_service::count_borrows_by_book(std::string book_id) const {
    int count = 0;
    node<borrow>* curr = borrow_list.get_head();
    while (curr != nullptr) {
        if (curr->get_data().get_book_id() == book_id) {
            count++;
        }
        curr = curr->get_next();
    }
    return count;
}

class borrow_service {

    void parse_line(std::string line, borrow& b);

    bool create_borrow_ticket(std::string borrow_id, std::string book_id, std::string user_id, date borrow_date, date due_date, book_service& b_service, user_service& u_service);

=== FILE: .\Service\file_helper.h ===

#pragma once
#include <fstream>
#include <string>
#include "../Structure/linked_list.h"

class file_helper {
public:
    // Ã„ÂÃ†Â¡n nhiÃ¡Â»â€¡m GHI: ChÃ¡Â»â€° lÃƒÂ m duy nhÃ¡ÂºÂ¥t 1 viÃ¡Â»â€¡c lÃƒÂ  ghi danh sÃƒÂ¡ch ra file
    template <typename T>
    static bool save_list_to_file(const std::string& filename, const linked_list<T>& list) {
        std::ofstream file(filename, std::ios::trunc);
        if (!file.is_open()) return false;

        node<T>* curr = list.get_head();
        while (curr != nullptr) {
            // GÃ¡Â»Âi hÃƒÂ m to_record_string() cÃ¡Â»Â§a chÃƒÂ­nh thÃ¡Â»Â±c thÃ¡Â»Æ’ (KÃ¡ÂºÂ¿ thÃ¡Â»Â«a tÃ¡Â»Â« library_entity)
            file << curr->get_data().to_record_string() << "\n";
            curr = curr->get_next();
        }
        file.close();
        return true;
    }
};




=== FILE: .\Service\shift_service.cpp ===

#include<iostream>
#include "shift_service.h"
#include "../Algorithm/search_algo.h"
#include"../Model/date.h"
shift_service::shift_service(std::string filename) {
    data_file = filename;
    load_data();
}

shift_service::~shift_service() {
    save_data(); // TÃ¡Â»Â± Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â„¢ng lÃ†Â°u file khi tÃ¡ÂºÂ¯t chÃ†Â°Ã†Â¡ng trÃƒÂ¬nh
}

// --- TÃ¡ÂºÂ¡o khÃƒÂ³a Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€¹nh danh cho Hash Table (VD: 01/10/2026_sang) ---
std::string shift_service::generate_key(date d, std::string session) const {
    std::stringstream ss;
    ss << d.get_day() << "/" << d.get_month() << "/" << d.get_year() << "_" << session;
    return ss.str();
}

// --- TÃƒÂ¡ch dÃƒÂ²ng tÃ¡Â»Â« shift.txt (Ã„ÂÃ¡Â»â€¹nh dÃ¡ÂºÂ¡ng: 01/10/2026;sang;AD01,AD02) ---
// --- TÃƒÂ¡ch dÃƒÂ²ng an toÃƒÂ n tÃ¡Â»Â« shift.txt (Ã„ÂÃ¡Â»â€¹nh dÃ¡ÂºÂ¡ng: DD/MM/YYYY;ca;AD01,AD02) ---
void shift_service::parse_line(std::string line, shift& s) {
    // 1. DÃ¡Â»Ân sÃ¡ÂºÂ¡ch kÃƒÂ½ tÃ¡Â»Â± xuÃ¡Â»â€˜ng dÃƒÂ²ng Windows \r\n hoÃ¡ÂºÂ·c khoÃ¡ÂºÂ£ng trÃ¡ÂºÂ¯ng 2 Ã„â€˜Ã¡ÂºÂ§u
    line = search_algo::trim(line);
    if (line.empty()) return;

    std::stringstream ss(line);
    std::string date_str, session, admins_str;

    if (!std::getline(ss, date_str, ';')) return;
    if (!std::getline(ss, session, ';')) return;
    std::getline(ss, admins_str, ';');

    date_str = search_algo::trim(date_str);
    session = search_algo::to_lower(search_algo::trim(session));

    // 2. KhÃ¡Â»Å¸i tÃ¡ÂºÂ¡o giÃƒÂ¡ trÃ¡Â»â€¹ mÃ¡ÂºÂ·c Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€¹nh Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»Æ’ trÃƒÂ¡nh sÃ¡Â»â€˜ rÃƒÂ¡c tÃ¡Â»Â« bÃ¡Â»â„¢ nhÃ¡Â»â€º RAM
    int d = 1, m = 1, y = 2026;
    char slash1 = '/', slash2 = '/';
    std::stringstream ss_d(date_str);
    if (!(ss_d >> d >> slash1 >> m >> slash2 >> y)) {
        d = 1; m = 1; y = 2026;
    }
    date shift_date(d, m, y);

    s = shift(shift_date, session);

    // 3. TÃƒÂ¡ch danh sÃƒÂ¡ch admin, lÃ¡Â»Âc bÃ¡Â»Â hoÃƒÂ n toÃƒÂ n chuÃ¡Â»â€”i rÃ¡Â»â€”ng vÃƒÂ  dÃ¡ÂºÂ¥u phÃ¡ÂºÂ©y thÃ¡Â»Â«a
    std::stringstream ss_adm(admins_str);
    std::string admin_id;
    while (std::getline(ss_adm, admin_id, ',')) {
        admin_id = search_algo::trim(admin_id);
        if (!admin_id.empty() && s.get_current_count() < 4) {
            s.add_admin(admin_id); // Ã„ÂÃ¡ÂºÂ£m bÃ¡ÂºÂ£o chÃ¡Â»â€° nhÃ¡ÂºÂ­n mÃƒÂ£ admin chuÃ¡ÂºÂ©n, tÃ¡Â»â€˜i Ã„â€˜a 4 ngÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Âi
        }
    }
}

// --- 1. Ã„ÂÃ¡Â»Å’C DÃ¡Â»Â® LIÃ¡Â»â€ U TÃ¡Â»Âª FILE ---
bool shift_service::load_data() {
    std::ifstream file(data_file);
    if (!file.is_open()) return false;

    std::string line;
    while (std::getline(file, line)) {
        if (line.empty()) continue;
        
        shift s;
        parse_line(line, s);
        
        shift_list.push_back(s);
        node<shift>* new_node = shift_list.get_tail(); 
        
        // Ã„ÂÃ„Æ’ng kÃƒÂ½ vÃƒÂ o bÃ¡ÂºÂ£ng bÃ„Æ’m vÃ¡Â»â€ºi khÃƒÂ³a ngÃƒÂ y_ca
        std::string key = generate_key(s.get_shift_date(), s.get_session());
        shift_index.insert(key, new_node);
    }
    file.close();
    return true;
}

// --- 2. GHI DÃ¡Â»Â® LIÃ¡Â»â€ U XUÃ¡Â»ÂNG FILE ---
bool shift_service::save_data() {
    return file_helper::save_list_to_file(data_file, shift_list);
}
// --- 3. Ã„ÂÃ„â€šNG KÃƒÂ CA TRÃ¡Â»Â°C CHO ADMIN ---
bool shift_service::register_shift(date shift_date, std::string session, std::string admin_id, user_service& u_service) {
    // KiÃ¡Â»Æ’m tra xem user cÃƒÂ³ tÃ¡Â»â€œn tÃ¡ÂºÂ¡i vÃƒÂ  cÃƒÂ³ phÃ¡ÂºÂ£i lÃƒÂ  admin/manager khÃƒÂ´ng
    node<user>* user_node = u_service.get_user_by_id(admin_id);
    if (user_node == nullptr) return false;
    
    std::string role = user_node->get_data().get_role();
    if (role != "admin" && role != "manager") return false;

    std::string key = generate_key(shift_date, session);
    node<shift>* shift_node = shift_index.find(key);

    if (shift_node == nullptr) {
        // NÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u ca trÃ¡Â»Â±c chÃ†Â°a tÃ¡Â»â€œn tÃ¡ÂºÂ¡i trong ngÃƒÂ y hÃƒÂ´m Ã„â€˜ÃƒÂ³ -> TÃ¡ÂºÂ¡o mÃ¡Â»â€ºi
        shift new_shift(shift_date, session);
        new_shift.add_admin(admin_id);

        shift_list.push_back(new_shift);
        shift_index.insert(key, shift_list.get_tail());

        save_data(); // LÃ†Â°u ngay xuÃ¡Â»â€˜ng file
        return true;
    } else {
        // NÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u ca Ã„â€˜ÃƒÂ£ tÃ¡Â»â€œn tÃ¡ÂºÂ¡i -> LÃ¡ÂºÂ¥y ra cÃ¡ÂºÂ­p nhÃ¡ÂºÂ­t
        shift target_shift = shift_node->get_data();
        
        if (!target_shift.add_admin(admin_id)) {
            return false; // ThÃ¡ÂºÂ¥t bÃ¡ÂºÂ¡i (do Ã„â€˜Ã¡ÂºÂ§y 4 ngÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Âi hoÃ¡ÂºÂ·c Ã„â€˜ÃƒÂ£ Ã„â€˜Ã„Æ’ng kÃƒÂ½ rÃ¡Â»â€œi)
        }

        shift_node->set_data(target_shift);
        save_data(); // LÃ†Â°u ngay xuÃ¡Â»â€˜ng file
        return true;
    }
}

// --- 4. HÃ¡Â»Â¦Y Ã„ÂÃ„â€šNG KÃƒÂ CA TRÃ¡Â»Â°C ---
bool shift_service::cancel_shift(date shift_date, std::string session, std::string admin_id) {
    std::string key = generate_key(shift_date, session);
    node<shift>* shift_node = shift_index.find(key);
    if (shift_node == nullptr) return false;

    shift target_shift = shift_node->get_data();
    if (!target_shift.remove_admin(admin_id)) {
        return false; // KhÃƒÂ´ng tÃƒÂ¬m thÃ¡ÂºÂ¥y admin nÃƒÂ y trong ca
    }

    // NÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u ca trÃ¡Â»Â±c trÃ¡Â»â€˜ng sÃ¡ÂºÂ¡ch bÃƒÂ³ng admin -> XÃƒÂ³a luÃƒÂ´n ca trÃ¡Â»Â±c Ã„â€˜ÃƒÂ³ khÃ¡Â»Âi danh sÃƒÂ¡ch
    if (target_shift.get_current_count() == 0) {
        shift_index.remove(key);
        shift_list.remove_node(shift_node);
    } else {
        shift_node->set_data(target_shift);
    }

    save_data(); // LÃ†Â°u ngay xuÃ¡Â»â€˜ng file
    return true;
}

// --- 5. TRA CÃ¡Â»Â¨U CA TRÃ¡Â»Â°C ---
node<shift>* shift_service::get_shift(date shift_date, std::string session) {
    std::string key = generate_key(shift_date, session);
    return shift_index.find(key);
}

// --- 6. XÃƒâ€œA TOÃƒâ‚¬N BÃ¡Â»Ëœ CA TRÃ¡Â»Â°C (DÃƒÂ nh cho Manager hÃ¡Â»Â§y lÃ¡Â»â€¹ch trÃ¡Â»Â±c ngÃƒÂ y lÃ¡Â»â€¦/Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â„¢t xuÃ¡ÂºÂ¥t) ---
bool shift_service::delete_entire_shift(date shift_date, std::string session) {
    std::string key = generate_key(shift_date, session);
    node<shift>* target = shift_index.find(key);
    
    if (target == nullptr) return false; // KhÃƒÂ´ng tÃ¡Â»â€œn tÃ¡ÂºÂ¡i ca trÃ¡Â»Â±c nÃƒÂ y

    // XÃƒÂ³a khÃ¡Â»Âi bÃ¡ÂºÂ£ng bÃ„Æ’m vÃƒÂ  danh sÃƒÂ¡ch liÃƒÂªn kÃ¡ÂºÂ¿t
    shift_index.remove(key);
    shift_list.remove_node(target);

    save_data(); // LÃ†Â°u ngay xuÃ¡Â»â€˜ng file
    return true;
}

// --- 7. Ã„ÂÃ¡ÂºÂ¾M SÃ¡Â»Â BUÃ¡Â»â€I TRÃ¡Â»Â°C CÃ¡Â»Â¦A ADMIN (HÃ¡Â»â€” trÃ¡Â»Â£ lÃ¡Â»Âc theo thÃƒÂ¡ng/nÃ„Æ’m, mÃ¡ÂºÂ·c Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€¹nh 0 lÃƒÂ  tÃƒÂ­nh tÃ¡ÂºÂ¥t cÃ¡ÂºÂ£) ---
int shift_service::count_shifts_by_admin(std::string admin_id, int month, int year) const {
    int count = 0;
    node<shift>* curr = shift_list.get_head();

    while (curr != nullptr) {
        shift s = curr->get_data();
        date d = s.get_shift_date();

        // KiÃ¡Â»Æ’m tra khÃ¡Â»â€ºp thÃƒÂ¡ng vÃƒÂ  nÃ„Æ’m (nÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u ngÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Âi dÃƒÂ¹ng truyÃ¡Â»Ân vÃƒÂ o > 0)
        bool match_time = true;
        if (month > 0 && d.get_month() != month) match_time = false;
        if (year > 0 && d.get_year() != year) match_time = false;

        // NÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u khÃ¡Â»â€ºp thÃ¡Â»Âi gian vÃƒÂ  cÃƒÂ³ mÃ¡ÂºÂ·t admin trong ca Ã„â€˜ÃƒÂ³ (dÃƒÂ¹ng hÃƒÂ m has_admin cÃ¡Â»Â§a class shift)
        if (match_time && s.has_admin(admin_id)) {
            count++;
        }
        curr = curr->get_next();
    }
    return count;
}

// --- 8. TÃƒÂNH LÃ†Â¯Ã†Â NG CA TRÃ¡Â»Â°C (MÃ¡ÂºÂ·c Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€¹nh: 200.000 VNÃ„Â / 1 buÃ¡Â»â€¢i) ---
long long shift_service::calculate_salary(std::string admin_id, int month, int year, long long rate_per_shift) const {
    int total_shifts = count_shifts_by_admin(admin_id, month, year);
    return total_shifts * rate_per_shift;
}

// --- 9. LÃ¡ÂºÂ¤Y DANH SÃƒÂCH LÃ¡Â»Å CH TRÃ¡Â»Â°C CÃ¡Â»Â¦A RIÃƒÅ NG 1 ADMIN ---
linked_list<shift> shift_service::get_shifts_by_admin(std::string admin_id) const {
    linked_list<shift> result;
    node<shift>* curr = shift_list.get_head();

    while (curr != nullptr) {
        shift s = curr->get_data();
        if (s.has_admin(admin_id)) {
            result.push_back(s);
        }
        curr = curr->get_next();
    }
    return result;
}

// --- 10. KIÃ¡Â»â€šM TRA ADMIN CÃƒâ€œ LÃ¡Â»Å CH TRÃ¡Â»Â°C KHÃƒâ€NG ---
bool shift_service::has_shifts(std::string admin_id) const {
    node<shift>* curr = shift_list.get_head();
    while (curr != nullptr) {
        if (curr->get_data().has_admin(admin_id)) {
            return true;
        }
        curr = curr->get_next();
    }
    return false;
}

// --- 11. HIÃ¡Â»â€šN THÃ¡Â»Å  TOÃƒâ‚¬N BÃ¡Â»Ëœ LÃ¡Â»Å CH TRÃ¡Â»Â°C ---
void shift_service::display_all() {
    node<shift>* curr = shift_list.get_head();
    if (curr == nullptr) {
        std::cout << "Hien chua co lich truc nao!\n";
        return;
    }

    std::cout << "\n================ DANH SACH CA TRUC ================\n";
    while (curr != nullptr) {
        shift s = curr->get_data();
        date d = s.get_shift_date();
        std::cout << "- Ngay: " << d << " (" << d.get_day_of_week() << ")"
                  << " | Ca: " << s.get_session()
                  << " | So luong: " << s.get_current_count() << "/4"
                  << " | Admin: " << s.get_all_admins_string() << "\n";
        curr = curr->get_next();
    }
    std::cout << "===================================================\n";
}

linked_list<shift> shift_service::get_all_shifts() const {
    return shift_list;
}

=== FILE: .\Service\user_service.cpp ===

#include "user_service.h"

user_service::user_service(std::string filename) {
    data_file = filename;
    load_data();
}

user_service::~user_service() {
    save_data();
}

// --- TÃƒÂ¡ch dÃƒÂ²ng tÃ¡Â»Â« user.txt (Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€¹nh dÃ¡ÂºÂ¡ng: ID;Name;Password;Role) ---
void user_service::parse_line(std::string line, user& u) {
    if (!line.empty() && line.back() == '\r') {
        line.pop_back();
    }
    std::stringstream ss(line);
    std::string id, name, password, role;

    std::getline(ss, id, ';');
    std::getline(ss, name, ';');
    std::getline(ss, password, ';');
    std::getline(ss, role, ';');

    u = user(id, name, password, role);
}

// --- 1. Ã„ÂÃ¡Â»Å’C DÃ¡Â»Â® LIÃ¡Â»â€ U TÃ¡Â»Âª FILE ---
bool user_service::load_data() {
    std::ifstream file(data_file);
    if (!file.is_open()) return false;

    std::string line;
    while (std::getline(file, line)) {
        if (line.empty()) continue;
        
        user u;
        parse_line(line, u);
        
        user_list.push_back(u);
        node<user>* new_node = user_list.get_tail(); 
        user_index.insert(u.get_id(), new_node); // Ã„ÂÃ„Æ’ng kÃƒÂ½ bÃ¡ÂºÂ£ng bÃ„Æ’m O(1)
    }
    file.close();
    return true;
}

// --- 2. GHI DÃ¡Â»Â® LIÃ¡Â»â€ U XUÃ¡Â»ÂNG FILE ---
bool user_service::save_data() {
    return file_helper::save_list_to_file(data_file, user_list);
}

// --- 3. XÃƒÂC THÃ¡Â»Â°C Ã„ÂÃ„â€šNG NHÃ¡ÂºÂ¬P ---
user* user_service::login(std::string id, std::string password) {
    node<user>* target = user_index.find(id);

    if (target == nullptr)
        return nullptr;

    if (target->get_Data().get_password() == password)
        return &target->get_Data();

    return nullptr; // sai mk
}

// --- KiÃ¡Â»Æ’m tra phÃƒÂ¢n quyÃ¡Â»Ân (HÃ¡Â»â€” trÃ¡Â»Â£ Manager > Admin > User) ---
bool user_service::check_permission_level(user* u, std::string required_role) {
    if (u == nullptr) return false;
    
    std::string role = u->get_role();
    if (role == "manager") return true; // Manager cÃƒÂ³ quyÃ¡Â»Ân tÃ¡Â»â€˜i cao
    if (role == "admin" && (required_role == "admin" || required_role == "user")) return true;
    if (role == "user" && required_role == "user") return true;

    return false;
}

// --- 4. THÃƒÅ M NGÃ†Â¯Ã¡Â»Å“I DÃƒâ„¢NG MÃ¡Â»Å¡I ---
bool user_service::add_user(user new_user) {
    if (user_index.find(new_user.get_id()) != nullptr) {
        return false; // TrÃƒÂ¹ng ID tÃƒÂ i khoÃ¡ÂºÂ£n
    }

    user_list.push_back(new_user);
    user_index.insert(new_user.get_id(), user_list.get_tail());

    save_data(); // LÃ†Â°u ngay xuÃ¡Â»â€˜ng file
    return true;
}

// --- 5. XÃƒâ€œA NGÃ†Â¯Ã¡Â»Å“I DÃƒâ„¢NG ---
bool user_service::delete_user(std::string id) {
    node<user>* target = user_index.find(id);
    if (target == nullptr) return false;

    user_index.remove(id);
    user_list.remove_node(target); 

    save_data(); // LÃ†Â°u ngay xuÃ¡Â»â€˜ng file
    return true;
}

// --- 6. CÃ¡ÂºÂ¬P NHÃ¡ÂºÂ¬T THÃƒâ€NG TIN NGÃ†Â¯Ã¡Â»Å“I DÃƒâ„¢NG ---
bool user_service::update_user(std::string old_id, user new_data) {
    node<user>* target = user_index.find(old_id);
    if (target == nullptr) return false;

    std::string new_id = new_data.get_id();

    if (old_id != new_id) {
        if (user_index.find(new_id) != nullptr) return false; 
        
        user_index.remove(old_id);             
        target->set_data(new_data);            
        user_index.insert(new_id, target);     
    } else {
        target->set_data(new_data);
    }

    save_data(); // LÃ†Â°u ngay xuÃ¡Â»â€˜ng file
    return true;
}

// --- 7. TRA CÃ¡Â»Â¨U NHANH THEO ID ---
node<user>* user_service::get_user_by_id(std::string id) {
    return user_index.find(id);
}

=== FILE: .\Structure\graph.cpp ===

#include "graph.h"

template <typename t>
graph<t>::graph(int cap) {
    this->capacity = cap;
    this->num_vertices = 0;
    
    // KhÃ¡Â»Å¸i tÃ¡ÂºÂ¡o mÃ¡ÂºÂ£ng Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â„¢ng chÃ¡Â»Â©a dÃ¡Â»Â¯ liÃ¡Â»â€¡u Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€°nh
    this->vertices = new t[capacity];
    
    // KhÃ¡Â»Å¸i tÃ¡ÂºÂ¡o mÃ¡ÂºÂ£ng Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â„¢ng chÃ¡Â»Â©a cÃƒÂ¡c danh sÃƒÂ¡ch liÃƒÂªn kÃ¡ÂºÂ¿t
    this->adj_list = new linked_list<int>[capacity]; 
}

template <typename t>
graph<t>::~graph() {
    clear();
}

template <typename t>
bool graph<t>::add_vertex(t data) {
    if (num_vertices >= capacity) return false; // Ã„ÂÃ¡Â»â€œ thÃ¡Â»â€¹ Ã„â€˜ÃƒÂ£ Ã„â€˜Ã¡ÂºÂ§y
    
    vertices[num_vertices] = data;
    num_vertices++;
    return true;
}

template <typename t>
bool graph<t>::add_edge(int src_index, int dest_index, bool is_directed) {
    // KiÃ¡Â»Æ’m tra tÃƒÂ­nh hÃ¡Â»Â£p lÃ¡Â»â€¡ cÃ¡Â»Â§a index
    if (src_index < 0 || src_index >= num_vertices || 
        dest_index < 0 || dest_index >= num_vertices) {
        return false;
    }
    
    // DÃƒÂ¹ng hÃƒÂ m push_back cÃ¡Â»Â§a linked_list Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»Æ’ thÃƒÂªm cÃ¡ÂºÂ¡nh nÃ¡Â»â€˜i
    adj_list[src_index].push_back(dest_index);
    
    // NÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u lÃƒÂ  Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€œ thÃ¡Â»â€¹ vÃƒÂ´ hÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»â€ºng (mÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£n A cÃƒÂ³ B, thÃƒÂ¬ mÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£n B cÃ…Â©ng cÃƒÂ³ A)
    if (!is_directed) {
        adj_list[dest_index].push_back(src_index);
    }
    
    return true;
}

template <typename t>
int graph<t>::get_num_vertices() const {
    return num_vertices;
}

template <typename t>
t graph<t>::get_vertex_data(int index) const {
    return vertices[index];
}

template <typename t>
linked_list<int>& graph<t>::get_adj_list(int index) const {
    return adj_list[index];
}

template <typename t>
linked_list<t> graph<t>::get_neighbors(t vertex_data) const {
    linked_list<t> neighbors;
    int src_index = -1;
    
    // TÃƒÂ¬m vÃ¡Â»â€¹ trÃƒÂ­ cÃ¡Â»Â§a Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€°nh trong mÃ¡ÂºÂ£ng vertices
    for (int i = 0; i < num_vertices; i++) {
        if (vertices[i] == vertex_data) {
            src_index = i;
            break;
        }
    }
    // LÃ¡ÂºÂ¥y toÃƒÂ n bÃ¡Â»â„¢ dÃ¡Â»Â¯ liÃ¡Â»â€¡u Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€°nh kÃ¡Â»Â Ã„â€˜Ã¡ÂºÂ©y vÃƒÂ o danh sÃƒÂ¡ch kÃ¡ÂºÂ¿t quÃ¡ÂºÂ£
    if (src_index != -1) {
        node<int>* curr = adj_list[src_index].get_head();
        while (curr != nullptr) {
            int neighbor_idx = curr->get_data();
            neighbors.push_back(vertices[neighbor_idx]);
            curr = curr->get_next();
        }
    }
    return neighbors;
}

template <typename t>
void graph<t>::clear() {
    if (vertices != nullptr) {
        delete[] vertices;
        vertices = nullptr;
    }
    if (adj_list != nullptr) {
        delete[] adj_list; // GÃ¡Â»Âi destructor cÃ¡Â»Â§a linked_list Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»Æ’ giÃ¡ÂºÂ£i phÃƒÂ³ng tÃ¡Â»Â«ng node
        adj_list = nullptr;
    }
    num_vertices = 0;
    capacity = 0;
}




=== FILE: .\Structure\graph.h ===

#pragma once
#include "linked_list.h" // TÃ¡ÂºÂ­n dÃ¡Â»Â¥ng lÃ¡ÂºÂ¡i chÃƒÂ­nh DSLK Ã„â€˜ÃƒÂ´i bÃ¡ÂºÂ¡n Ã„â€˜ÃƒÂ£ code

template <typename t>
class graph {
private:
    int capacity;                 // SÃ¡Â»Â©c chÃ¡Â»Â©a tÃ¡Â»â€˜i Ã„â€˜a cÃ¡Â»Â§a Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€œ thÃ¡Â»â€¹
    int num_vertices;             // SÃ¡Â»â€˜ lÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£ng Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€°nh hiÃ¡Â»â€¡n tÃ¡ÂºÂ¡i
    t* vertices;                  // MÃ¡ÂºÂ£ng lÃ†Â°u dÃ¡Â»Â¯ liÃ¡Â»â€¡u cÃƒÂ¡c Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€°nh (vd: mÃƒÂ£ sÃƒÂ¡ch, Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€˜i tÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£ng book)
    linked_list<int>* adj_list;   // MÃ¡ÂºÂ£ng cÃƒÂ¡c DSLK, mÃ¡Â»â€”i DSLK lÃ†Â°u chÃ¡Â»â€° sÃ¡Â»â€˜ (index) cÃ¡Â»Â§a cÃƒÂ¡c Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€°nh kÃ¡Â»Â

public:
    graph(int cap = 100);
    ~graph();

    // Thao tÃƒÂ¡c xÃƒÂ¢y dÃ¡Â»Â±ng Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€œ thÃ¡Â»â€¹
    bool add_vertex(t data);
    bool add_edge(int src_index, int dest_index, bool is_directed = false);

    // Truy xuÃ¡ÂºÂ¥t thÃƒÂ´ng tin
    int get_num_vertices() const;
    t get_vertex_data(int index) const;
    
   // LÃ¡ÂºÂ¥y ra danh sÃƒÂ¡ch cÃƒÂ¡c Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€°nh kÃ¡Â»Â cÃ¡Â»Â§a mÃ¡Â»â„¢t Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€°nh
    linked_list<int>& get_adj_list(int index) const; 

    // CHÃƒË†N VÃƒâ‚¬O Ã„ÂÃƒâ€šY:
    linked_list<t> get_neighbors(t vertex_data) const;

    void clear();
};

#include "graph.cpp"




=== FILE: .\Structure\hash_table.cpp ===

#include"hash_table.h"

// --- KhÃ¡Â»Å¸i tÃ¡ÂºÂ¡o Node phÃ¡Â»Â¥ ---
template <typename t>
hash_table<t>::hash_node::hash_node(std::string k, node<t>* addr) {
    key = k;
    address = addr;
    next = nullptr;
}

// --- KhÃ¡Â»Å¸i tÃ¡ÂºÂ¡o BÃ¡ÂºÂ£ng bÃ„Æ’m ---
template <typename t>
hash_table<t>::hash_table(int cap) {
    capacity = cap;
    current_size = 0;
    table = new hash_node*[capacity];
    for (int i = 0; i < capacity; i++) table[i] = nullptr;
}

// --- HÃ¡Â»Â§y BÃ¡ÂºÂ£ng bÃ„Æ’m ---
template <typename t>
hash_table<t>::~hash_table() {
    for (int i = 0; i < capacity; i++) {
        hash_node* curr = table[i];
        while (curr != nullptr) {
            hash_node* temp = curr;
            curr = curr->next;
            delete temp;
        }
    }
    delete[] table;
}

// --- ThuÃ¡ÂºÂ­t toÃƒÂ¡n BÃ„Æ’m ---
template <typename t>
int hash_table<t>::hash_function(std::string key) const {
    long long hash_val = 0;
    long long p = 31;
    long long p_pow = 1;
    
    for (unsigned char c : key) {
        hash_val = (hash_val + c * p_pow) % capacity;
        p_pow = (p_pow * p) % capacity;
    }
    return (int)((hash_val % capacity + capacity) % capacity);
}

// --- ThuÃ¡ÂºÂ­t toÃƒÂ¡n GiÃƒÂ£n nÃ¡Â»Å¸ tÃ¡Â»Â± Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â„¢ng ---
template <typename t>
void hash_table<t>::rehash() {
    int old_capacity = capacity;
    hash_node** old_table = table;

    capacity *= 2;
    table = new hash_node*[capacity];
    for (int i = 0; i < capacity; i++) table[i] = nullptr;
    
    current_size = 0;

    for (int i = 0; i < old_capacity; i++) {
        hash_node* curr = old_table[i];
        while (curr != nullptr) {
            insert(curr->key, curr->address); 
            hash_node* temp = curr;
            curr = curr->next;
            delete temp;
        }
    }
    delete[] old_table;
}

// --- ThÃƒÂªm Node ---
template <typename t>
void hash_table<t>::insert(std::string key, node<t>* address) {
    if ((float)(current_size + 1) / capacity > MAX_LOAD_FACTOR) {
        rehash();
    }

    int index = hash_function(key);
    
    hash_node* new_node = new hash_node(key, address);
    new_node->next = table[index];
    table[index] = new_node;
    
    current_size++;
}

// --- TÃƒÂ¬m kiÃ¡ÂºÂ¿m Node ---
template <typename t>
node<t>* hash_table<t>::find(std::string key) const {
    int index = hash_function(key);
    hash_node* curr = table[index];
    
    while (curr != nullptr) {
        if (curr->key == key) {
            return curr->address;
        }
        curr = curr->next;
    }
    return nullptr;
}

// --- XÃƒÂ³a Node ---
template <typename t>
void hash_table<t>::remove(std::string key) {
    int index = hash_function(key);
    hash_node* curr = table[index];
    hash_node* prev = nullptr;

    while (curr != nullptr) {
        if (curr->key == key) {
            if (prev == nullptr) {
                table[index] = curr->next;
            } else {
                prev->next = curr->next;
            }
            delete curr;
            current_size--;
            return;
        }
        prev = curr;
        curr = curr->next;
    }
}




=== FILE: .\Structure\hash_table.h ===

#pragma once
#include <string>
#include "node.h"

template <typename t>
class hash_table {
private:
    // Khai bÃƒÂ¡o cÃ¡ÂºÂ¥u trÃƒÂºc node phÃ¡Â»Â¥ xÃ¡Â»Â­ lÃƒÂ½ Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»Â¥ng Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â„¢
    struct hash_node {
        std::string key;
        node<t>* address;
        hash_node* next;
        
        hash_node(std::string k, node<t>* addr);
    };

    hash_node** table;
    int capacity;
    int current_size;
    const float MAX_LOAD_FACTOR = 0.75;

    int hash_function(std::string key) const;
    void rehash();

public:
    hash_table(int cap = 101);
    ~hash_table();

    void insert(std::string key, node<t>* address);
    node<t>* find(std::string key) const;
    void remove(std::string key);
};

#include "hash_table.cpp"




=== FILE: .\Structure\linked_list.cpp ===

#include "linked_list.h"

template <typename t>
linked_list<t>::linked_list() {
    head = nullptr;
    tail = nullptr;
    size = 0;
}

template <typename t>
linked_list<t>::linked_list(const linked_list<t>& other) {
    head = nullptr;
    tail = nullptr;
    size = 0;

    node<t>* curr = other.head;

    while (curr != nullptr) {
        push_back(curr->get_data());
        curr = curr->get_next();
    }
}

template <typename t>
linked_list<t>& linked_list<t>::operator=(const linked_list<t>& other) {
    if (this == &other) {
        return *this;
    }

    clear();

    node<t>* curr = other.head;

    while (curr != nullptr) {
        push_back(curr->get_data());
        curr = curr->get_next();
    }

    return *this;
}

template <typename t>
linked_list<t>::~linked_list() {
    clear();
}

template <typename t>
bool linked_list<t>::empty() const {
    return head == nullptr;
}

template <typename t>
int linked_list<t>::get_size() const {
    return size;
}

template <typename t>
node<t>* linked_list<t>::get_head() const {
    return head;
}

template <typename t>
node<t>* linked_list<t>::get_tail() const {
    return tail;
}

template <typename t>
void linked_list<t>::push_front(t data) {
    node<t>* new_node = new node<t>(data);

    if (empty()) {
        head = new_node;
        tail = new_node;
    }
    else {
        new_node->set_next(head);
        head->set_prev(new_node);
        head = new_node;
    }

    size++;
}

template <typename t>
void linked_list<t>::push_back(t data) {
    node<t>* new_node = new node<t>(data);

    if (empty()) {
        head = new_node;
        tail = new_node;
    }
    else {
        new_node->set_prev(tail);
        tail->set_next(new_node);
        tail = new_node;
    }

    size++;
}

template <typename t>
void linked_list<t>::pop_front() {
    if (empty())
        return;

    node<t>* temp = head;

    if (head == tail) {
        head = nullptr;
        tail = nullptr;
    }
    else {
        head = head->get_next();
        head->set_prev(nullptr);
    }

    delete temp;
    size--;
}

template <typename t>
void linked_list<t>::pop_back() {
    if (empty())
        return;

    node<t>* temp = tail;

    if (head == tail) {
        head = nullptr;
        tail = nullptr;
    }
    else {
        tail = tail->get_prev();
        tail->set_next(nullptr);
    }

    delete temp;
    size--;
}

template <typename t>
void linked_list<t>::insert(int position, t data) {
    if (position < 0 || position > size)
        return;

    if (position == 0) {
        push_front(data);
        return;
    }

    if (position == size) {
        push_back(data);
        return;
    }

    node<t>* current = head;

    for (int i = 0; i < position; i++)
        current = current->get_next();

    node<t>* new_node = new node<t>(data);

    new_node->set_prev(current->get_prev());
    new_node->set_next(current);

    current->get_prev()->set_next(new_node);
    current->set_prev(new_node);

    size++;
}

template <typename t>
void linked_list<t>::remove(int position) {
    if (position < 0 || position >= size)
        return;

    if (position == 0) {
        pop_front();
        return;
    }

    if (position == size - 1) {
        pop_back();
        return;
    }

    node<t>* current = head;

    for (int i = 0; i < position; i++)
        current = current->get_next();

    current->get_prev()->set_next(current->get_next());
    current->get_next()->set_prev(current->get_prev());

    delete current;
    size--;
}

template <typename t>
void linked_list<t>::clear() {
    while (!empty())
        pop_front();
}
// --- CÃ¡ÂºÂ­p nhÃ¡ÂºÂ­t lÃ¡ÂºÂ¡i head, tail vÃƒÂ  size sau khi sÃ¡ÂºÂ¯p xÃ¡ÂºÂ¿p (Merge Sort) ---

template <typename t>
void linked_list<t>::update_after_sort(node<t>* new_head) {
    head = new_head;
    if (head == nullptr) {
        tail = nullptr;
        size = 0;
        return;
    }
    head->set_prev(nullptr);
    node<t>* curr = head;
    node<t>* prev = nullptr;
    int count = 0;
    while (curr != nullptr) {
        curr->set_prev(prev);
        prev = curr;
        curr = curr->get_next();
        count++;
    }
    tail = prev;
    tail->set_next(nullptr);
    size = count;
}


// --- XÃƒÂ³a mÃ¡Â»â„¢t node bÃ¡ÂºÂ¥t kÃ¡Â»Â³ khi Ã„â€˜ÃƒÂ£ cÃƒÂ³ Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€¹a chÃ¡Â»â€° Ã„â€˜ÃƒÂ­ch ---
template <typename t>
void linked_list<t>::remove_node(node<t>* target) {
    if (target == nullptr || empty()) return;
    if (target == head) { pop_front(); return; }
    if (target == tail) { pop_back(); return; }

    target->get_prev()->set_next(target->get_next());
    target->get_next()->set_prev(target->get_prev());
    delete target;
    size--;
}

// --- CÃ¡ÂºÂ­p nhÃ¡ÂºÂ­t con trÃ¡Â»Â head ---
template <typename t>
void linked_list<t>::set_head(node<t>* new_head) {
    this->head = new_head;
}

// --- CÃ¡ÂºÂ­p nhÃ¡ÂºÂ­t con trÃ¡Â»Â tail ---
template <typename t>
void linked_list<t>::set_tail(node<t>* new_tail) {
    this->tail = new_tail;
}

class linked_list {
private:
    node<t>* head;
    node<t>* tail;
    int size;

public:
    linked_list();
    linked_list(const linked_list<t>& other); //tÃ¡ÂºÂ¡o danh sÃƒÂ¡ch mÃ¡Â»â€ºi bÃ¡ÂºÂ±ng cÃƒÂ¡ch sao chÃƒÂ©p dÃ¡Â»Â¯ liÃ¡Â»â€¡u tÃ¡Â»Â« danh sÃƒÂ¡ch khÃƒÂ¡c.
    linked_list<t>& operator=(const linked_list<t>& other); //sao chÃƒÂ©p dÃ¡Â»Â¯ liÃ¡Â»â€¡u khi gÃƒÂ¡n mÃ¡Â»â„¢t danh sÃƒÂ¡ch cÃƒÂ³ sÃ¡ÂºÂµn cho danh sÃƒÂ¡ch khÃƒÂ¡c.
    ~linked_list();

    bool empty() const;

    void push_back(t data);

    void set_tail(node<t>* new_tail);

};

template <typename t>
node<t>::node() {
    this->data = t();
    this->next = nullptr;
    this->prev = nullptr;
}

node<t>::node(t data) {
    this->data = data;
    this->next = nullptr;
    this->prev = nullptr;
}

template <typename t>
t node<t>::get_data() const {
    return data;
}

template <typename t>
node<t>* node<t>::get_next() const {
    return next;
}

template <typename t>
node<t>* node<t>::get_prev() const {
    return prev;
}

    this->data = data;
}

    this->next = next;
}

template <typename t>
void node<t>::set_prev(node<t>* prev) {
    this->prev = prev;
}

t& node<t>::get_Data()
{
    return data;
}

=== FILE: .\Structure\queue.cpp ===

#include"queue.h"

template <typename t>
queue<t>::queue() {
    this->front_node = nullptr;
    this->rear_node = nullptr;
    this->size = 0;
}

template <typename t>
queue<t>::~queue() {
    clear();
}

template <typename t>
void queue<t>::push(t item) {
    node<t>* new_node = new node<t>(item);
    
    if (is_empty()) {
        // NÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u hÃƒÂ ng Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»Â£i rÃ¡Â»â€”ng, Ã„â€˜Ã¡ÂºÂ§u vÃƒÂ  cuÃ¡Â»â€˜i Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»Âu trÃ¡Â»Â vÃƒÂ o node mÃ¡Â»â€ºi
        front_node = rear_node = new_node;
    } else {
        // ThÃƒÂªm vÃƒÂ o cuÃ¡Â»â€˜i hÃƒÂ ng Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»Â£i
        rear_node->set_next(new_node); // LiÃƒÂªn kÃ¡ÂºÂ¿t phÃ¡ÂºÂ§n tÃ¡Â»Â­ cuÃ¡Â»â€˜i hiÃ¡Â»â€¡n tÃ¡ÂºÂ¡i vÃ¡Â»â€ºi node mÃ¡Â»â€ºi
        new_node->set_prev(rear_node); // TÃ¡ÂºÂ¡o liÃƒÂªn kÃ¡ÂºÂ¿t ngÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£c vÃ¡Â»Â phÃ¡ÂºÂ§n tÃ¡Â»Â­ cuÃ¡Â»â€˜i cÃ…Â© (nhÃ¡Â»Â node cÃƒÂ³ prev)
        rear_node = new_node;          // CÃ¡ÂºÂ­p nhÃ¡ÂºÂ­t lÃ¡ÂºÂ¡i con trÃ¡Â»Â Ã„â€˜uÃƒÂ´i
    }
    size++;
}

template <typename t>
void queue<t>::pop() {
    if (is_empty()) return;
    
    node<t>* temp = front_node;
    front_node = front_node->get_next(); // DÃ¡Â»â€¹ch con trÃ¡Â»Â Ã„â€˜Ã¡ÂºÂ§u sang phÃ¡ÂºÂ§n tÃ¡Â»Â­ kÃ¡ÂºÂ¿ tiÃ¡ÂºÂ¿p
    
    if (front_node == nullptr) {
        // NÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u lÃ¡ÂºÂ¥y phÃ¡ÂºÂ§n tÃ¡Â»Â­ cuÃ¡Â»â€˜i cÃƒÂ¹ng ra, hÃƒÂ ng Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»Â£i trÃ¡Â»Å¸ vÃ¡Â»Â rÃ¡Â»â€”ng
        rear_node = nullptr;
    } else {
        // NÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u vÃ¡ÂºÂ«n cÃƒÂ²n phÃ¡ÂºÂ§n tÃ¡Â»Â­, cÃ¡ÂºÂ¯t Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»Â©t liÃƒÂªn kÃ¡ÂºÂ¿t ngÃ†Â°Ã¡Â»Â£c cÃ¡Â»Â§a phÃ¡ÂºÂ§n tÃ¡Â»Â­ Ã„â€˜Ã¡ÂºÂ§u mÃ¡Â»â€ºi
        front_node->set_prev(nullptr);
    }
    
    delete temp; // GiÃ¡ÂºÂ£i phÃƒÂ³ng bÃ¡Â»â„¢ nhÃ¡Â»â€º cÃ¡Â»Â§a mÃ¡ÂºÂ¯t xÃƒÂ­ch vÃ¡Â»Â«a rÃƒÂºt
    size--;
}

template <typename t>
t queue<t>::front() const {
    if (is_empty()) return t(); // TrÃ¡ÂºÂ£ vÃ¡Â»Â giÃƒÂ¡ trÃ¡Â»â€¹ mÃ¡ÂºÂ·c Ã„â€˜Ã¡Â»â€¹nh cÃ¡Â»Â§a kiÃ¡Â»Æ’u T nÃ¡ÂºÂ¿u rÃ¡Â»â€”ng
    return front_node->get_data(); // SÃ¡Â»Â­ dÃ¡Â»Â¥ng hÃƒÂ m get_data() cÃ¡Â»Â§a lÃ¡Â»â€ºp node
}

template <typename t>
bool queue<t>::is_empty() const {
    return front_node == nullptr;
}

template <typename t>
int queue<t>::get_size() const {
    return size;
}

template <typename t>
void queue<t>::clear() {
    while (!is_empty()) {
        pop();
    }
}

=== FILE: .\Structure\stack.cpp ===

#include "stack.h"

template <typename t>
stack<t>::stack() {
    top = nullptr;
    size = 0;
}

template <typename t>
stack<t>::~stack() {
    clear();
}

template <typename t>
bool stack<t>::empty() const {
    return top == nullptr;
}

template <typename t>
int stack<t>::get_size() const {
    return size;
}

template <typename t>
void stack<t>::push(t data) {
    node<t>* new_node = new node<t>(data);

    new_node->set_next(top);
    top = new_node;

    size++;
}

template <typename t>
void stack<t>::pop() {
    if (empty())
        return;

    node<t>* temp = top;

    top = top->get_next();

    delete temp;
    size--;
}

template <typename t>
t stack<t>::peek() const {
    if (empty())
        return t();

    return top->get_data();
}

template <typename t>
void stack<t>::clear() {
    while (!empty())
        pop();
}




=== FILE: .\Structure\stack.h ===

#pragma once

#include "node.h"

template <typename t>
class stack {
private:
    node<t>* top;
    int size;

public:
    stack();
    ~stack();

    stack(const stack<t>&) = delete;
    stack<t>& operator=(const stack<t>&) = delete;

    bool empty() const;
    int get_size() const;

    void push(t data);
    void pop();

    t peek() const;

    void clear();
};
    
#include "stack.cpp"




=== FILE: .\gui_main.cpp ===
#include "raylib.h"
#include "GUI/login.h"
#include "GUI/library_gui.h"
#include "GUI/ui_font.h"
#include <vector>

Font app_font;

int main() {
    SetConfigFlags(FLAG_WINDOW_RESIZABLE);
    InitWindow(1220, 750, "PBL2 - Quáº£n lÃ½ thÆ° viá»‡n");
    SetWindowMinSize(900, 600);
    SetTargetFPS(60);

    // Nap day du ky tu tieng Viet. Neu truyen nullptr, raylib chi nap ASCII
    // nen cac chu co dau se bi hien thanh dau '?'.
    std::vector<int> codepoints;
    for (int c = 32; c <= 126; ++c) codepoints.push_back(c);          // ASCII
    for (int c = 0xA0; c <= 0x17F; ++c) codepoints.push_back(c);      // Latin-1, Latin mo rong A (A, D, I, U...)
    codepoints.push_back(0x1A0); codepoints.push_back(0x1A1);         // O, o co rau
    codepoints.push_back(0x1AF); codepoints.push_back(0x1B0);         // U, u co rau
    for (int c = 0x1EA0; c <= 0x1EF9; ++c) codepoints.push_back(c);   // Latin mo rong bo sung (dau thanh)
    codepoints.push_back(0x2013); codepoints.push_back(0x2014);       // gach ngang
    codepoints.push_back(0x2026);                                     // dau ba cham

    app_font = LoadFontEx("C:/Windows/Fonts/segoeui.ttf", 64,
                          codepoints.data(), (int)codepoints.size());
    bool custom_font = app_font.texture.id != 0;
    if (!custom_font) app_font = GetFontDefault();   // du phong neu khong tim thay font
    SetTextureFilter(app_font.texture, TEXTURE_FILTER_BILINEAR);

    library_controller controller;
    login_screen login(controller);
    library_gui app(controller);

    while (!WindowShouldClose()) {
        if (!login.is_logged_in()) {
            app.reset();
            login.draw();
            continue;
        }

        app.draw(login.get_role());

        if (app.wants_logout()) {
            login.reset();
            app.reset();
        }
    }

    if (custom_font) UnloadFont(app_font);
    CloseWindow();
    return 0;
}

#include <iostream>

// ================= CÃC HÃ€M NHáº¬P LIá»†U CHá»NG TREO & RÃ€NG BUá»˜C Táº I CHá»– =================
int input_int(string prompt, int min_val = 0, int max_val = 2147483647) {
    int value;
    while (true) {
        cout << prompt;
        if (cin >> value && value >= min_val && value <= max_val) {
            cin.ignore(numeric_limits<streamsize>::max(), '\n');
            return value;
        }
        cout << "[Loi] Nhap sai! Phai la so tu " << min_val << " den " << max_val << ". Vui long nhap lai.\n";
        cin.clear();
        cin.ignore(numeric_limits<streamsize>::max(), '\n');
    }
}

string input_string(string prompt) {
    string str;
    while (true) {
        cout << prompt;
        getline(cin >> ws, str);
        if (!str.empty()) return str;
        cout << "[Loi] Khong duoc de trong! Vui long nhap lai.\n";
    }
}

string input_session(string prompt) {
    string session;
    while (true) {
        cout << prompt;
        cin >> session;
        if (session == "sang" || session == "chieu") {
            cin.ignore(numeric_limits<streamsize>::max(), '\n');
            return session;
        }
        cout << "[Loi] Ca truc chi duoc la 'sang' hoac 'chieu'! Vui long nhap lai.\n";
        cin.clear();
        cin.ignore(numeric_limits<streamsize>::max(), '\n');
    }
}

date input_valid_date(string prompt) {
    while (true) {
        cout << prompt;
        int d, m, y;
        if (cin >> d >> m >> y) {
            date dt(d, m, y);
            if (dt.is_valid()) {
                cin.ignore(numeric_limits<streamsize>::max(), '\n');
                return dt;
            }
        }
        cout << "[Loi] Ngay khong hop le hoac khong ton tai! Vui long nhap lai.\n";
        cin.clear();
        cin.ignore(numeric_limits<streamsize>::max(), '\n');
    }
}

    cout << "\n--- CHON THOI HAN MUON ---\n";
    cout << "1. 1 tuan (7 ngay)\n";
    cout << "2. 2 tuan (14 ngay)\n";
    cout << "3. 1 thang (30 ngay)\n";

    int choice = input_int("Lua chon: ", 1, 3);

    if (choice == 1) return 7;
    if (choice == 2) return 14;
    return 30;
}

// ================= CHÆ¯Æ NG TRÃŒNH CHÃNH =================
int main() {
    library_controller controller;

    cout << "=====================================================\n";
    cout << "       HE THONG QUAN LY THU VIEN PHAN QUYEN         \n";
    cout << "=====================================================\n";

    while (true) {
        cout << "\n-------------------- DANG NHAP --------------------\n";
        string id = input_string("Nhap Ma tai khoan (ID) [Go 'thoat' de tat chuong trinh]: ");
        if (id == "thoat" || id == "exit") {
            if (confirm_action("Ban co chac chan muon dong ung dung va thoat he thong?")) {
                cout << "Dang dong he thong va luu toan bo du lieu. Tam biet!\n";
                break;
            } else {
                cout << "[Thong bao] Da huy thoat chuong trinh.\n";
                continue;
            }
        }

        string password = input_string("Nhap Mat khau: ");

        if (controller.login(id, password)) {
            user* u = controller.get_current_user();
            string role = controller.get_current_role();

                 << " | Xin chao: " << u->get_name() << "!\n";

            if (role == "manager") {
                while (is_logged_in) {

                    if (choice == 1) {
                        string keyword = input_string("Nhap tu khoa tim kiem (Ten/Tac gia/The loai): ");
                        linked_list<book> results = controller.search_books(keyword);
                        cout << "\n--- KET QUA TIM KIEM ---\n";
                        node<book>* curr = results.get_head();
                        string found_cat = "";
                        string found_id = "";
                        if (curr == nullptr) {
                            cout << "Khong tim thay sach phu hop!\n";
                        }
                        while (curr != nullptr) {
                            book b = curr->get_data();
                            if (found_cat.empty()) {
                                found_cat = b.get_category();
                                found_id = b.get_id();
                            }
                            cout << "- [" << b.get_id() << "] " << b.get_title() 
                                    << " | The loai: " << b.get_category() 
                                    << " | Tac gia: " << b.get_author() 
                                    << " | Ton kho: " << b.get_quantity() 
                                    << " | Vi tri: Ke " << controller.get_book_shelf(b.get_id()) << "\n";
                            curr = curr->get_next();
                        }
                        // Gá»£i Ã½ sÃ¡ch liÃªn quan
                        if (!found_cat.empty()) {
                            linked_list<book> related = controller.get_related_books(found_cat, found_id);
                            if (!related.empty()) {
                                cout << "\n--- SACH GOI Y LIEN QUAN (Cung the loai: " << found_cat << ") ---\n";
                                node<book>* rel_curr = related.get_head();
                                while (rel_curr != nullptr) {
                                    book rb = rel_curr->get_data();
                                    cout << " * [" << rb.get_id() << "] " << rb.get_title() 
                                         << " | Tac gia: " << rb.get_author() 
                                         << " | Ton kho: " << rb.get_quantity() << "\n";
                                    rel_curr = rel_curr->get_next();
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        string b_id = input_string("Nhap Ma sach (ID): ");
                        string title = input_string("Nhap ten sach: ");

                        string cat = input_string("Nhap the loai: ");
                        int qty = input_int("So luong ton kho: ", 0, 10000);
                        int year = input_int("Nam xuat ban: ", 1000, 2026);

                        else
                            cout << "[Loi] Trung ma sach hoac khong the them!\n";
